FILE: CLOUD_SYNC_SETUP.txt
================================================================================
V2A CLOUD SYNC SETUP (SUPABASE)

The app has been prepared to sync a signed-in user's app state through Supabase. Cloud sync stays off until you create a Supabase project, run the SQL below, and add the project URL/key. Without those settings, the app continues to run in local demo mode.

IMPORTANT LIMITS
- This starter stores one JSON state document per signed-in account. It is intended for this prototype, not a production system for sensitive financial, identity, or farm records.
- Each account's data is protected by Supabase Row Level Security (RLS) policies in supabase/schema.sql.
- Existing anonymous browser data is NOT automatically copied to a new cloud account. The old data remains in that browser's local storage. A new account starts with its own empty farm, parcel, task, and transaction records plus a clearly labeled planning template and local sample catalogs; it does not receive the separate VIJAY demo farmer or transaction data. Sample catalog entries may not reflect actual availability or prices.
- Do not use a Supabase service-role/secret key in this browser app. Use only the project URL and the publishable/anon key, with the SQL RLS policies enabled.

1. CREATE A SUPABASE PROJECT
- Sign in or create an account at https://supabase.com/.
- Create a new project and save its database password somewhere secure.
- Wait until the project is ready.

2. CREATE THE PRIVATE USER-STATE TABLE
- Open the Supabase project and choose SQL Editor > New query.
- Open this project's supabase/schema.sql file, copy all its SQL into the query, and click Run.
- The table stores one app-state record per Supabase user. RLS ensures an authenticated user can read/write only their own row.

3. ENABLE EMAIL SIGN-IN AND SET REDIRECT URLS
- In Supabase, open Authentication > Providers and ensure Email is enabled.
- In Authentication > URL Configuration, set the Site URL to:
  https://vithai-to-aruvadai.vercel.app
- Add these Redirect URLs:
  https://vithai-to-aruvadai.vercel.app/**
  http://localhost:5173/**
- Keep email confirmation enabled for real accounts. Supabase may send a confirmation email after sign-up.
- For password reset, the email link returns to the hash-routed reset form. Keep the production origin and local development URL on the allowed Redirect URLs list.

4. SET UP LOCAL DEVELOPMENT
- In the project folder, copy .env.example to .env.local.
- Open .env.local and replace the placeholders with the Supabase Project URL and the publishable/anon key from Project Settings > API.
- Do not share .env.local or commit it to GitHub; it is ignored by .gitignore.
- Install packages if needed with: npm.cmd install
- Run the app with: npm.cmd run dev
- Open the local URL printed in the terminal. To use personal email/password accounts, first replace both Supabase placeholders in .env.local with real project values, then restart the dev server. The app will show the cloud sign-in screen; choose "Create an account" to register or open `http://localhost:5173/#/signup`.
- If Supabase is not configured, the app runs in the local-only VIJAY demo mode and does not offer personal account registration. Personal email/password authentication requires valid Supabase settings.
- The separate "Continue as demo user (VIJAY)" option never signs into Supabase and uses only this browser's demo data.

5. CONNECT THE DEPLOYED VERCEL APP
- Open the V2A project in Vercel > Settings > Environment Variables.
- Add VITE_SUPABASE_URL with the Supabase Project URL.
- Add VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY with the publishable/anon key.
- Apply them to Production (and Preview if desired), save, then redeploy the latest deployment.
- Open the live app, create an account/sign in, and look for “Cloud Synced” only after the initial account data and a change have been saved successfully. If the app reports an offline or sync error, reconnect and use Retry in the top banner.

6. TEST SYNC BETWEEN DEVICES
- Sign in to the same account on two browsers/devices.
- Make a small test change on one, wait for “Cloud synced,” then refresh the other.
- Avoid editing the same records on both devices at the same time: this prototype uses last-write-wins for the whole state document rather than merging simultaneous changes.

If cloud sync fails, check that the SQL ran successfully, both Vercel environment variables are exact, the current deployment was rebuilt after setting them, and the user is signed in. The app's old local-only data is not migrated automatically; make a separate backup before attempting any manual migration.

FILE: EDIT_APP_FROM_ANOTHER_LAPTOP.txt
================================================================================
HOW TO MODIFY THE VITHAI TO ARUVADAI APP FROM ANOTHER LAPTOP

Live app:
https://v2-a-eight.vercel.app/

GitHub source code:
https://github.com/Niranjan150208/V2A

WHAT YOU NEED ON THE OTHER LAPTOP
1. Internet access.
2. Access to the GitHub account/repository above.
3. Git for Windows: https://git-scm.com/downloads
4. Node.js LTS (npm is included): https://nodejs.org/
5. Visual Studio Code (recommended): https://code.visualstudio.com/

STEP 1 — DOWNLOAD THE PROJECT
Open PowerShell or the VS Code terminal and run:

  git clone https://github.com/Niranjan150208/V2A.git
  cd V2A

If GitHub asks you to sign in, complete the sign-in in your browser. Do not share your password or verification codes.

STEP 2 — INSTALL THE PROJECT PACKAGES
Run this once after cloning:

  npm.cmd install

STEP 3 — OPEN AND RUN THE APP ON YOUR LAPTOP
Open the V2A folder in VS Code. In the terminal, run:

  npm.cmd run dev

Open the local address printed in the terminal (usually http://localhost:5173) in your browser. The local version is for testing; visitors will continue to see the deployed version until your changes are pushed and deployed.

STEP 4 — MAKE CHANGES
Edit the files in the src folder using VS Code. Save your changes, then check the local app in your browser. Before publishing, you can check that it builds by running:

  npm.cmd run build

STEP 5 — SEND YOUR CHANGES TO GITHUB
In the VS Code terminal, run:

  git add .
  git commit -m "Describe your changes"
  git push

On a new laptop, Git may ask for your commit name and email. Set them once with your own details:

  git config --global user.name "Your Name"
  git config --global user.email "you@example.com"

Replace the example name and email with your preferred commit identity. These identify your commits; they are separate from GitHub sign-in.

STEP 6 — PUBLISH THE UPDATED APP
If the Vercel project is connected to this GitHub repository, pushing changes to the main branch should start a new Vercel deployment automatically. Check the deployment in your Vercel dashboard and open the live URL after its status says Ready.

If no deployment starts, sign in at https://vercel.com, open the project, and check Settings > Git to confirm it is connected to Niranjan150208/V2A and deploys from the main branch.

IMPORTANT
- Do not commit passwords, access tokens, or other private credentials to GitHub.
- Keep the GitHub repository as the main copy of your source code. Downloading the live website alone is not the same as getting the editable source project.
- If you edit on more than one laptop, run "git pull" inside the V2A folder before starting new work so you get the latest changes.

FILE: HACKATHON_PITCH.md
================================================================================
# VITHAI TO ARUVADAI — Judge Presentation Guide

**Live demo:** https://vithai-to-aruvadai.vercel.app/  
**One-line pitch:** A Tamil-first crop-season workspace that lets a farmer keep field records together and follow a harvest lot through buyer offers, a sale record, and a clearly separated payment ledger.

## Problem and approach

Small farmers often manage crop plans, input costs, harvest details, buyer conversations, and payment follow-up across disconnected channels. V2A demonstrates how those records could be brought into one crop-season journey, with a focus on making the economics of a sale easier to inspect.

The prototype's strongest working slice is intentionally narrow and demonstrable: reset a prepared harvest, publish a local demo listing, compare generated buyer offers, accept one, create a sale record, and track the receivable separately from a locally recorded receipt.

## 60-second pitch

> Farmers make many important decisions across a season, but their plans, costs, harvest details, buyer offers, and payment follow-up are often scattered. VITHAI TO ARUVADAI is a Tamil-first crop-season workspace designed to connect those steps in one place. Our demo focuses on the point where value is most visible: a farmer broadcasts a harvested lot, compares buyer terms, accepts an offer, sees the sale calculation, and tracks pending dues separately from money marked received. The journey is replayable, so judges can inspect each state change. Today this is a front-end prototype using local demo data—not a live marketplace, AI diagnosis service, or bank integration. Our next step is to validate this workflow with farmers and buyers, then add secure shared storage and verified integrations based on that feedback.

## Three-minute live demo

1. Open the live demo URL. The app opens to the dashboard.
2. Select **Reset demo journey** and confirm. This resets demo data in this browser only and prepares a harvested, unsold sample crop.
3. Open **Harvest** and select **Broadcast Lot to Buyers**.
4. Compare the generated sample offers. Explain that these are demo offers, not bids from connected buyers.
5. Accept one offer. Show the generated sale record and its itemized calculation.
6. Open **Payments**. Point out the pending receivable, then select **Simulate Bank Credit**.
7. Explain that this marks a local ledger record as received; it does not initiate or verify a bank transfer.

**If time is short:** skip the optional seed and crop-advice screens; the harvest-to-payment path is the clearest end-to-end demonstration.

## How the implementation supports the judging criteria

| Criterion                          | What to show                                                                        | Evidence and honest boundary                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Innovation & Creativity (25%)      | The connected crop-season journey and farmer-facing financial view                  | The product combines records and decision steps into one workflow. The differentiator is workflow integration, not a claim of novel AI or blockchain.                                                                       |
| Technical Implementation (25%)     | Broadcast → offers → accept → sale → payment status                                 | React + TypeScript + Vite app; season records persist in browser `localStorage`; store logic generates listings, offers, sale calculations, and payment milestones. State is local to one browser, not shared across users. |
| Problem Solving (20%)              | Compare terms and track outstanding dues separately                                 | The prototype makes trade terms and gross/net calculations inspectable. Buyer offers and settlement are simulated; the app does not connect farmers to actual buyers or banks.                                              |
| UX & Design (20%)                  | Switch between English/Tamil and follow the dashboard workflow on desktop or mobile | Responsive layouts and language controls support the intended audience. Some translations and module content are not yet complete.                                                                                          |
| Presentation & Documentation (10%) | Use the replay steps and explain what is real today                                 | This guide and the README document setup, demo flow, and integration boundaries. Do not present mock records as verified services.                                                                                          |

## Technical snapshot

- **Frontend:** React, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, lucide-react.
- **Navigation:** client-side route/hash state.
- **Persistence:** browser `localStorage` through the app store.
- **Production hosting:** Vercel static deployment.
- **External services:** none currently connected for authentication, weather, market prices, crop diagnosis, buyer matching, government verification, or payments.

## Practical next steps

1. Interview farmers and buyers in one pilot region; validate terminology, trust needs, and the order of the trade workflow.
2. Add a hosted database and authentication with farmer/buyer roles; protect personal and land data with explicit consent and access controls.
3. Replace sample weather/market values with sourced feeds, showing source and timestamp.
4. Connect diagnosis to a validated advisory workflow with expert review; label uncertainty and avoid unsafe treatment instructions.
5. Integrate payments only through an authorized provider, with user confirmation and auditable receipts. Never treat a local demo toggle as proof of bank settlement.
6. Add automated tests for season isolation, duplicate listing prevention, offer acceptance, calculation reconciliation, and payment transitions.

## Judge Q&A

**Is this a live marketplace?**  
No. Buyer profiles and offers are generated demo data; there is no multi-user backend or live buyer connection yet.

**Does the payment button transfer money?**  
No. It updates a local demo ledger only. A real transfer requires a regulated payment partner, consent, and verification.

**What is the technical innovation?**  
The prototype demonstrates one consistent crop-season workflow and separates sale value, pending receivables, and amounts marked received, rather than treating them as one number.

**What would you build next?**  
Validate the offer and settlement workflow with a small regional pilot, then build secure shared data and integrate only verified services that farmers actually need.

## Safety and privacy note

Use sample information only. Do not enter real Aadhaar, patta, bank, phone, or health data. Demo state is stored in the current browser and can be cleared with **Reset demo journey**.

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# VITHAI TO ARUVADAI (V2A)

A Tamil Nadu farmer workflow prototype that connects crop planning, field records, harvest quality, buyer offers, sale invoices, and payment tracking.

For the evaluation rubric, short pitch, judge walkthrough, and practical roadmap, see [HACKATHON_PITCH.md](HACKATHON_PITCH.md).

## Run locally

Requirements: Node.js 20.19+ or 22.12+.

1. Install dependencies: `npm install`
2. Start the development server: `npm run dev`
3. Open the local URL printed by Vite.
4. Create a production build: `npm run build`

For camera and microphone access, open the deployed HTTPS URL or `http://localhost`; mobile LAN URLs served over plain HTTP may not be considered secure by the browser. Approve camera/microphone access when prompted. QR scanning uses the camera and a local QR decoder; browser speech recognition requires browser support and may require internet access.

## Demo access and media

- Demo farmer: **VIJAY**
- Demo phone: `9000000421`
- Demo OTP: `4210` (local UI demo only; no SMS or real authentication)
- Personal accounts: configure Supabase using [CLOUD_SYNC_SETUP.txt](CLOUD_SYNC_SETUP.txt), then use the email/password sign-up and sign-in options. Demo access remains a separate local-only session.
- A new personal account starts with blank farm and transaction records plus a clearly labeled starter planning template; it does not inherit VIJAY's demo records.
- Crop and soil images are compressed and stored with the local browser demo record. They are not uploaded to a server or sent to an AI model.
- Soil pH/N/P/K values must be entered from a test kit or laboratory result; a photo cannot measure soil chemistry.

## Hackathon demo walkthrough

The app stores its demo data in this browser's `localStorage`. To replay the trade journey:

1. On the dashboard, select **Reset demo journey** and confirm. This resets this browser's demo edits and prepares a harvested but unsold lot.
2. Open **Harvest** and choose **Broadcast Lot to Buyers**.
3. Review generated demo buyer offers and accept one.
4. Review the created sale invoice and open **Payments**.
5. Choose **Simulate Bank Credit** to record a demo settlement and see the receivable update.

The trade walkthrough is replayable, persists across reloads in the same browser, prevents duplicate active listings and duplicate sales, and calculates sale proceeds and pending receivables from the selected season's records.

## Current integration boundary

This is a front-end hackathon prototype. Farmer, buyer, weather, crop-diagnosis, offer, and payment data are demo/simulated data; shared sample catalogs may not reflect actual availability or prices. Without Supabase configuration, records are stored per browser; they are not shared between users or devices. With Supabase configured, email/password accounts use Supabase Authentication and each account's app state is stored separately with row-level security. Demo access is always separate and local-only. The payment action only updates the app ledger and does not initiate a bank transfer. No government, bank, weather, or AI service is connected.

For a multi-user public product, connect a hosted backend (for example Supabase or Firebase), configure real authentication and role-based access, and integrate verified external data/services. Do not enter real identity, land, bank, or health information into this demo.

## Technology

React, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, and lucide-react.

import React, { useEffect } from "react";
import { NavigationProvider, useNavigation } from "./context/NavigationContext";
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "./context/AuthContext";
import { AppLayout } from "./components/layout/AppLayout";
import { VoiceAssistantModal } from "./components/modals/VoiceAssistantModal";
import { LoginPage } from "./pages/LoginPage";
import { LoaderCircle } from "lucide-react";

// All 36 Route Pages
import { HomePage } from "./pages/HomePage";
import { OnboardingPage } from "./pages/OnboardingPage";
import { DashboardPage } from "./pages/DashboardPage";
import { MyFarmPage } from "./pages/MyFarmPage";
import { CropPlannerPage } from "./pages/CropPlannerPage";
import { SoilHealthPage } from "./pages/SoilHealthPage";
import { WeatherPage } from "./pages/WeatherPage";
import { BudgetPlannerPage } from "./pages/BudgetPlannerPage";
import { SeedPassportPage } from "./pages/SeedPassportPage";
import { InputMarketplacePage } from "./pages/InputMarketplacePage";
import { InputLockerPage } from "./pages/InputLockerPage";
import { MachineryPage } from "./pages/MachineryPage";
import { LabourPage } from "./pages/LabourPage";
import { FarmCalendarPage } from "./pages/FarmCalendarPage";
import { IrrigationPage } from "./pages/IrrigationPage";
import { CropDoctorPage } from "./pages/CropDoctorPage";
import { ExpertAdvicePage } from "./pages/ExpertAdvicePage";
import { CropHealthPage } from "./pages/CropHealthPage";
import { InsurancePage } from "./pages/InsurancePage";
import { HarvestPage } from "./pages/HarvestPage";
import { BuyerDiscoveryPage } from "./pages/BuyerDiscoveryPage";
import { StoragePage } from "./pages/StoragePage";
import { LogisticsPage } from "./pages/LogisticsPage";
import { MarketPricesPage } from "./pages/MarketPricesPage";
import { BuyersPage } from "./pages/BuyersPage";
import { OffersPage } from "./pages/OffersPage";
import { NetProceedsPage } from "./pages/NetProceedsPage";
import { SalesPage } from "./pages/SalesPage";
import { PaymentsPage } from "./pages/PaymentsPage";
import { FinancePage } from "./pages/FinancePage";
import { SeasonComparisonPage } from "./pages/SeasonComparisonPage";
import { NextSeasonPage } from "./pages/NextSeasonPage";
import { SchemesPage } from "./pages/SchemesPage";
import { CropJourneyPage } from "./pages/CropJourneyPage";
import { ProfilePage } from "./pages/ProfilePage";
import { AppStore } from "./services/store";

const AppContent: React.FC = () => {
  const { currentRoute, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const {
    session,
    demoMode,
    passwordRecovery,
    authInitialized,
    ready,
    setupError,
  } = useAuth();
  const accountState = AppStore.getState();
  const needsPersonalSetup =
    Boolean(session) &&
    accountState.currentUser.id === session?.user.id &&
    (accountState.farm.parcels.length === 0 ||
      accountState.farmer.totalLandAcres <= 0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authInitialized || !ready) return;
    const authRoutes = ["/login", "/signup", "/forgot-password", "/reset-password"];

    if (passwordRecovery) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[ROUTE] Opening password recovery.");
      if (currentRoute !== "/reset-password") navigate("/reset-password");
      return;
    }

    if (needsPersonalSetup) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[ROUTE] Authenticated account needs farm setup.");
      if (currentRoute !== "/onboarding") navigate("/onboarding");
      return;
    }

    if (session || demoMode) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[ROUTE] Authenticated/demo access accepted.");
      if (authRoutes.includes(currentRoute)) {
        if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[ROUTE] Navigating to /dashboard.");
        navigate("/dashboard");
      }
    } else {
      if (!authRoutes.includes(currentRoute)) {
        if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[ROUTE] Unauthenticated; redirecting to /login.");
        navigate("/login");
      }
    }
  }, [
    currentRoute,
    authInitialized,
    demoMode,
    navigate,
    needsPersonalSetup,
    passwordRecovery,
    ready,
    session,
  ]);

  if (!authInitialized || !ready) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-cream-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-forest-900">
        <LoaderCircle className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-paddy-600" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide">
          Connecting securely to V2A…
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (setupError && !demoMode) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-cream-50 flex items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-terracotta-300 bg-white p-6 shadow-agro">
          <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-forest-900">
            {session ? "Account data is unavailable" : "Cloud setup needs attention"}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-700">{setupError}</p>
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-ink-600">
            Check the Supabase environment variables and run the SQL setup in
            the cloud sync guide, then reload this page.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-forest-900"
            >
              Reload app
            </button>
            {!session && (
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/login")}
                className="rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-forest-900 hover:bg-cream-100"
              >
                Continue to Login
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // When not signed in and not in demo mode, display the public login screen without farmer sidebar
  if (!session && !demoMode) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-organic-waves py-8 px-4 flex flex-col justify-center">
        <LoginPage />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const renderRoute = () => {
    switch (currentRoute) {
      case "/":
        return <HomePage />;
      case "/login":
      case "/signup":
      case "/forgot-password":
      case "/reset-password":
        return <LoginPage />;
      case "/onboarding":
        return <OnboardingPage />;
      case "/dashboard":
        return <DashboardPage />;
      case "/my-farm":
        return <MyFarmPage />;
      case "/crop-planner":
        return <CropPlannerPage />;
      case "/soil-health":
        return <SoilHealthPage />;
      case "/weather":
        return <WeatherPage />;
      case "/budget-planner":
        return <BudgetPlannerPage />;
      case "/seed-passport":
        return <SeedPassportPage />;
      case "/input-marketplace":
        return <InputMarketplacePage />;
      case "/input-locker":
        return <InputLockerPage />;
      case "/machinery":
        return <MachineryPage />;
      case "/labour":
        return <LabourPage />;
      case "/farm-calendar":
        return <FarmCalendarPage />;
      case "/irrigation":
        return <IrrigationPage />;
      case "/crop-doctor":
        return <CropDoctorPage />;
      case "/expert-advice":
        return <ExpertAdvicePage />;
      case "/crop-health":
        return <CropHealthPage />;
      case "/insurance":
        return <InsurancePage />;
      case "/harvest":
        return <HarvestPage />;
      case "/buyer-discovery":
        return <BuyerDiscoveryPage />;
      case "/storage":
        return <StoragePage />;
      case "/logistics":
        return <LogisticsPage />;
      case "/market-prices":
        return <MarketPricesPage />;
      case "/buyers":
        return <BuyersPage />;
      case "/offers":
        return <OffersPage />;
      case "/net-proceeds":
        return <NetProceedsPage />;
      case "/sales":
        return <SalesPage />;
      case "/payments":
        return <PaymentsPage />;
      case "/finance":
        return <FinancePage />;
      case "/season-comparison":
        return <SeasonComparisonPage />;
      case "/next-season":
        return <NextSeasonPage />;
      case "/schemes":
        return <SchemesPage />;
      case "/crop-journey":
        return <CropJourneyPage />;
      case "/profile":
        return <ProfilePage />;
      default:
        return <DashboardPage />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <AppLayout>
      {renderRoute()}
      <VoiceAssistantModal />
    </AppLayout>
  );
};

export function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <NavigationProvider>
        <AppContent />
      </NavigationProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

import React from 'react';

interface LogoProps {
  variant?: 'full' | 'compact' | 'icon' | 'badge';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';
  showTamil?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  theme?: 'dark' | 'light';
}

export const Logo: React.FC<LogoProps> = ({
  variant = 'full',
  size = 'md',
  showTamil = true,
  className = '',
  theme = 'light',
}) => {
  const sizeMap = {
    sm: { icon: 32, text: 'text-base', sub: 'text-[10px]' },
    md: { icon: 44, text: 'text-xl', sub: 'text-xs' },
    lg: { icon: 58, text: 'text-2xl', sub: 'text-sm' },
    xl: { icon: 76, text: 'text-3xl', sub: 'text-base' },
  };

  const currentSize = sizeMap[size];

  // SVG Seed + Sprout + Paddy Grain + Farming Cycle
  const LogoIcon = (
    <div className="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
      <svg
        width={currentSize.icon}
        height={currentSize.icon}
        viewBox="0 0 64 64"
        fill="none"
        xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
        className="transition-transform duration-300 hover:rotate-6"
      >
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="v2aGoldGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#F6E7BF" />
            <stop offset="50%" stop-color="#E4B94F" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#C99E35" />
          </linearGradient>
          <linearGradient id="v2aGreenGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#477A42" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#173C2C" />
          </linearGradient>
          <filter id="shadowFilter" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="120%">
            <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="2" stdDeviation="2" floodOpacity="0.15" />
          </filter>
        </defs>

        {/* Circular Farming Cycle Symbol */}
        <circle
          cx="32"
          cy="32"
          r="30"
          fill={theme === 'dark' ? '#173C2C' : '#F8F5E9'}
          stroke="#477A42"
          strokeWidth="2.5"
          strokeDasharray="7 3.5"
        />

        {/* Inner glow ring */}
        <circle cx="32" cy="32" r="26" fill="none" stroke="#DCE8D3" strokeWidth="1" />

        {/* V2A dynamic letterform stylized into furrow lines */}
        <path
          d="M 18 42 L 24 50 L 32 46"
          stroke="#173C2C"
          strokeWidth="2"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          opacity="0.25"
        />

        {/* Terracotta Seed in Soil */}
        <path
          d="M 28 48 C 23 45 22 39 25 35 C 27 31 33 32 35 36 C 37 41 34 47 28 48 Z"
          fill="#C76E48"
          filter="url(#shadowFilter)"
        />

        {/* Sprouting Stem with Paddy Green */}
        <path
          d="M 30 36 Q 30 20 42 14"
          stroke="#477A42"
          strokeWidth="3.2"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          fill="none"
        />

        {/* First Sprout Leaf */}
        <path
          d="M 30 28 C 22 26 19 21 18 16 C 23 15 29 20 30 28 Z"
          fill="#477A42"
        />

        {/* Second Leaf emerging into Harvest Gold */}
        <path
          d="M 33 22 C 38 19 44 19 47 15 C 47 19 41 23 33 22 Z"
          fill="url(#v2aGoldGrad)"
        />

        {/* Clustered Golden Paddy Grains (Harvest) */}
        <g filter="url(#shadowFilter)">
          <ellipse
            cx="44"
            cy="19"
            rx="3.8"
            ry="2.2"
            transform="rotate(-30 44 19)"
            fill="url(#v2aGoldGrad)"
            stroke="#7F5E16"
            strokeWidth="0.8"
          />
          <ellipse
            cx="47"
            cy="25"
            rx="3.8"
            ry="2.2"
            transform="rotate(-15 47 25)"
            fill="url(#v2aGoldGrad)"
            stroke="#7F5E16"
            strokeWidth="0.8"
          />
          <ellipse
            cx="49"
            cy="32"
            rx="3.8"
            ry="2.2"
            transform="rotate(10 49 32)"
            fill="url(#v2aGoldGrad)"
            stroke="#7F5E16"
            strokeWidth="0.8"
          />
          <ellipse
            cx="47"
            cy="39"
            rx="3.5"
            ry="2"
            transform="rotate(35 47 39)"
            fill="url(#v2aGoldGrad)"
            stroke="#7F5E16"
            strokeWidth="0.8"
          />
        </g>

        {/* Center Seedling Heart */}
        <circle cx="30" cy="36" r="2.2" fill="#E4B94F" />
      </svg>

      {/* Micro V2A Monogram Tag */}
      {variant === 'badge' && (
        <span className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-400 font-bold text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-harvest-500 shadow-sm">
          V2A
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  if (variant === 'icon') {
    return <div className={`inline-flex items-center ${className}`}>{LogoIcon}</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-3 ${className}`}>
      {LogoIcon}
      <div className="flex flex-col text-left">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
            className={`font-display font-extrabold tracking-tight ${
              theme === 'dark' ? 'text-cream-100' : 'text-forest-900'
            } ${currentSize.text}`}
          >
            VITHAI TO ARUVADAI
          </span>
          <span className="bg-forest-800 text-harvest-400 font-bold text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-forest-600 shadow-sm tracking-wider">
            V2A
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
            className={`font-medium ${
              theme === 'dark' ? 'text-harvest-400' : 'text-forest-700'
            } ${currentSize.sub}`}
          >
            From Seed to Harvest. Every Step Connected.
          </span>
          {showTamil && (
            <>
              <span className="text-terracotta-400 font-bold">•</span>
              <span
                className={`font-tamil font-semibold ${
                  theme === 'dark' ? 'text-sage-200' : 'text-paddy-700'
                } ${currentSize.sub}`}
              >
                விதை முதல் அறுவடை வரை
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";

} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation, AppRoute } from "../../context/NavigationContext";
import { Logo } from "../brand/Logo";
import { translations } from "../../translations";
import { AppStore } from "../../services/store";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

interface NavItem {
  id: string;
  route: AppRoute;
  labelKey: keyof typeof translations.en.nav;
  icon: React.ElementType;
  badge?: string;
  highlight?: boolean;
}

interface NavGroup {
  groupKey: keyof typeof translations.en.sections;
  items: NavItem[];
}

export const AppLayout: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({
  children,
}) => {
  const {
    currentRoute,
    navigate,
    language,
    setLanguage,
    activeSeasonId,
    setActiveSeasonId,
    setIsVoiceModalOpen,
  } = useNavigation();

  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [collapsedGroups, setCollapsedGroups] = useState<
    Record<string, boolean>
  >({});
  const { session, demoMode, signOut } = useAuth();
  const [syncStatus, setSyncStatus] = useState(AppStore.getSyncStatus());
  const [signOutError, setSignOutError] = useState("");
  const [syncRetrying, setSyncRetrying] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => AppStore.subscribeToSyncStatus(setSyncStatus), []);

  const handleSignOut = async () => {
    setSignOutError("");
    try {
      const warning = await signOut();
      navigate("/login");
      if (warning) setSignOutError(warning);
    } catch (error) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("Sign out did not complete", error);
      setSignOutError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "We couldn't complete sign-out. Please try again.",
      );
    }
  };

  const retryCloudSync = async () => {
    setSyncRetrying(true);
    setSignOutError("");
    try {
      await AppStore.flushCloudSync();
    } catch (error) {
      setSignOutError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Cloud sync failed. Please try again.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSyncRetrying(false);
    }
  };

  const t = translations[language];
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const activeSeason =
    state.seasons.find((s) => s.id === activeSeasonId) ||
    state.seasons[0] || {
      id: "CR-DEFAULT",
      seasonCode: "CR-DEFAULT",
      cropName: "Paddy (Samba)",
      variety: "CR 1009 Sub 1",
      tamilCropName: "நெல்",
    };

  const navGroups: NavGroup[] = [
    {
      groupKey: "planning",
      items: [
        {
          id: "dash",
          route: "/dashboard",
          labelKey: "dashboard",
          icon: LayoutDashboard,
        },
        { id: "farm", route: "/my-farm", labelKey: "myFarm", icon: Layers },
        {
          id: "planner",
          route: "/crop-planner",
          labelKey: "cropPlanner",

        },

        {

          icon: CloudSun,
        },
        {

          icon: FileSpreadsheet,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      groupKey: "inputs",
      items: [
        {
          id: "seed",
          route: "/seed-passport",
          labelKey: "seedPassport",
          icon: ShieldCheck,
          badge: "QR",
          highlight: true,
        },
        {
          id: "market",
          route: "/input-marketplace",
          labelKey: "inputMarketplace",
          icon: ShoppingBag,
        },
        {

        },
      ],

  const handleNavClick = (route: AppRoute) => {
    navigate(route);
    setIsMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const toggleGroup = (key: string) => {
    setCollapsedGroups((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-organic-waves">
      {/* Top Banner / Announcement */}
      <div className="bg-forest-900 text-cream-100 text-xs py-1.5 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-forest-800">

              className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${
                demoMode
                  ? "text-amber-400"
                  : syncStatus === "error"
                    ? "text-terracotta-300"
                    : syncStatus === "offline"
                      ? "text-amber-300"
                      : "text-paddy-400"
              }`}
            />
            <span className="hidden md:inline font-medium">
              {demoMode
                ? "DEMO (Local only)"
                : syncStatus === "syncing"
                  ? "Saving to cloud…"
                  : syncStatus === "synced"
                    ? "Cloud Synced"

                        : "Cloud Ready"}
