            {!demoMode && (syncStatus === "error" || syncStatus === "offline") && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => void retryCloudSync()}
                          disabled={syncRetrying}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-sage-500/40 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-cream-50 hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-60"
                          aria-label="Retry cloud sync"
                        >
                          <RotateCw className={`h-3 w-3 ${syncRetrying ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />
                          Retry
                        </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setLanguage(language === "en" ? "ta" : "en")}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-700 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs border border-forest-700 transition-colors"
          >
            <Languages className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{language === "en" ? "தமிழ்" : "English"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Top Header */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-sage-300 shadow-sm px-4 lg:px-8 py-3">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          {/* Logo & Mobile Menu Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(!isMobileMenuOpen)}
              className="lg:hidden p-2 text-forest-800 hover:bg-sage-100 rounded-xl"
              aria-label="Toggle navigation menu"
            >
              {isMobileMenuOpen ? (
                <X className="w-6 h-6" />
              ) : (
                <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />
              )}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/")}
              className="text-left focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-forest-600 rounded-xl p-1"
            >
              <Logo size="md" showTamil={true} />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Actions & Header Controls */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4">
            {/* Active Season Dropdown */}
            <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 bg-cream-200 border border-sage-400/80 rounded-2xl px-3 py-1.5 shadow-sm">
              <Sprout className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              <div className="flex flex-col text-left">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {language === "ta" ? "நடப்பு பருவம்" : "Active Season"}
                </span>
                <select
                  value={activeSeasonId}
                  onChange={(e) => setActiveSeasonId(e.target.value)}
                  className="bg-transparent text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 border-none outline-none cursor-pointer pr-2"
                >
                  {state.seasons.length > 0 ? (
                    state.seasons.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                        {language === "ta"
                          ? s.tamilCropName || s.cropName
                          : s.cropName}{" "}
                        ({s.variety.split("(")[0]})
                      </option>
                    ))
                  ) : (
                    <option value="">No Active Seasons</option>
                  )}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Voice Assistant Mic Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsVoiceModalOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-700 text-harvest-400 font-semibold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"
              title={t.actions.voicePrompt}
            >
              <Mic className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="hidden md:inline font-bold">
                {language === "ta" ? "குரல் உதவி" : "Voice Assistant"}
              </span>
            </button>

            {/* Quick Demo Walkthrough Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/crop-journey")}
              className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-harvest-400 hover:bg-harvest-500 text-forest-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all"
            >
              <PlayCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-900" />
              <span>{language === "ta" ? "முழு பயணம்" : "Full Journey"}</span>
            </button>

            {/* Farmer Avatar & Profile Link */}
            {(session || demoMode) && (
              <button
                onClick={() => void handleSignOut()}
                aria-label={demoMode ? "Exit demo mode" : "Sign out"}
                title={demoMode ? "Exit demo mode" : "Sign out"}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-ink-600 hover:bg-sage-100 hover:text-forest-900 border border-sage-300 transition-colors"
              >
                {demoMode ? (
                  <>
                    <LogIn className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600" />
                    <span className="hidden sm:inline">Exit Demo</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <LogOut className="h-4 w-4 text-ink-500" />
                    <span className="hidden sm:inline">Sign Out</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/profile")}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 pl-2 border-l border-sage-300 hover:opacity-85 transition-opacity"
            >
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-harvest-400 text-sm shadow-sm">
                {state.currentUser.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div className="hidden xl:flex flex-col text-left">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? state.currentUser.tamilName || state.currentUser.name
                    : state.currentUser.name}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-medium">
                  {demoMode
                    ? "Demo account (VIJAY)"
                    : session?.user.email ||
                      `${state.farmer.village}, ${state.farmer.district}`}
                </span>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {signOutError && (
        <p
          role="status"
          className="mx-auto mt-3 w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-7xl rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 bg-terracotta-50 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-terracotta-800"
        >
          {signOutError}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* Main Body with Sidebar + Content */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full">
        {/* Desktop Sidebar Navigation */}
        <aside className="hidden lg:block w-72 shrink-0 py-6 pr-6 border-r border-sage-300">
          <div className="sticky top-24 space-y-6 max-h-[calc(100vh-7rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
            {/* Farmer Identity Badge in Sidebar */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-forest-900 to-forest-800 text-cream-100 rounded-2xl shadow-agro border border-forest-700">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-harvest-400/20 text-harvest-300 border border-harvest-400/30 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-base">
                  🌾
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-sm leading-tight text-cream-50">
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? state.farmer.tamilFullName
                      : state.farmer.fullName}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-sage-300">
                    {state.farmer.totalLandAcres}{" "}
                    {language === "ta" ? "ஏக்கர் நன்செய்" : "Acres Wetland"}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] pt-2 border-t border-forest-700/80">
                <span className="text-harvest-400 font-semibold">
                  {activeSeason.seasonCode}
                </span>
                <span className="bg-paddy-600/80 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold">
                  {activeSeason.stage}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Navigation Groups */}
            <nav className="space-y-4">
              {navGroups.map((group) => {
                const isCollapsed = collapsedGroups[group.groupKey];
                return (
                  <div key={group.groupKey} className="space-y-1">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => toggleGroup(group.groupKey)}
                      className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider text-ink-500 uppercase hover:text-forest-800 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <span>{t.sections[group.groupKey]}</span>
                      <ChevronDown
                        className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${
                          isCollapsed ? "-rotate-90" : "rotate-0"
                        }`}
                      />
                    </button>

                    {!isCollapsed && (
                      <div className="space-y-1 pl-1">
                        {group.items.map((item) => {
                          const isActive = currentRoute === item.route;
                          const Icon = item.icon;
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={item.id}
                              onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.route)}
                              className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                                isActive
                                  ? "bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 shadow-sm font-bold"
                                  : item.highlight
                                    ? "bg-harvest-100/70 text-forest-900 hover:bg-harvest-200/70 border border-harvest-300"
                                    : "text-ink-800 hover:bg-sage-100 hover:text-forest-900"
                              }`}
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                                <Icon
                                  className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${
                                    isActive
                                      ? "text-harvest-400"
                                      : item.highlight
                                        ? "text-terracotta-600"
                                        : "text-forest-700"
                                  }`}
                                />
                                <span className="truncate">
                                  {t.nav[item.labelKey]}
                                </span>
                              </div>

                              {item.badge && (
                                <span
                                  className={`text-[9px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider ${
                                    isActive
                                      ? "bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950"
                                      : item.highlight
                                        ? "bg-terracotta-500 text-white"
                                        : "bg-sage-200 text-forest-800"
                                  }`}
                                >
                                  {item.badge}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </nav>

            {/* Quick System Links */}
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-sage-300 space-y-1">
              <button
                onClick={() => handleNavClick("/onboarding")}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-ink-600 hover:text-forest-800 hover:bg-sage-50 rounded-xl"
              >
                <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
                <span>{t.nav.onboarding}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleNavClick("/login")}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-ink-600 hover:text-forest-800 hover:bg-sage-50 rounded-xl"
              >
                <LogIn className="w-4 h-4 text-terracotta-600" />
                <span>{t.nav.login}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* Mobile Full Navigation Drawer */}
        {isMobileMenuOpen && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 lg:hidden flex flex-col bg-white">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-sage-300 flex items-center justify-between bg-cream-100">
              <Logo size="sm" showTamil={true} />
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="p-2 text-forest-800 hover:bg-sage-200 rounded-full"
              >
                <X className="w-6 h-6" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-6 pb-24">
              {navGroups.map((group) => (
                <div key={group.groupKey}>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                    {t.sections[group.groupKey]}
                  </h4>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1">
                    {group.items.map((item) => {
                      const isActive = currentRoute === item.route;
                      const Icon = item.icon;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={item.id}
                          onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.route)}
                          className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold ${
                            isActive
                              ? "bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold"
                              : "text-ink-800 hover:bg-sage-100"
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                            <span>{t.nav[item.labelKey]}</span>
                          </div>
                          {item.badge && (
                            <span className="text-xs bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                              {item.badge}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Main Content Area */}
        <main className="flex-1 py-6 px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 overflow-x-hidden min-w-0">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Sticky Bottom Navigation Bar */}
      <nav className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-sage-300 shadow-lg px-2 py-2 flex items-center justify-around">
        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavClick("/dashboard")}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center p-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold ${
            currentRoute === "/dashboard" ? "text-forest-800" : "text-ink-500"
          }`}
        >
          <LayoutDashboard className="w-5 h-5 mb-0.5" />
          <span>{language === "ta" ? "மையம்" : "Dashboard"}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavClick("/seed-passport")}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center p-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold ${
            currentRoute === "/seed-passport"
              ? "text-forest-800"
              : "text-ink-500"
          }`}
        >
          <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 mb-0.5" />
          <span>{language === "ta" ? "விதை" : "Seed QR"}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavClick("/crop-journey")}
          className="relative -top-4 flex flex-col items-center bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 p-3 rounded-full shadow-gold border-2 border-harvest-400"
        >
          <ScrollText className="w-6 h-6 animate-pulse" />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider mt-0.5">
            {language === "ta" ? "பயணம்" : "Journey"}
          </span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavClick("/market-prices")}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center p-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold ${
            currentRoute === "/market-prices"
              ? "text-forest-800"
              : "text-ink-500"
          }`}
        >
          <TrendingUp className="w-5 h-5 mb-0.5" />
          <span>{language === "ta" ? "சந்தை" : "Market"}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(true)}
          className="flex flex-col items-center p-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-500 hover:text-forest-800"
        >
          <Menu className="w-5 h-5 mb-0.5" />
          <span>{language === "ta" ? "மெனு" : "All (36)"}</span>
        </button>
      </nav>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="mt-auto bg-forest-900 text-cream-100 py-8 px-4 border-t border-forest-800">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <Logo size="md" showTamil={true} theme="dark" />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-6 text-xs text-sage-300">
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/crop-journey")}
              className="hover:text-harvest-400"
            >
              {t.nav.cropJourney}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/schemes")}
              className="hover:text-harvest-400"
            >
              {t.nav.schemes}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/crop-doctor")}
              className="hover:text-harvest-400"
            >
              {t.nav.cropDoctor}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/soil-health")}
              className="hover:text-harvest-400"
            >
              {t.nav.soilHealth}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/profile")}
              className="hover:text-harvest-400"
            >
              {t.nav.profile}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="text-center md:text-right text-[11px] text-sage-400">
            <p>VITHAI TO ARUVADAI (V2A) — Tamil Nadu Agro Innovation</p>
            <p className="text-harvest-400/90 font-medium">
              விதை முதல் அறுவடை வரை • Delta AgriOS
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\components\modals\VoiceAssistantModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Volume2,
  X,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowRight,
  CornerDownRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation, AppRoute } from "../../context/NavigationContext";

interface SpeechRecognitionResultLike {
  isFinal: boolean;
  0: { transcript: string };
}

interface SpeechRecognitionEventLike {
  resultIndex: number;
  results: ArrayLike<SpeechRecognitionResultLike>;
}

interface BrowserSpeechRecognition {
  lang: string;
  continuous: boolean;
  interimResults: boolean;
  onresult: ((event: SpeechRecognitionEventLike) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((event: { error: string }) => void) | null;
  onend: (() => void) | null;
  start: () => void;
  stop: () => void;
  abort: () => void;
}

type SpeechRecognitionConstructor = new () => BrowserSpeechRecognition;

export const VoiceAssistantModal: React.FC = () => {
  const { isVoiceModalOpen, setIsVoiceModalOpen, language, navigate } =
    useNavigation();
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState(false);
  const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState("");
  const [assistantReply, setAssistantReply] = useState("");
  const [suggestedRoute, setSuggestedRoute] = useState<AppRoute | null>(null);
  const [micError, setMicError] = useState("");
  const recognitionRef = useRef<BrowserSpeechRecognition | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => recognitionRef.current?.abort(), []);

  if (!isVoiceModalOpen) return null;

  const samplePromptsEn = [
    {
      text: "Check Seed Passport for batch TN-TSSCA-2025",
      target: "/seed-passport" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "What is today’s paddy price in Thanjavur Mandi?",
      target: "/market-prices" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "Diagnose leaf spots on Samba paddy",
      target: "/crop-doctor" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "Show total net proceeds and pending payments",
      target: "/crop-journey" as AppRoute,
    },
  ];

  const samplePromptsTa = [
    {
      text: "விதை பாஸ்போர்ட் QR சரிபார்க்கவும்",
      target: "/seed-passport" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "தஞ்சாவூர் நேரடி நெல் கொள்முதல் நிலைய விலை என்ன?",
      target: "/market-prices" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "நெல் இலைச்சுருட்டுப் புழுவுக்கு என்ன மருந்து?",
      target: "/crop-doctor" as AppRoute,
    },
    {
      text: "பயிர் பயண பாஸ்போர்ட் மற்றும் லாப விபரம் காட்டு",
      target: "/crop-journey" as AppRoute,
    },
  ];

  const currentPrompts = language === "ta" ? samplePromptsTa : samplePromptsEn;

  const suggestDestination = (promptText: string): AppRoute => {
    const text = promptText.toLowerCase();
    if (/seed|விதை|qr/.test(text)) return "/seed-passport";
    if (/price|market|விலை|சந்தை/.test(text)) return "/market-prices";
    if (/disease|leaf|crop|நோய்|இலை|பயிர்/.test(text)) return "/crop-doctor";
    if (/finance|profit|payment|லாப|பணம்|வரவு/.test(text))
      return "/crop-journey";
    return "/dashboard";
  };

  const setQuery = (promptText: string, wasSpoken = false) => {
    setIsListening(false);
    setTranscript(promptText);
    const route = suggestDestination(promptText);
    setSuggestedRoute(route);
    setAssistantReply(
      language === "ta"
        ? wasSpoken
          ? "உங்கள் குரல் உரையாக மாற்றப்பட்டது. பரிந்துரைக்கப்பட்ட பகுதியைத் திறக்கலாம்."
          : "மாதிரி உரை தேர்ந்தெடுக்கப்பட்டது. பரிந்துரைக்கப்பட்ட பகுதியைத் திறக்கலாம்."
        : wasSpoken
          ? "Speech converted to text. Review it, then open the suggested section."
          : "Suggested text selected. Review it, then open the suggested section.",
    );
    setMicError("");
  };

  const toggleMic = () => {
    if (isListening) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      return;
    }

    const browserWindow = window as Window & {
      SpeechRecognition?: SpeechRecognitionConstructor;
      webkitSpeechRecognition?: SpeechRecognitionConstructor;
    };
    const Recognition =
      browserWindow.SpeechRecognition || browserWindow.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!Recognition) {
      setMicError(
        "Speech recognition is not supported in this browser. Try Chrome or Edge, or type/use a suggested query.",
      );
      return;
    }

    setTranscript("");
    setAssistantReply("");
    setSuggestedRoute(null);
    setMicError("");
    const recognition = new Recognition();
    recognition.lang = language === "ta" ? "ta-IN" : "en-IN";
    recognition.continuous = false;
    recognition.interimResults = true;
    recognition.onresult = (event) => {
      let text = "";
      let finalResult = false;
      for (
        let index = event.resultIndex;
        index < event.results.length;
        index += 1
      ) {
        text += event.results[index][0].transcript;
        finalResult ||= event.results[index].isFinal;
      }
      setTranscript(text.trim());
      if (finalResult && text.trim()) setQuery(text.trim(), true);
    };
    recognition.onerror = (event) => {
      setIsListening(false);
      setMicError(
        event.error === "not-allowed"
          ? "Microphone permission was denied. Allow it in browser site settings and try again."
          : `Speech recognition stopped (${event.error}). Try again or use a suggested query.`,
      );
    };
    recognition.onend = () => setIsListening(false);
    recognitionRef.current = recognition;
    try {
      recognition.start();
      setIsListening(true);
    } catch {
      setIsListening(false);
      setMicError(
        "Could not start microphone recognition. Check site permission and try again.",
      );
    }
  };

  const openSuggestedRoute = () => {
    if (!suggestedRoute) return;
    recognitionRef.current?.abort();
    setIsVoiceModalOpen(false);
    navigate(suggestedRoute);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start sm:items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
      <div className="relative my-auto w-full max-w-lg max-h-[calc(100vh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto bg-cream-100 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border-2 border-forest-600/30">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-between p-5 bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 via-forest-800 to-paddy-700 text-cream-50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 bg-harvest-500/20 text-harvest-300 rounded-xl border border-harvest-400/40">
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "V2A உழவர் குரல் உதவியாளர்"
                  : "V2A Farmer Voice Assistant"}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-sage-200">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "தமிழ் & ஆங்கிலத்தில் பேசலாம் (Voice AI)"
                  : "Bilingual Agro Voice Engine"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              recognitionRef.current?.abort();
              setIsListening(false);
              setIsVoiceModalOpen(false);
            }}
            aria-label="Close voice assistant"
            className="p-1.5 text-sage-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-forest-700/50 rounded-full transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Voice Animation Area */}
        <div className="p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
          <div className="relative mb-6">
            {isListening && (
              <>
                <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full bg-harvest-400/30 animate-ping" />
                <div className="absolute -inset-4 rounded-full bg-paddy-500/20 animate-pulse" />
              </>
            )}
            <button
              onClick={toggleMic}
              aria-label={
                isListening
                  ? "Stop microphone listening"
                  : "Start microphone listening"
              }
              className={`relative z-10 w-24 h-24 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 ${
                isListening
                  ? "bg-terracotta-500 text-white scale-110 shadow-terracotta-500/40"
                  : "bg-forest-800 text-harvest-400 hover:bg-forest-700 hover:scale-105"
              }`}
            >
              {isListening ? (
                <Mic className="w-10 h-10 animate-bounce" />
              ) : (
                <Mic className="w-10 h-10" />
              )}
            </button>
          </div>

          <p className="font-semibold text-forest-900 mb-1">
            {isListening
              ? language === "ta"
                ? "உங்கள் குரலை கவனிக்கிறது..."
                : "Listening to your speech..."
              : language === "ta"
                ? "பேச மைக்கை அழுத்தவும்"
                : "Tap microphone to speak"}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mb-6">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விதை, நோய், உரம், விலை அல்லது சாகுபடி விவரங்களை கேட்கலாம்"
              : "Ask about seeds, crop disease, fertilizers, market prices, or finances"}
          </p>
          {micError && (
            <p
              role="alert"
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 bg-terracotta-50 p-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-terracotta-800"
            >
              {micError}
            </p>
          )}

          {/* Transcript / Reply Display */}
          {(transcript || assistantReply) && (
            <div className="w-full p-4 mb-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 text-left shadow-sm">
              {transcript && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 mb-2">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-400 uppercase tracking-wider shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    {language === "ta" ? "நீங்கள்:" : "You:"}
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-ink-900">
                    {transcript}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
              {assistantReply && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                  <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-forest-800">
                    {assistantReply}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
              {suggestedRoute && (
                <button
                  onClick={openSuggestedRoute}
                  className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-harvest-300"
                >
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "பரிந்துரைத்த பகுதியைத் திற"
                    : `Open suggested section: ${suggestedRoute}`}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Quick Clickable Farmer Queries */}
          <div className="w-full text-left">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-500 mb-2">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "மாதிரி கேள்விகள் (தட்டவும்):"
                : "Suggested farmer queries (Tap to test):"}
            </p>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {currentPrompts.map((p, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={idx}
                  onClick={() => setQuery(p.text)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-forest-50 border border-sage-300 hover:border-paddy-500 text-left text-sm text-forest-900 font-medium transition-all group"
                >
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <CornerDownRight className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
                    {p.text}
                  </span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-400 group-hover:text-forest-800 transition-colors" />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer info */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-sage-100/60 border-t border-sage-200 text-center text-xs text-ink-600">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "உலாவி பேச்சு-உரை சேவை தேவை. மொழி ஆதரவு உலாவி மற்றும் இணைய இணைப்பைப் பொறுத்தது."
            : "Uses browser speech recognition. Support and network requirements vary by browser; audio is not sent to this app backend."}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\context\AuthContext.tsx
================================================================================
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabase, isCloudSyncEnabled } from '../services/supabase';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';
import type { User as AppUser } from '../types';

const DEMO_MODE_KEY = 'v2a_demo_mode';

interface AuthContextValue {
  cloudEnabled: boolean;
  session: Session | null;
  authInitialized: boolean;
  ready: boolean;
  demoMode: boolean;
  setupError: string | null;
  passwordRecovery: boolean;
  signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  signUp: (email: string, password: string, displayName: string) => Promise<{ confirmationRequired: boolean }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<string | null>;
  startDemo: () => Promise<void>;
  sendPasswordReset: (email: string) => Promise<void>;
  updatePassword: (password: string) => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

function debugAuth(message: string): void {
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug(`[AUTH] ${message}`);
}

function getAuthErrorMessage(error: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) return error.message;
  return fallback;
}

function activateSavedDemo(): void {
  localStorage.setItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY, 'true');
  AppStore.activateDemoMode();
}

function toAppUser(sessionUser: Session['user']): AppUser {
  const metadata = sessionUser.user_metadata;
  const name =
    typeof metadata.full_name === 'string' && metadata.full_name.trim()
      ? metadata.full_name.trim()
      : sessionUser.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Farmer';
  return {
    id: sessionUser.id,
    phone: sessionUser.phone ?? '',
    name,
    tamilName: typeof metadata.tamil_name === 'string' ? metadata.tamil_name : undefined,
    role: 'farmer',
  };
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const cloudEnabled = isCloudSyncEnabled;
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [authInitialized, setAuthInitialized] = useState(!cloudEnabled);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(!cloudEnabled);
  const [demoMode, setDemoMode] = useState(() => !cloudEnabled || localStorage.getItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY) === 'true');
  const [setupError, setSetupError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [passwordRecovery, setPasswordRecovery] = useState(false);
  const activatedUserId = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const sessionRef = useRef<Session | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) {
      activateSavedDemo();
      return;
    }

    let disposed = false;
    let authEventRevision = 0;
    let initialSessionEventReceived = false;
    let initialSessionSettled = false;
    let authChangeAfterInitialSession = false;
    const handleSession = (nextSession: Session | null) => {
      if (disposed) return;
      const nextUserId = nextSession?.user.id ?? null;

      if (nextUserId) {
        debugAuth("Session established.");
        localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY);
        setDemoMode(false);
        if (activatedUserId.current !== nextUserId) {
          AppStore.clearCloudUser();
          activatedUserId.current = nextUserId;
          setSetupError(null);
          setReady(false);
        }
      } else {
        debugAuth("No active session.");
        const resumeDemo = localStorage.getItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY) === 'true';
        activatedUserId.current = null;
        AppStore.clearCloudUser();
        setDemoMode(resumeDemo);
        if (resumeDemo) AppStore.activateDemoMode();
        setSetupError(null);
        setReady(true);
      }
      sessionRef.current = nextSession;
      setSession(nextSession);
    };

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, nextSession) => {
      if (event === 'INITIAL_SESSION') {
        if (initialSessionEventReceived || initialSessionSettled || authChangeAfterInitialSession) return;
        initialSessionEventReceived = true;
        initialSessionSettled = true;
      } else {
        authChangeAfterInitialSession = true;
      }
      authEventRevision += 1;
      setAuthInitialized(true);
      debugAuth(`State changed: ${event}.`);
      if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') setPasswordRecovery(true);
      if (event === 'SIGNED_OUT') setPasswordRecovery(false);
      handleSession(nextSession);
    });

    const initialEventRevision = authEventRevision;
    void supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data, error }) => {
      if (disposed || authEventRevision !== initialEventRevision) return;
      if (error) {
        setSetupError(`Unable to check your sign-in session: ${error.message}`);
        setReady(true);
        setAuthInitialized(true);
        return;
      }
      initialSessionSettled = true;
      handleSession(data.session);
      setAuthInitialized(true);
    }).catch((error: unknown) => {
      if (disposed || authEventRevision !== initialEventRevision) return;
      setSetupError(`Unable to check your sign-in session: ${getAuthErrorMessage(error, 'Please try again.')}`);
      setReady(true);
      setAuthInitialized(true);
    });

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const sessionUserId = session?.user.id;
  useEffect(() => {
    const authUser = sessionRef.current?.user;
    if (!supabase || !authUser || authUser.id !== sessionUserId) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    const user = toAppUser(authUser);
    void AppStore.activateCloudUser(user).then(() => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      activatedUserId.current = user.id;
      setSetupError(null);
      debugAuth("Account data loaded.");
    }).catch((error: unknown) => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      debugAuth("Account data could not be loaded.");
      setSetupError(`Unable to load your account data: ${getAuthErrorMessage(error, 'Please try again.')}`);
    }).finally(() => {
      if (!cancelled) setReady(true);
    });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [sessionUserId]);

  const signIn = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    if (!supabase) throw new Error('Cloud sign-in is not configured. You can continue with the local demo.');
    debugAuth("Password sign-in started.");
    setReady(false);
    setSetupError(null);
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
      if (error) throw error;
      if (!data.session?.user.id) throw new Error('Sign-in completed without an active session. Please try again.');
      localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY);
      setDemoMode(false);
      sessionRef.current = data.session;
      setSession(data.session);
      setAuthInitialized(true);
      debugAuth("Password sign-in succeeded; waiting for account data.");
    } catch (error) {
      setReady(true);
      throw error;
    }
  }, []);

  const signUp = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string, displayName: string) => {
    if (!supabase) throw new Error('Cloud sign-up is not configured. You can continue with the local demo.');
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
      email,
      password,
      options: { data: { full_name: displayName.trim() } },
    });
    if (error) throw error;
    if (data.session) {
      localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY);
      setDemoMode(false);
      setReady(false);
      sessionRef.current = data.session;
      setSession(data.session);
    }
    return { confirmationRequired: !data.session };
  }, []);

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    let syncError: unknown = null;
    if (session && supabase) {
      try {
        await AppStore.flushCloudSync();
      } catch (error) {
        syncError = error;
      }

      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut();
      if (error) throw error;
    }

    localStorage.removeItem(DEMO_MODE_KEY);
    sessionRef.current = null;
    setSession(null);
    setDemoMode(false);
    setPasswordRecovery(false);
    setSetupError(null);
    activatedUserId.current = null;
    AppStore.clearCloudUser();

    if (syncError) {
      return 'You are signed out, but the latest changes may not have synced. Sign in again when online to retry.';
    }
    return null;
  }, [session]);

  const startDemo = useCallback(async () => {
    if (session && supabase) {
      let syncError: unknown = null;
      try {
        await AppStore.flushCloudSync();
      } catch (error) {
        syncError = error;
      }
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut();
      if (error) throw error;
      if (syncError) {
        throw new Error('Your account has been signed out, but some changes may not have synced. Please reconnect and sign in again before using demo mode.');
      }
    }

    activateSavedDemo();
    activatedUserId.current = null;
    sessionRef.current = null;
    setSession(null);
    setDemoMode(true);
    setPasswordRecovery(false);
    setSetupError(null);
    setReady(true);
  }, [session]);

  const sendPasswordReset = useCallback(async (email: string) => {
    if (!supabase) throw new Error('Password recovery is not configured.');
    const redirectTo = `${window.location.origin}/#/reset-password`;
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, { redirectTo });
    if (error) throw error;
  }, []);

  const updatePassword = useCallback(async (password: string) => {
    if (!supabase) throw new Error('Password updates are not configured.');
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password });
    if (error) throw error;

    const { error: signOutError } = await supabase.auth.signOut();
    if (signOutError) throw signOutError;
    setPasswordRecovery(false);
    sessionRef.current = null;
    setSession(null);
    setDemoMode(false);
    setReady(true);
    AppStore.clearCloudUser();
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(() => ({
    cloudEnabled,
    session,
    authInitialized,
    ready,
    demoMode,
    setupError,
    passwordRecovery,
    signIn,
    signUp,
    signOut,
    startDemo,
    sendPasswordReset,
    updatePassword,
  }), [cloudEnabled, session, authInitialized, ready, demoMode, setupError, passwordRecovery, signIn, signUp, signOut, startDemo, sendPasswordReset, updatePassword]);

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return context;
}

FILE: src\context\NavigationContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Language } from "../types";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export type AppRoute =
  | "/"
  | "/login"
  | "/signup"
  | "/forgot-password"
  | "/reset-password"
  | "/onboarding"
  | "/dashboard"
  | "/my-farm"
  | "/crop-planner"
  | "/soil-health"
  | "/weather"
  | "/budget-planner"
  | "/seed-passport"
  | "/input-marketplace"
  | "/input-locker"
  | "/machinery"
  | "/labour"
  | "/farm-calendar"
  | "/irrigation"
  | "/crop-doctor"
  | "/expert-advice"
  | "/crop-health"
  | "/insurance"
  | "/harvest"
  | "/buyer-discovery"
  | "/storage"
  | "/logistics"
  | "/market-prices"
  | "/buyers"
  | "/offers"
  | "/net-proceeds"
  | "/sales"
  | "/payments"
  | "/finance"
  | "/season-comparison"
  | "/next-season"
  | "/schemes"
  | "/crop-journey"
  | "/profile";

interface NavigationContextType {
  currentRoute: AppRoute;
  navigate: (route: AppRoute) => void;
  language: Language;
  setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
  activeSeasonId: string;
  setActiveSeasonId: (id: string) => void;
  isVoiceModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsVoiceModalOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  refreshTrigger: number;
  triggerRefresh: () => void;
}

const NavigationContext = createContext<NavigationContextType | undefined>(
  undefined,
);

export const NavigationProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({
  children,
}) => {
  // Read initial route from location hash or pathname
  const getInitialRoute = (): AppRoute => {
    const fullHash = window.location.hash;
    if (fullHash.includes("type=recovery")) {
      return "/reset-password";
    }
    const hash = fullHash.replace("#", "");
    if (hash && hash.startsWith("/")) {
      return hash.split(/[?#&]/, 1)[0] as AppRoute;
    }
    const path = window.location.pathname;
    if (path && path !== "/" && path.length > 1) {
      return path as AppRoute;
    }
    return "/dashboard";
  };

  const [currentRoute, setCurrentRoute] = useState<AppRoute>(getInitialRoute);
  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(
    () => AppStore.getState().language,
  );
  const [activeSeasonId, setActiveSeasonIdState] = useState<string>(
    () => AppStore.getState().activeSeasonId,
  );
  const [isVoiceModalOpen, setIsVoiceModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState<number>(0);

  const triggerRefresh = () => {
    const currentState = AppStore.getState();
    setLanguageState(currentState.language);
    setActiveSeasonIdState(currentState.activeSeasonId);
    setRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
  };

  const navigate = useCallback((route: AppRoute) => {
    if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug(`[ROUTE] Navigating to ${route}.`);
    window.location.hash = route;
    setCurrentRoute(route);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  }, []);

  const setLanguage = (lang: Language) => {
    AppStore.setLanguage(lang);
    setLanguageState(lang);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  const setActiveSeasonId = (id: string) => {
    AppStore.setActiveSeason(id);
    setActiveSeasonIdState(id);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleHashChange = () => {
      const fullHash = window.location.hash;
      if (fullHash.includes("type=recovery")) {
        setCurrentRoute("/reset-password");
        return;
      }
      if (fullHash.includes("access_token=") || fullHash.includes("error_description=")) {
        return;
      }
      const hash = fullHash.replace("#", "");
      if (hash && hash.startsWith("/")) {
        setCurrentRoute(hash.split(/[?#&]/, 1)[0] as AppRoute);
      } else {
        const path = window.location.pathname;
        setCurrentRoute(
          (path && path !== "/" ? path : "/dashboard") as AppRoute,
        );
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", handleHashChange);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", handleHashChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <NavigationContext.Provider
      value={{
        currentRoute,
        navigate,
        language,
        setLanguage,
        activeSeasonId,
        setActiveSeasonId,
        isVoiceModalOpen,
        setIsVoiceModalOpen,
        refreshTrigger,
        triggerRefresh,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </NavigationContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useNavigation = () => {
  const context = useContext(NavigationContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useNavigation must be used within a NavigationProvider");
  }
  return context;
};

  height: 8px;
}

  background: #F1F6ED;
}
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: #C2D6B5;
  border-radius: 9999px;

  background: #477A42;
}

/* Tamil font class */
.font-tamil {
  font-family: 'Noto Sans Tamil', system-ui, sans-serif;
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FileSpreadsheet, CircleDollarSign, TrendingUp, Calculator, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, PieChart } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';

export const BudgetPlannerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();

  const [acres, setAcres] = useState(3.5);
  const [seedCost, setSeedCost] = useState(1800);
  const [ploughCost, setPloughCost] = useState(3200);
  const [plantingCost, setPlantingCost] = useState(4200);
  const [nutrientCost, setNutrientCost] = useState(4000);
  const [weedingCost, setWeedingCost] = useState(2800);
  const [harvestCost, setHarvestCost] = useState(4500);
  const [logisticsCost, setLogisticsCost] = useState(1000);

  // Revenue projections
  const [yieldBagsPerAcre, setYieldBagsPerAcre] = useState(32);
  const [pricePerBag, setPricePerBag] = useState(2280);

  // Mathematical Calculations
  const costPerAcre = seedCost + ploughCost + plantingCost + nutrientCost + weedingCost + harvestCost + logisticsCost;
  const totalCost = costPerAcre * acres;

  const totalBags = Math.round(yieldBagsPerAcre * acres);
  const totalKg = totalBags * 75;
  const grossRevenue = totalBags * pricePerBag;
  const netProfit = grossRevenue - totalCost;
  const roiPct = totalCost > 0 ? Math.round((netProfit / totalCost) * 100) : 0;
  const costPerKg = totalKg > 0 ? (totalCost / totalKg).toFixed(2) : '0';

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Calculator className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'சாகுபடி செலவு & லாபக் கணக்கீட்டாளர்' : 'Precision Crop Cultivation Budget Planner'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'சாகுபடி பட்ஜெட் கால்குலேட்டர்' : 'Per-Acre Farm Budget Calculator'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'விதை முதல் அறுவடை வரை விரிவான செலவு மற்றும் லாப மதிப்பீடு' : 'Input variables and analyze expected returns, cost per kg, and seasonal ROI'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/crop-journey')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'உண்மையான பயிர் வரவு-செலவு' : 'View Actual Farm Ledger'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Top Real-time Projected Margin Summary Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 via-forest-800 to-paddy-900 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-cream-100 shadow-agro grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider">{language === 'ta' ? 'மொத்த சாகுபடி செலவு' : 'Total Expenses'}</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-cream-50 mt-1">₹{Math.round(totalCost).toLocaleString()}</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-sage-300 font-medium">₹{costPerAcre.toLocaleString()} / acre</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider">{language === 'ta' ? 'எதிர்பார்க்கப்படும் விற்பனை' : 'Gross Revenue'}</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-harvest-300 mt-1">₹{Math.round(grossRevenue).toLocaleString()}</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-sage-300 font-medium">{totalBags} bags @ ₹{pricePerBag}</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider">{language === 'ta' ? 'நிகர லாபம் (Profit)' : 'Projected Net Margin'}</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-paddy-400 mt-1">₹{Math.round(netProfit).toLocaleString()}</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-paddy-300 font-bold">ROI: {roiPct}% return</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider">{language === 'ta' ? 'உற்பத்தி அடக்க விலை' : 'Cost of Production'}</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-cream-50 mt-1">₹{costPerKg} <span className="text-xs font-normal">/ kg</span></p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-sage-300 font-medium">MSP Benchmark: ₹23.20/kg</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Calculator Interactive Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left 2 Cols: Expense Parameters */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-sage-200">
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === 'ta' ? 'ஏக்கர் வாரியான செலவுகள் (திருத்தலாம்)' : 'Per-Acre Cost Variables (Editable)'}
            </h3>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-600">Land Area:</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                step="0.5"
                value={acres}
                onChange={(e) => setAcres(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 1)}
                className="w-20 px-2 py-1 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-center text-forest-900"
              />
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-600">Acres</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 mb-1">
                {language === 'ta' ? '1. விதை & நாற்றங்கால் செலவு' : '1. Seeds & Nursery Prep'}
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={seedCost}
                onChange={(e) => setSeedCost(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-white border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
              />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-1">Certified seed bags + Azospirillum + polythene sheet</p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 mb-1">
                {language === 'ta' ? '2. உழவு & சேற்றுழவு இயந்திரம்' : '2. Tractor Ploughing & Puddling'}
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={ploughCost}
                onChange={(e) => setPloughCost(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-white border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
              />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-1">Summer dry rotavator + 2 passes puddle levelling</p>

              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={plantingCost}
                onChange={(e) => setPlantingCost(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-white border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Store, Search, Filter, ShieldCheck, Star, Clock, ArrowRight, UserCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const BuyerDiscoveryPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('All');

  const buyers = state.buyers.filter((b) => filterType === 'All' || b.buyerType === filterType);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Store className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'நேரடி கொள்முதல் வாங்குவோர்' : 'Direct Institutional Buyers & Rice Mills'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'வாங்குவோர் வலையமைப்பு' : 'Buyer Discovery Network'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'மாதிரி கொள்முதல் நிறுவனங்கள், உழவர் உற்பத்தியாளர் நிறுவனங்கள் மற்றும் நேரடி கொள்முதல் மையங்கள் (Demo)'
              : 'Demonstration catalog of regional food aggregators, millers, and procurement centers'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/offers')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'வந்திருக்கும் ஏலங்கள்' : 'Active Buyer Bids'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Chips */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {['All', 'Modern Rice Mill', 'FPO Aggregator', 'Government DPC (Direct Purchase Centre)'].map((type) => (
          <button
            key={type}
            onClick={() => setFilterType(type)}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              filterType === type
                ? 'bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-white text-ink-700 border border-sage-300 hover:bg-sage-50'
            }`}
          >
            {type}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Buyer Directory Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {buyers.map((buyer) => (
          <div
            key={buyer.id}
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-paddy-500 transition-all"
          >
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  {buyer.buyerType}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 bg-harvest-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg">
                  <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-harvest-600 text-harvest-600" />
                  {buyer.rating}
                </span>
              </div>

              <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900 leading-snug">
                {language === 'ta' ? buyer.tamilName : buyer.name}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1">{buyer.organization} • {buyer.district}</p>

              <div className="my-4 p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1.5 text-xs text-ink-700">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">GST Registration:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-forest-900">{buyer.gstNumber}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">Payment Turnaround:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-paddy-700">{buyer.averagePaymentDays} Days Settlement</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">Procuring Crops:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-forest-900">{buyer.targetCrops.join(', ')}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-sage-200">
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/offers')}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'விலை சலுகையை காண்க' : 'View Tender Offers'}</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                  {buyer.organization} • {buyer.district} District

              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-right">

                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Settlement Speed</span>
                  <span className="font-display font-extrabold text-base text-forest-900">

                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Farmer Rating</span>
                  <span className="font-display font-extrabold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                    <Star className="w-4 h-4 fill-harvest-500 text-harvest-500" />
                    {buyer.rating}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs">
              <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 block">FSSAI Licence:</span>
                <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-forest-900">{buyer.fssaiNumber}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 block">GST Identification:</span>
                <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-forest-900">{buyer.gstNumber}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 block">Target Procurement:</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-forest-900">{buyer.targetCrops.join(', ')}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\CropDoctorPage.tsx
