import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Stethoscope,
  Upload,
  Camera,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Sparkles,
  HelpCircle,
  ShieldCheck,
  Send,
  Info,
  Layers,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { compressImageToDataUrl } from "../utils/images";

export const CropDoctorPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [selectedCrop, setSelectedCrop] = useState("Paddy (CR 1009 Sub 1)");
  const [symptomText, setSymptomText] = useState(
    "Longitudinal folded leaves with white scraped patches",
  );
  const [isDiagnosing, setIsDiagnosing] = useState(false);
  const [activeDiagnosis, setActiveDiagnosis] = useState<any>(
    state.cropObservations[0]?.aiDiagnosis || null,
  );
  const [requestExpert, setRequestExpert] = useState(false);
  const [expertSubmitted, setExpertSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [photoDataUrl, setPhotoDataUrl] = useState("");
  const [photoName, setPhotoName] = useState("");
  const [photoError, setPhotoError] = useState("");

  const sampleDiseases = [
    {
      name: "Paddy Leaf Folder (Cnaphalocrocis medinalis)",
      tamilName: "நெல் இலைச்சுருட்டுப் புழு",
      confidence: 89,
      severity: "Moderate",
      symptoms: [
        "Folded leaf blades",
        "White longitudinal feeding patches",
        "Presence of larvae silk",
      ],
      cause:
        "Aggravated by cloudy humidity and high split application of urea.",
      remedy: [
        "Release Trichogramma chilonis egg parasitoid cards @ 2 cc/acre",
        "Foliar spray of 10,000 ppm Neem Oil @ 2 ml/litre with sticker solution",
        "Avoid excessive top-dressing of inorganic nitrogen during peak overcast spells",
      ],
    },
    {
      name: "Bacterial Leaf Blight (Xanthomonas oryzae)",
      tamilName: "பாக்டீரியா இலைக்கருகல் நோய்",
      confidence: 84,
      severity: "High",
      symptoms: [
        "Wavy margins turning yellow and straw-colored",
        "Milky bacterial ooze on young cut stems",
      ],
      cause:
        "Warm temperature and cyclonic windy monsoon rain splashing bacteria.",
      remedy: [
        "Spray Streptocycline 15g + Copper Oxychloride 500g in 200L water per acre",
        "Drain excess standing water from field for 48 hours to aerate rootzone",
        "Apply additional 25 kg Muriate of Potash (MOP) to harden cell walls",
      ],
    },
  ];

  const handleDiagnose = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDiagnosing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsDiagnosing(false);
      const chosen = symptomText.toLowerCase().includes("blight")
        ? sampleDiseases[1]
        : sampleDiseases[0];
      const newDiag = {
        diseaseName: chosen.name,
        tamilDiseaseName: chosen.tamilName,
        confidencePct: chosen.confidence,
        severity: chosen.severity as any,
        scientificCause: chosen.cause,
        recommendedRemedy: chosen.remedy,
        isMockSimulation: true,
      };
      setActiveDiagnosis(newDiag);

      AppStore.addCropObservation({
        seasonId: activeSeasonId,
        date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
        stage: "Active Vegetative Tillering",
        symptoms: [symptomText],
        imageUrl: photoDataUrl || undefined,
        aiDiagnosis: newDiag,
        expertReviewRequested: requestExpert,
        status: "Reported",
      });
      triggerRefresh();
    }, 1200);
  };

  const handlePhotoChange = async (file?: File) => {
    if (!file) return;
    setPhotoError("");
    try {
      setPhotoDataUrl(await compressImageToDataUrl(file));
      setPhotoName(file.name);
    } catch (error) {
      setPhotoError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load this image.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleEscalateExpert = () => {
    AppStore.addExpertRequest({
      farmerId: state.farmer.id,
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      expertName: "Agronomy review queue (demo)",
      institution: "Local demo request queue",
      topic: `Crop observation: ${selectedCrop}`,
      question: `${symptomText}${photoName ? ` Photo attached: ${photoName}` : ""}`,
      status: "Pending Review",
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
    });
    setExpertSubmitted(true);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Stethoscope className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta" ? "பயிர் அறிகுறி டெமோ" : "Crop Symptom Demo"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பயிர் மருத்துவர் ஆலோசனை"
              : "Crop Doctor Advisory"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "இலை அறிகுறிகளை ஆய்வு செய்து இயற்கை உயிரி பாதுகாப்பு முறைகளை பரிந்துரைக்கும் தளம்"
              : "Attach a crop photo and symptoms to save a local advisory record. Diagnosis is a sample symptom-rule demo, not computer vision or expert validation."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/expert-advice")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விஞ்ஞானியிடம் ஆலோசனை"
              : "KVK Agronomist Forum"}
          </span>
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/soil-health")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-sage-300 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "மண் புகைப்படம் / பரிசோதனை"
            : "Soil Photo & Test Entry"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Mandatory Disclaimer (Requirement E) */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border-l-4 border-harvest-500 text-xs text-forest-900 flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm">
        <Info className="w-5 h-5 text-harvest-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <p className="font-bold">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "AI நோய் கண்டறிதல் வெளிப்படைத்தன்மை குறிப்பு:"
              : "Sample Advisory Limitations:"}
          </p>
          <p className="text-ink-600 leading-relaxed">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "இந்த ஆய்வு மாதிரி செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு மாதிரியாகும் (Demo AI Result). இது விவசாயிகளுக்கு ஆரம்ப வழிகாட்டுதலை மட்டுமே வழங்குகிறது. முக்கியமான பூச்சி தாக்குதலுக்கு உள்ளூர் வேளாண் அதிகாரியை தொடர்பு கொள்ளவும்."
              : "The sample advisory below is selected from symptom text only; the photo is attached to your local record but is not analyzed. This is not a diagnosis. Ask a local agricultural expert before treating crops."}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Upload & Symptom Form Matrix */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left Col: Upload & Input */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "இலை புகைப்படம் & அறிகுறிகள்"
                : "Capture Leaf Symptoms"}
            </span>
          </h3>

          <form onSubmit={handleDiagnose} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Target Crop
              </label>
              <select
                value={selectedCrop}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedCrop(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="Paddy (CR 1009 Sub 1)">
                  Paddy (CR 1009 Sub 1)
                </option>
                <option value="Blackgram (VBN 8)">Blackgram (VBN 8)</option>
                <option value="Sesame">Sesame (TMV 7)</option>
                <option value="Banana">Grand Naine Banana</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Photo Upload Area */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Leaf Photo
              </label>
              <label className="block border-2 border-dashed border-sage-300 rounded-2xl p-4 text-center bg-cream-50 hover:bg-cream-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                <Upload className="w-8 h-8 text-paddy-600 mx-auto mb-1" />
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {photoName ||
                    (language === "ta"
                      ? "புகைப்படம் எடுக்க / பதிவேற்ற தட்டவும்"
                      : "Take or upload a crop photo")}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  Image stays in this browser demo and is not sent to an AI
                  service.
                </p>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*"
                  capture="environment"
                  className="sr-only"
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    void handlePhotoChange(event.target.files?.[0])
                  }
                />
              </label>
              {photoError && (
                <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-terracotta-700">
                  {photoError}
                </p>
              )}
              {photoDataUrl && (
                <img
                  src={photoDataUrl}
                  alt="Selected crop for the local observation record"
                  className="max-h-56 w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 object-contain bg-cream-50"
                />
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Symptom Description
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={symptomText}
                onChange={(e) => setSymptomText(e.target.value)}
                rows={3}
                required
                className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-ink-900"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                id="reqExp"
                checked={requestExpert}
                onChange={(e) => setRequestExpert(e.target.checked)}
                className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-800 rounded border-sage-300"
              />
              <label
                htmlFor="reqExp"
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-forest-900"
              >
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "KVK வேளாண் விஞ்ஞானி ஆய்வை கோருக"
                  : "Save Local Expert Review Request"}
              </label>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isDiagnosing}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
              <span>
                {isDiagnosing
                  ? "Analyzing Symptoms..."
                  : language === "ta"
                    ? "மாதிரி அறிகுறி ஆலோசனை காட்டு"
                    : "Show Sample Symptom Suggestion"}
              </span>
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Right 2 Cols: Diagnostic Certificate Card */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          {activeDiagnosis ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border-2 border-paddy-600/40 shadow-agro space-y-6">
              {/* Header with Uncertainty Metric */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-sage-200">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      Sample Symptom-Rule Match · Not AI
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase ${
                        activeDiagnosis.severity === "Severe" ||
                        activeDiagnosis.severity === "High"
                          ? "bg-terracotta-100 text-terracotta-800"
                          : "bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900"
                      }`}
                    >
                      Severity: {activeDiagnosis.severity}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-2">
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? activeDiagnosis.tamilDiseaseName
                      : activeDiagnosis.diseaseName}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 mt-0.5 italic">
                    {activeDiagnosis.scientificCause}
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* AI Confidence Gauge */}
                <div className="shrink-0 text-right">
                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-2xl font-black text-base shadow-sm">
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                    <span>{activeDiagnosis.confidencePct}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 mt-1 uppercase">
                    Demo Rule Score
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Prescribed Biological Protocol (Requirement E) */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <h4 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                  <span>
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? "பரிந்துரைக்கப்பட்ட உயிரி பாதுகாப்பு முறைகள்"
                      : "Recommended Integrated Pest Management (IPM)"}
                  </span>
                </h4>

                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {activeDiagnosis.recommendedRemedy.map(
                    (step: string, idx: number) => (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50/60"
                      >
                        <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-paddy-700 text-white font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-xs shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                          {idx + 1}
                        </span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-forest-900 leading-relaxed">
                          {step}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    ),
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Action Buttons: Request Expert Validation or View Locker */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  onClick={handleEscalateExpert}
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all"
                >
                  <Send className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                  <span>
                    {expertSubmitted
                      ? language === "ta"
                        ? "உள்ளூர் டெமோ கோரிக்கை சேமிக்கப்பட்டது"
                        : "Saved to local demo request queue"
                      : language === "ta"
                        ? "உள்ளூர் மதிப்பாய்வு கோரிக்கையைச் சேமி"
                        : "Save Local Expert Review Request"}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <button
                  onClick={() => navigate("/input-marketplace")}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-sage-300 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-sage-100 transition-colors"
                >
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "உயிரி மருந்து வாங்க"
                    : "Buy Neem Oil / Bio-Pesticide"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-12 text-center border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-ink-600">
                Enter symptoms or upload a leaf photo to generate diagnostic
                advisory.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Satellite, Activity, ShieldCheck, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Layers, Sprout } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const CropHealthPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const stages = [
    { name: 'Nursery Bed', status: 'Completed', date: 'Aug 28' },
    { name: 'Machine Transplanting', status: 'Completed', date: 'Sep 15' },
    { name: 'Active Tillering (AWD)', status: 'Completed', date: 'Oct 15' },
    { name: 'Panicle Emergence', status: 'Completed', date: 'Nov 20' },
    { name: 'Grain Filling & Maturity', status: 'Completed', date: 'Dec 25' },

          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-paddy-600 h-full rounded-full" style={{ width: '78%' }} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">Dense chlorophyll absorption; zero nitrogen starvation.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">Leaf Area Index (LAI)</span>
            <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              Optimal
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">4.6 m²/m²</p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-forest-700 h-full rounded-full" style={{ width: '85%' }} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">Uniform canopy closure across 3.5 acres.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">Crop Water Stress</span>
            <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              Hydrated
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">0.12</p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-paddy-500 h-full rounded-full" style={{ width: '15%' }} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold">Zero root drought stress detected.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Growth Stage Progression Timeline */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'பயிர் வளர்ச்சி நிலைகள்' : 'Phenological Growth Milestones'}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
          {stages.map((stg, idx) => (
            <div
              key={idx}
              className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 text-center space-y-2"
            >
              <div className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto rounded-full bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-xs">
                {idx + 1}
              </div>
              <p className="font-bold text-xs text-forest-900 leading-tight">{stg.name}</p>
              <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold text-paddy-800 bg-paddy-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                {stg.status}
              </span>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500">{stg.date}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ScrollText,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  CircleDollarSign,
  TrendingUp,
  Clock,
  Plus,
  Printer,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertTriangle,
  Scale,
  Wheat,
  Sprout,
  Truck,
  Store,
  Receipt,
  FileCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { JourneyEvent } from "../types";

export const CropJourneyPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const activeSeason =
    state.seasons.find((s) => s.id === activeSeasonId) || state.seasons[0];
  const financials = AppStore.calculateSeasonFinancials(activeSeason.id);
  const seasonSales = state.sales.filter(
    (sale) => sale.seasonId === activeSeason.id,
  );
  const seasonBagsSold = seasonSales.reduce(
    (total, sale) => total + sale.bagsSold,
    0,
  );
  const seasonSaleRate =
    seasonBagsSold > 0
      ? Math.round(
          seasonSales.reduce((total, sale) => total + sale.grossAmount, 0) /
            seasonBagsSold,
        )
      : 0;

  const [showAddEventModal, setShowAddEventModal] = useState(false);
  const [newEventTitle, setNewEventTitle] = useState("");
  const [newEventStage, setNewEventStage] = useState<any>("Crop Nutrition");
  const [newEventDesc, setNewEventDesc] = useState("");
  const [newEventCost, setNewEventCost] = useState("");

  const events = state.journeyEvents.filter(
    (ev) => ev.seasonId === activeSeason.id,
  );

  const handleAddEvent = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newEventTitle) return;

    AppStore.addJourneyEvent({
      seasonId: activeSeason.id,
      stage: newEventStage,
      title: newEventTitle,
      tamilTitle: newEventTitle,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      description: newEventDesc,
      costImpact: parseFloat(newEventCost) || 0,
      badge: "Farmer Logged",
    });

    if (parseFloat(newEventCost) > 0) {
      AppStore.addExpense({
        seasonId: activeSeason.id,
        category: "Bio-Inputs",
        description: newEventTitle,
        amount: parseFloat(newEventCost),
        date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
        paymentMode: "Cash",
        paidTo: "Field Log",
      });
    }

    setShowAddEventModal(false);
    setNewEventTitle("");
    setNewEventDesc("");
    setNewEventCost("");
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  const handleExportPassport = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-16 print:p-0">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 print:hidden">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <ScrollText className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>★ V2A Signature Agricultural Passport</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பயிர் பயண பாஸ்போர்ட் (Crop Journey)"
              : "Crop Season Journey Passport"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விதை தேர்வு முதல் வங்கி வரவு வரை இணைக்கப்பட்ட காலவரிசை டிஜிட்டல் ஆவணம்"
              : "Local demo timeline connecting sample seed, field, harvest, sale, and payment records"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowAddEventModal(true)}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-paddy-700 hover:bg-paddy-800 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{language === "ta" ? "நிகழ்வு சேர்க்க" : "Add Event"}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={handleExportPassport}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "பாஸ்போர்ட் அச்சிடுக"
                : "Print Demo Passport"}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Demo Passport Identity Card */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-forest-900 shadow-agro border-2 border-harvest-400/70 relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Subtle decorative grain background */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10 bg-paddy-contour pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-6 pb-6 border-b border-sage-300">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg uppercase tracking-wider">
                PASSPORT: {activeSeason.seasonCode}
              </span>
              <span className="bg-sage-100 text-forest-900 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-700" />

              </span>
            </div>

            <h2 className="font-display font-extrabold text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-forest-950 pt-1">
              {language === "ta"
                ? activeSeason.tamilCropName || activeSeason.cropName
                : activeSeason.cropName}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-paddy-800">
              Variety: {activeSeason.variety}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-700">
              Farmer:{" "}
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900">
                {state.farmer.fullName}
              </span>
              <br />
              Farm:{" "}
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900">
                {state.farm.farmName}

              ({activeSeason.areaAcres} Acres • {state.farmer.village},{" "}

              {seasonBagsSold} bags

                  </option>

                  <option value="Storage & Logistics">
                    Storage & Logistics (கிடங்கு & லாரி)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Market Sale">
                    Market Sale (விற்பனை ஒப்பந்தம்)
                  </option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Event Title
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newEventTitle}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewEventTitle(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. Zinc Sulphate foliar nutrition applied"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Description & Details
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={newEventDesc}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewEventDesc(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Details of application, dosage, workers..."
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Cost Impact (₹, optional)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={newEventCost}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewEventCost(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. 1500"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowAddEventModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                >
                  Save Milestone
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\CropPlannerPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Sprout,
  HelpCircle,
  TrendingUp,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  ArrowRight,
  Sparkles,
  Info,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  Send,
  GitCompare
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const CropPlannerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  // User input criteria
  const [district, setDistrict] = useState('Thanjavur');
  const [soilType, setSoilType] = useState('Clay Loam');
  const [landAcres, setLandAcres] = useState(3.5);
  const [waterAvailability, setWaterAvailability] = useState<'High' | 'Medium' | 'Low'>('High');
  const [targetSeason, setTargetSeason] = useState('Samba (Main-Rabi)');
  const [budgetPerAcre, setBudgetPerAcre] = useState(22000);

  // Expert question state
  const [expertQuestion, setExpertQuestion] = useState('');
  const [expertSubmitted, setExpertSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Active tab for Comparison vs Suitability
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'advisory' | 'compare' | 'prices'>('advisory');

  // Interactive suitability logic with explicit explanation
  const calculateSuitability = (crop: any) => {
    let score = 70;
    const reasons: string[] = [];

    if (crop.idealSoilTypes.includes(soilType)) {
      score += 15;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? `மண் வகை (${soilType}) இந்த பயிருக்கு மிகவும் பொருத்தமானது.` : `Matches ideal soil profile (${soilType}).`);
    } else {
      score -= 10;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? `மண் வகைக்கு கூடுதல் அங்கக உரம் தேவைப்படலாம்.` : `Suboptimal soil type; requires organic amendment.`);
    }

    if (waterAvailability === 'High' && crop.waterRequirement === 'High') {
      score += 10;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? 'கால்வாய் பாசன வசதி நெல் வளர்ச்சிக்கு உகந்தது.' : 'Ample canal water availability aligns with high water demand.');
    } else if (waterAvailability === 'Low' && crop.waterRequirement === 'Low') {
      score += 15;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? 'குறைந்த நீரில் அதிக லாபம் தரும் பயிர்.' : 'Drought-hardy crop fits water scarcity.');
    } else if (waterAvailability === 'Low' && crop.waterRequirement === 'High') {
      score -= 25;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? 'நீர் பற்றாக்குறை ஆபத்து அதிகம்.' : 'Severe water deficit risk.');
    }

    if (crop.recommendedSeasons.includes(targetSeason)) {
      score += 10;
      reasons.push(language === 'ta' ? `பருவகால வெப்பநிலை (${targetSeason}) உகந்தது.` : `Recommended season (${targetSeason}) maximizes grain filling.`);
    }

    return {
      score: Math.min(Math.max(score, 45), 98),
      reasons,
    };
  };

  const handleAskExpert = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!expertQuestion) return;
    setExpertSubmitted(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setExpertQuestion('');
      setExpertSubmitted(false);
      alert(language === 'ta' ? 'உங்கள் கேள்வி KVK வேளாண் விஞ்ஞானிக்கு அனுப்பப்பட்டது.' : 'Your query has been submitted to ICAR-KVK Agronomist.');
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Sprout className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'அறிவார்ந்த பயிர் திட்டமிடல்' : 'Agronomic Crop Suitability Engine'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'பயிர் தேர்வு & சாத்தியக்கூறுகள்' : 'Crop Advisory & Feasibility Matrix'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'மண்வளம், பாசன வசதி, மற்றும் சந்தை மதிப்பீட்டின் அடிப்படையில் வெளிப்படையான பரிந்துரை'
              : 'Deterministic recommendations based on soil test, water availability, and agro-climatic zones'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('advisory')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'advisory' ? 'bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300' : 'bg-white text-ink-700 border border-sage-300'
            }`}
          >
            {language === 'ta' ? 'பரிந்துரைகள்' : 'Suitability'}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('compare')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'compare' ? 'bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300' : 'bg-white text-ink-700 border border-sage-300'
            }`}
          >
            {language === 'ta' ? 'பயிர் ஒப்பீடு' : 'Crop Comparison'}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('prices')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'prices' ? 'bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300' : 'bg-white text-ink-700 border border-sage-300'
            }`}
          >
            {language === 'ta' ? 'விலை வரலாறு' : 'Mandi Trends'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Input Parameter Form Card */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-sage-200">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'உங்கள் பண்ணை விபரங்களை மாற்றியமைக்க:' : 'Configure Farm Parameters:'}</span>
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'டெல்டா பகுதி மாதிரி தர்க்கம்' : 'Delta Demo Logic Active'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">District</label>
            <select
              value={district}
              onChange={(e) => setDistrict(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <option value="Thanjavur">Thanjavur</option>
              <option value="Tiruvarur">Tiruvarur</option>
              <option value="Nagapattinam">Nagapattinam</option>
              <option value="Madurai">Madurai</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">Soil Type</label>
            <select
              value={soilType}
              onChange={(e) => setSoilType(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <option value="Clay Loam">Clay Loam</option>
              <option value="Alluvial">Alluvial</option>
              <option value="Red Sandy Loam">Red Sandy Loam</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">Land (Acres)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={landAcres}
              onChange={(e) => setLandAcres(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 1)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">Water Supply</label>
            <select
              value={waterAvailability}
              onChange={(e) => setWaterAvailability(e.target.value as any)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <option value="High">High (Canal + Bore)</option>
              <option value="Medium">Medium (Borewell)</option>
              <option value="Low">Low (Rainfed)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">Season</label>
            <select
              value={targetSeason}
              onChange={(e) => setTargetSeason(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <option value="Samba (Main-Rabi)">Samba (Main-Rabi)</option>
              <option value="Kuruvai (Summer-Kharif)">Kuruvai (Kharif)</option>
              <option value="Navarai (Summer)">Navarai (Summer)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 mb-1">Budget / Acre (₹)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="1000"
              value={budgetPerAcre}
              onChange={(e) => setBudgetPerAcre(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 10000)}
              className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tab 1: Advisory Recommendations */}
      {activeTab === 'advisory' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {state.crops.map((crop) => {
              const suitability = calculateSuitability(crop);
              const estCost = crop.avgCostPerAcre * landAcres;
              const estYieldBags = crop.avgYieldBagsPerAcre * landAcres;
              const estGross = estYieldBags * (crop.marketRateRange.max * 0.95);
              const estNet = estGross - estCost;

              return (
                <div
                  key={crop.id}
                  className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro hover:border-paddy-500 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
                >
                  <div>
                    {/* Header with Suitability Badge */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                          {crop.category} • {crop.durationDays} Days
                        </span>
                        <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                          {language === 'ta' ? crop.tamilName : crop.name}
                        </h3>
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-right">
                        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl font-black text-sm">
                          <span>{suitability.score}%</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                          {language === 'ta' ? 'பொருத்தம்' : 'Suitability Match'}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 mb-4">
                      {language === 'ta' ? crop.tamilDescription : crop.description}
                    </p>

                    {/* Why this crop appears (Requirement A) */}
                    <div className="p-3 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 space-y-1.5 mb-4">
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-paddy-600" />
                        <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'ஏன் இந்த பயிர் பரிந்துரைக்கப்படுகிறது?' : 'Why this crop was suggested:'}</span>
                      </p>
                      <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs text-ink-700">
                        {suitability.reasons.map((r, i) => (
                          <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-1.5">
                            <span className="text-paddy-600 font-bold">•</span>
                            <span>{r}</span>
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </div>

                    {/* Financial Projection on this Parcel */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center p-3 bg-sage-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 mb-4">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">{language === 'ta' ? 'மதிப்பீட்டு செலவு' : 'Est. Expenses'}</p>
                        <p className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">₹{Math.round(estCost).toLocaleString()}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">{language === 'ta' ? 'எதிர்பார்க்கும் மகசூல்' : 'Est. Yield'}</p>
                        <p className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">{Math.round(estYieldBags)} Bags</p>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">{language === 'ta' ? 'நிகர வரவு' : 'Est. Margin'}</p>
                        <p className="font-bold text-sm text-paddy-700">₹{Math.round(estNet).toLocaleString()}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigate('/budget-planner')}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-forest-900 transition-colors text-center"
                    >
                      {language === 'ta' ? 'வரவு-செலவு கணக்கிடு' : 'Budget Calculator'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigate('/seed-passport')}
                      className="py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-sage-300 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-sage-100 transition-colors"
                    >
                      {language === 'ta' ? 'விதை பாஸ்போர்ட்' : 'Seed QR'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Tab 2: Crop Comparison Matrix */}
      {activeTab === 'compare' && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'பயிர் ஒப்பீட்டு அட்டவணை (Comparison)' : 'Crop Side-by-Side Comparison'}
          </h3>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-sage-300 bg-cream-100">
                  <th className="p-3 font-bold text-forest-900">Parameters</th>
                  {state.crops.map((c) => (
                    <th key={c.id} className="p-3 font-bold text-forest-900">
                      {c.name}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-sage-200">
                <tr>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold text-ink-600">Duration (Days)</td>
                  {state.crops.map(c => <td key={c.id} className="p-3 font-semibold">{c.durationDays} days</td>)}
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold text-ink-600">Water Requirement</td>
                  {state.crops.map(c => <td key={c.id} className="p-3 font-semibold">{c.waterRequirement}</td>)}
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold text-ink-600">Avg Cost / Acre</td>
                  {state.crops.map(c => <td key={c.id} className="p-3 font-semibold">₹{c.avgCostPerAcre.toLocaleString()}</td>)}
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold text-ink-600">Average Yield / Acre</td>
                  {state.crops.map(c => <td key={c.id} className="p-3 font-semibold">{c.avgYieldBagsPerAcre} bags</td>)}
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold text-ink-600">Govt MSP / Benchmark</td>
                  {state.crops.map(c => <td key={c.id} className="p-3 font-semibold text-paddy-700">₹{c.mspPerQuintal}/Qtl</td>)}
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Tab 3: Historical Market Price Trends (Demo Data) */}
      {activeTab === 'prices' && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === 'ta' ? 'வரலாற்று சந்தை விலை நிலவரம் (மாதிரி தகவல்)' : 'Historical Agmarknet Mandi Price Trends (Demo Data)'}
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs text-ink-500">Thanjavur & Delta Regulated Markets</span>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'குறிப்பு: இந்த வரைபடம் கடந்த 6 மாத டெல்டா மண்டல மாதிரி விலைகளை காட்டுகிறது. நேரடி அரசு இணையதள தகவலாக கருத வேண்டாம்.'
              : 'Clearly Identified Demo Data: Shows representative modal procurement prices per 75kg bag over the last 6 months.'}
          </p>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2 pt-4 items-end h-48 bg-cream-50 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
            {[
              { month: 'Aug', price: 2180, height: '65%' },
              { month: 'Sep', price: 2210, height: '70%' },
              { month: 'Oct', price: 2190, height: '68%' },
              { month: 'Nov', price: 2250, height: '78%' },
              { month: 'Dec', price: 2270, height: '82%' },
              { month: 'Jan', price: 2320, height: '90%' },
            ].map((p, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="flex flex-col items-center h-full justify-end group">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-forest-900 mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                  ₹{p.price}
                </span>
                <div
                  className="w-full bg-forest-800 hover:bg-paddy-600 rounded-t-xl transition-all"
                  style={{ height: p.height }}
                />
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 mt-2">{p.month}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Expert Advice Request Form (Requirement A) */}
      <div className="bg-cream-100 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
          <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
          <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'வேளாண் விஞ்ஞானியிடம் ஆலோசனை கேட்க' : 'Request Agronomist / KVK Consultation'}</span>
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
          {language === 'ta'
            ? 'மண்வளம், விதை ரகங்கள் மற்றும் பயிர் சுழற்சி குறித்து TNAU / KVK விஞ்ஞானிகளிடம் நேரடியாக கேள்வி கேட்கலாம்.'
            : 'Submit questions regarding variety selection, flood tolerance, or rotation directly to ICAR-KVK agricultural scientists.'}
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleAskExpert} className="space-y-3">
          <textarea
            value={expertQuestion}
            onChange={(e) => setExpertQuestion(e.target.value)}
            rows={3}
            required
            placeholder={
              language === 'ta'
                ? 'உங்கள் கேள்வியை இங்கே எழுதுங்கள்... (உதாரணம்: சம்பா பருவத்தில் CR 1009 Sub 1 விதைக்கு எவ்வளவு தழைச்சத்து தேவை?)'
                : 'Type your agronomist query here... (e.g., Optimal nitrogen split doses for flood-tolerant CR 1009 Sub 1 in clay loam?)'
            }
            className="w-full p-3 bg-white border border-sage-300 rounded-2xl text-xs font-semibold text-ink-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-forest-600"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all"
          >
            <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'கேள்வியை சமர்ப்பிக்கவும்' : 'Submit Consultation Request'}</span>
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\DashboardPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Wheat,
  Activity,
  CloudSun,
  ShieldCheck,
  TrendingUp,
  CircleDollarSign,
  ArrowRight,
  PlusCircle,
  Clock,
  CheckCircle2,
  ScrollText,
  Layers,
  RotateCcw,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { translations } from "../translations";

export const DashboardPage: React.FC = () => {
  const {
    navigate,
    language,
    activeSeasonId,
    setActiveSeasonId,
    triggerRefresh,
  } = useNavigation();
  const t = translations[language];

  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const isDemoAccount = state.currentUser.id === "USER-VIJAY-DEMO";
  const activeSeason =
    state.seasons.find((s) => s.id === activeSeasonId) || state.seasons[0];
  const financials = AppStore.calculateSeasonFinancials(activeSeason.id);

  // Quick Expense modal state
  const [showExpenseModal, setShowExpenseModal] = useState(false);
  const [newExpDesc, setNewExpDesc] = useState("");
  const [newExpAmount, setNewExpAmount] = useState("");
  const [newExpCategory, setNewExpCategory] = useState<any>("Bio-Inputs");

  const handleAddQuickExpense = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newExpDesc || !newExpAmount) return;

    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeason.id,
      category: newExpCategory,
      description: newExpDesc,
      amount: parseFloat(newExpAmount),
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      paymentMode: "Cash",
      paidTo: "Local Agri Vendor",
    });

    setNewExpDesc("");
    setNewExpAmount("");
    setShowExpenseModal(false);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  const handleResetDemo = () => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "Reset this browser's demo data? Local edits and trade records will be cleared.",
    );
    if (!confirmed) return;
    const resetState = AppStore.resetToDemo();
    setActiveSeasonId(resetState.activeSeasonId);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Top Welcome & Active Season Banner */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 via-forest-800 to-paddy-900 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-cream-100 shadow-agro border border-forest-700">
        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                {activeSeason.seasonCode}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-sage-200">
                {state.farmer.village}, {state.farmer.district}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-cream-50">
              {language === "ta"
                ? state.farmer.tamilFullName
                : state.farmer.fullName}{" "}
              —{" "}
              <span className="text-harvest-400">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? activeSeason.tamilCropName || activeSeason.cropName
                  : activeSeason.cropName}
              </span>
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-sage-200">
              {activeSeason.parcelName} • {activeSeason.areaAcres}{" "}
              {language === "ta" ? "ஏக்கர்" : "Acres"} • {activeSeason.variety}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowExpenseModal(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-terracotta-500 hover:bg-terracotta-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all"
            >
              <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta" ? "செலவு சேர்க்க" : "Add Field Expense"}
              </span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/crop-journey")}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-harvest-400 hover:bg-harvest-500 text-forest-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-gold transition-all"
            >
              <ScrollText className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-900" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "பயிர் பயணம் (Passport)"
                  : "Crop Journey Passport"}
              </span>
            </button>

            {isDemoAccount && (
              <button
                onClick={handleResetDemo}
                title="Clear local demo edits and restart the harvest-to-sale walkthrough"
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-cream-100/30 bg-forest-950/30 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-cream-100 transition-colors hover:bg-forest-950/60"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "டெமோவை மீட்டமை"
                    : "Reset demo journey"}
                </span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Season Stage Progress Bar */}
        <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-forest-700/80">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-sage-200 mb-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-harvest-300">
              {language === "ta" ? "சாகுபடி நிலை:" : "Current Lifecycle Stage:"}{" "}
              {activeSeason.stage}
            </span>
            <span>
              {language === "ta" ? "அறுவடை இலக்கு: " : "Target: "}{" "}
              {activeSeason.targetYieldBags}{" "}
              {language === "ta" ? "மூட்டைகள்" : "Bags"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full h-3 bg-forest-950/60 rounded-full overflow-hidden p-0.5 border border-forest-600">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-paddy-500 via-harvest-400 to-harvest-300 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{
                width: activeSeason.stage === "Sold & Settled" ? "100%" : "65%",
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4 Core Financial & Field Metric Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* Card 1: Active Land */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-500 mb-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
              {t.metrics.activeAcres}
            </span>
            <div className="p-2 bg-paddy-50 text-paddy-700 rounded-xl">
              <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900">
              {activeSeason.areaAcres}{" "}
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink-500">
                {language === "ta" ? "ஏக்கர்" : "Acres"}
              </span>
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-medium mt-1">
              ✓ {activeSeason.seasonName}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 2: Total Cultivation Expenses */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-500 mb-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
              {t.metrics.investedCost}
            </span>
            <div className="p-2 bg-terracotta-50 text-terracotta-700 rounded-xl">
              <CircleDollarSign className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900">
              ₹{financials.totalExpenses.toLocaleString()}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 font-medium mt-1">
              {financials.expenseCount}{" "}
              {language === "ta" ? "செலவு பதிவுகள்" : "Recorded entries"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 3: Realized Net Cashflow */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-500 mb-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
              {t.metrics.netProfit}
            </span>
            <div className="p-2 bg-forest-50 text-forest-800 rounded-xl">
              <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-paddy-700">
              ₹{financials.realizedCashflow.toLocaleString()}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-paddy-800 font-semibold mt-1">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "வரவு பெற்ற தொகை - செலவுகள்"
                : "Received payments - expenses"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 4: Pending Payments (SEPARATE!) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border-2 border-harvest-400 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between bg-harvest-50/40">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-500 mb-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-900">
              {t.metrics.pendingDues}
            </span>
            <div className="p-2 bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 rounded-xl">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-terracotta-600">
              ₹{financials.pendingPayments.toLocaleString()}
            </p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-600 font-bold mt-1">
              ⚠{" "}
              {language === "ta"
                ? "வங்கியில் வரவு வைக்கப்படாத தொகை"
                : "Not counted as received income"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid: Weather Alert + Field Tasks + Quick Actions */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left Column: Urgent Alerts & Tasks (2 Cols) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          {/* Hyperlocal Weather Alert Card */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-cream-200 text-harvest-600 flex items-center justify-center border border-harvest-400/40 shrink-0">
                <CloudSun className="w-8 h-8" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                    {state.farmer.village} / {state.farmer.district}
                  </span>
                  <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {language === "ta" ? "அறுவடைக்கு உகந்தது" : "Dry & Sunny"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                  29°C{" "}
                  <span className="text-sm font-normal text-ink-600">
                    • Humidity 68% • Wind 12 km/h
                  </span>
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 mt-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "அடுத்த 4 நாட்களுக்கு மிதமான வெயில். நெல் அறுவடை மற்றும் உலர்த்துவதற்கு உகந்த காலம்."
                    : "Clear skies forecast for next 4 days. Optimal window for mechanical harvesting and sun drying."}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/weather")}
              className="shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-forest-700 transition-colors"
            >
              {language === "ta" ? "வானிலை விபரம்" : "7-Day Forecast"}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Connected Operational Tasks */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "நடப்பு பருவ களப் பணிகள்"
                    : "Active Season Field Checklist"}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "விதைப்பு முதல் அறுவடை வரை திட்டமிடப்பட்ட பணிகள்"
                    : "Chronological agronomic operations for active parcel"}
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/farm-calendar")}
                className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:text-paddy-900 flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <span>
                  {language === "ta" ? "முழு நாட்காட்டி" : "Full Calendar"}
                </span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              {state.tasks.slice(0, 4).map((task) => (
                <div
                  key={task.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 bg-cream-50/50 hover:bg-cream-100/60 transition-colors"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <div className="mt-0.5">
                      {task.status === "Completed" ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                      ) : (
                        <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-harvest-600" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                        {language === "ta"
                          ? task.tamilTitle || task.title
                          : task.title}
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500">
                        {task.cropStage} • {task.scheduledDate}{" "}
                        {task.actualCost ? `• Cost: ₹${task.actualCost}` : ""}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <span
                    className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                      task.status === "Completed"
                        ? "bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800"
                        : "bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {task.status}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Quick Action Grid & Soil Health Summary */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {/* Quick Action Matrix */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta" ? "விரைவு செயல்பாடுகள்" : "Quick Actions"}
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/seed-passport")}
                className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 border border-sage-300 text-center transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 text-harvest-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-2 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta" ? "விதை QR ஆய்வு" : "Verify Seed QR"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  TSSCA Certified
                </span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/crop-doctor")}
                className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 border border-sage-300 text-center transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-paddy-700 text-cream-50 flex items-center justify-center mb-2 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                  <Activity className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta" ? "AI பயிர் மருத்துவர்" : "AI Crop Doctor"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  Leaf Diagnosis
                </span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/offers")}
                className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 border border-sage-300 text-center transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 flex items-center justify-center mb-2 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                  <Wheat className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta" ? "கொள்முதல் ஏலம்" : "Buyer Offers"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-bold mt-0.5">
                  {language === "ta" ? "சலுகைகளைப் பார்க்க" : "Review bids"}
                </span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/net-proceeds")}
                className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 border border-sage-300 text-center transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-forest-900 text-cream-50 flex items-center justify-center mb-2 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                  <CircleDollarSign className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta" ? "நிகர லாபக் கணக்கு" : "Net Proceeds"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  Post deductions
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Soil Health Card Mini Widget */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
                <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "மண் பரிசோதனை சுருக்கம்"
                    : "Soil Health Status"}
                </h4>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/soil-health")}
                className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:underline"
              >
                {language === "ta" ? "முழு அறிக்கை" : "View Card"}
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center pt-1">
              <div className="p-2 bg-cream-100 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500">pH Level</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-paddy-700">7.1</p>
                <p className="text-[9px] text-forest-800">Neutral (சரியானது)</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-2 bg-cream-100 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500">Org Carbon</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-forest-900">0.68%</p>
                <p className="text-[9px] text-forest-800">Medium</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-2 bg-cream-100 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500">Zinc (Zn)</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-terracotta-600">
                  Deficient
                </p>
                <p className="text-[9px] text-terracotta-700">Treated ✓</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Quick Add Expense Modal */}
      {showExpenseModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "புதிய விவசாய செலவு பதிவு"
                : "Record New Field Expense"}
            </h3>
            <form onSubmit={handleAddQuickExpense} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "செலவு வகை (Category)" : "Category"}
                </label>
                <select
                  value={newExpCategory}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewExpCategory(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="Seeds">Seeds (விதை)</option>
                  <option value="Fertilizers">Fertilizers (உரம்)</option>
                  <option value="Bio-Inputs">Bio-Inputs (உயிரி உரங்கள்)</option>
                  <option value="Labour">Labour (கூலி)</option>
                  <option value="Machinery">Machinery (இயந்திர வாடகை)</option>
                  <option value="Irrigation">Irrigation (பாசனம்)</option>
                  <option value="Transport">Transport (போக்குவரத்து)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "விளக்கம் (Description)" : "Description"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newExpDesc}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewExpDesc(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. Zinc Sulphate 10kg + Neem Cake"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "தொகை (Amount in ₹)" : "Amount (₹)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={newExpAmount}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewExpAmount(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. 1500"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowExpenseModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600 hover:bg-sage-100"
                >
                  {t.actions.cancel}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-forest-900 shadow-sm"
                >
                  {t.actions.save}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
