import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  HelpCircle,
  Send,
  CheckCircle2,
  Phone,
  Star,
  MessageSquare,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const ExpertAdvicePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [question, setQuestion] = useState("");
  const [topic, setTopic] = useState("Pest & Disease Control");
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!question) return;

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      AppStore.addExpertRequest({
        farmerId: state.farmer.id,
        seasonId: state.activeSeasonId,
        expertName: "Dr. M. Soundararajan, Ph.D. (Agronomy)",
        institution:
          "Tamil Nadu Agricultural University (TNAU) Soil & Water Centre",
        topic,
        question,
        status: "Pending Review",
        date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      });

      setIsSubmitting(false);
      setQuestion("");
      triggerRefresh();
      alert(
        language === "ta"
          ? "உங்கள் கேள்வி TNAU வேளாண் விஞ்ஞானிகளுக்கு சமர்ப்பிக்கப்பட்டது."
          : "Your inquiry has been submitted to TNAU Agronomy Scientists.",
      );
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "விஞ்ஞானிகள் நேரடி ஆலோசனை"
                : "Agronomist & Scientist Advisory Desk"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "KVK & TNAU வேளாண் விஞ்ஞானியிடம் கேளுங்கள்"
              : "Ask ICAR-KVK & TNAU Agronomists"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "தமிழ்நாடு வேளாண்மைப் பல்கலைக்கழகம் மற்றும் வேளாண் அறிவியல் நிலைய நிபுணர்கள்"
              : "Direct two-way advisory channel linking delta farmers with certified plant pathologists"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/crop-doctor")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === "ta" ? "AI பயிர் மருத்துவர்" : "AI Crop Doctor"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Query Submission Box */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
          <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta"
              ? "புதிய கேள்வியை சமர்ப்பிக்கவும்"
              : "Post an Agricultural Inquiry"}
          </span>
        </h3>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Inquiry Topic
              </label>
              <select
                value={topic}
                onChange={(e) => setTopic(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="Pest & Disease Control">
                  Pest & Disease Control (பூச்சி & நோய்)
                </option>
                <option value="Nutrient Deficiency">
                  Soil Nutrient Deficiency (ஊட்டச்சத்து குறைபாடு)
                </option>
                <option value="Water & Salinity Management">
                  Water & Salinity Management (நீர் & உவர்த்தன்மை)
                </option>
                <option value="Next Season Crop Rotation">
                  Next Season Crop Rotation (பயிர் சுழற்சி)
                </option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Target Crop / Plot
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                disabled
                value="Paddy (CR 1009 Sub 1) • Keezha Theru Vayal (3.5 Acres)"
                className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-100 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
              Your Detailed Question
            </label>
            <textarea
              value={question}
              onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
              required
              rows={3}
              placeholder={
                language === "ta"
                  ? "உங்கள் கேள்வியை தமிழில் அல்லது ஆங்கிலத்தில் எழுதலாம்..."
                  : "Type your question clearly. Photos from Crop Doctor will automatically be attached."
              }
              className="w-full p-3 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-ink-900"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all"
            >
              <Send className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
              <span>
                {isSubmitting
                  ? "Submitting..."
                  : language === "ta"
                    ? "கேள்வி அனுப்ப"
                    : "Submit to Agronomist"}
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* Answered Advisory History */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "விஞ்ஞானிகள் அளித்த பதில்கள்"
            : "Answered Agronomic Consultations"}
        </h3>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {state.expertRequests.map((req) => (
            <div
              key={req.id}
              className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50/60 space-y-3"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {req.topic}
                  </span>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900 mt-1">
                    {req.question}
                  </h4>
                </div>
                <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                  {req.status}
                </span>
              </div>

              {req.answer && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="font-bold text-forest-900">
                      👨‍🔬 {req.expertName}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-ink-500">{req.institution}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-forest-900 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                    "{req.answer}"
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Calendar,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Plus,
  Tag,
  ArrowRight,
  Layers,
  DollarSign,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const FarmCalendarPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [filterStage, setFilterStage] = useState("All");
  const [showTaskModal, setShowTaskModal] = useState(false);
  const [taskTitle, setTaskTitle] = useState("");
  const [taskCategory, setTaskCategory] = useState<any>("Fertilization");
  const [taskDate, setTaskDate] = useState(
    new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
  );
  const [taskCost, setTaskCost] = useState("1200");

  const tasks = state.tasks.filter(
    (t) => filterStage === "All" || t.cropStage === filterStage,
  );

  const handleCreateTask = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!taskTitle) return;

    AppStore.addTask({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      title: taskTitle,
      tamilTitle: taskTitle,
      category: taskCategory,
      cropStage: "Vegetative Growth",
      scheduledDate: taskDate,
      status: "Pending",
      priority: "Normal",
      estimatedCost: parseFloat(taskCost) || 0,
    });

    setShowTaskModal(false);
    setTaskTitle("");
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "பருவகால களச் செயல்பாடுகள்"
                : "Agronomic Timeline & Field Operations"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விவசாய கள நாட்காட்டி"
              : "Farm Activity Calendar & Sowing Schedule"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "உழவு, விதைப்பு, களை எடுத்தல், உரம் இடுதல் மற்றும் அறுவடை பணிகள்"
              : "End-to-end task scheduling synchronized with Tamil Nadu agricultural package of practices"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => setShowTaskModal(true)}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta" ? "புதிய பணி சேர்க்க" : "Create Farm Task"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Sowing Schedule Overview Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 to-paddy-900 rounded-3xl p-6 text-cream-100 shadow-agro grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-sage-300">
            Sowing Date
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-xl text-cream-50 mt-0.5">
            Aug 28, 2025
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-harvest-300">Raised Bed Mat Nursery</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-sage-300">
            Transplanting Date
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-xl text-cream-50 mt-0.5">
            Sep 15, 2025
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-harvest-300">8-Row Machine Planting</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-sage-300">
            Harvest Window
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-xl text-cream-50 mt-0.5">
            Jan 10 - 15, 2026
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-harvest-300">110 Bags Harvested ✓</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-sage-300">
            Next Fallow Pulse
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-xl text-cream-50 mt-0.5">
            Jan 25, 2026
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-harvest-300">Blackgram Relay Sowing</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Task List */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "சாகுபடி பணிகள் பட்டியல்"
            : "Field Operations Task Log"}
        </h3>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {tasks.map((task) => (
            <div
              key={task.id}
              className="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 bg-cream-50/60 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="mt-1">
                  {task.status === "Completed" ? (
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                  ) : (
                    <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-harvest-600" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? task.tamilTitle || task.title
                      : task.title}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                    Stage:{" "}
                    <span className="font-semibold text-forest-800">
                      {task.cropStage}
                    </span>{" "}
                    • Scheduled: {task.scheduledDate}
                  </p>
                  {task.notes && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-ink-700 mt-1 italic">
                      "{task.notes}"
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between md:justify-end gap-6 text-right">
                {task.actualCost && (
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">
                      Actual Cost
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-display font-black text-sm text-forest-900">
                      ₹{task.actualCost.toLocaleString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <span
                  className={`text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${
                    task.status === "Completed"
                      ? "bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800"
                      : "bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900"
                  }`}
                >
                  {task.status}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Create Task Modal */}
      {showTaskModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "புதிய பண்ணை பணி உருவாக்கு"
                : "Create Farm Task"}
            </h3>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateTask} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Task Title
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={taskTitle}
                  onChange={(e) => setTaskTitle(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. Zinc Sulphate foliar spray"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    Category
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={taskCategory}
                    onChange={(e) => setTaskCategory(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                  >
                    <option value="Fertilization">Fertilization</option>
                    <option value="Weeding">Weeding</option>
                    <option value="Irrigation">Irrigation</option>
                    <option value="Pest Control">Pest Control</option>
                    <option value="Harvesting">Harvesting</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    Date
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    value={taskDate}
                    onChange={(e) => setTaskDate(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Estimated Cost (₹)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={taskCost}
                  onChange={(e) => setTaskCost(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowTaskModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                >
                  Add Task
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\FinancePage.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  PieChart,
  TrendingUp,
  CircleDollarSign,
  Plus,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  Clock,
  CheckCircle2,
  DollarSign,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const FinancePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const financials = AppStore.calculateSeasonFinancials(activeSeasonId);

  const [showExpenseModal, setShowExpenseModal] = useState(false);
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [cat, setCat] = useState<any>("Labour");

  const handleAddExpense = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!desc || !amount) return;

    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      category: cat,
      description: desc,
      amount: parseFloat(amount),
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      paymentMode: "Cash",
      paidTo: "Field Vendor",
    });

    setDesc("");
    setAmount("");
    setShowExpenseModal(false);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <PieChart className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "பண்ணை நிதி நிலை & கணக்கியல்"
                : "Comprehensive Farm Financial Ledger & P&L"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பண்ணை வரவு-செலவு & லாப-நஷ்ட அறிக்கை"
              : "Farm Balance Sheet & Cashflow Audit"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விதை முதல் விற்பனை வரை வெளிப்படையான செலவு மற்றும் நிகர லாப பகுப்பாய்வு"
              : "Category-wise expenditures, realized bank cashflow, and segregated buyer receivables"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowExpenseModal(true)}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-terracotta-500 hover:bg-terracotta-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta" ? "செலவு சேர்க்க" : "Record Expense"}
            </span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/crop-journey")}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
          >
            {language === "ta" ? "பயிர் பயணம் (Passport)" : "Crop Passport"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main 4 Financial Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Gross Sales Invoiced
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            ₹{financials.grossSales.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-medium">
            110 Bags contracted
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Total Field Expenses
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-terracotta-600">
            ₹{financials.totalExpenses.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {financials.expenseCount} Itemized records
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Realized Net Cashflow
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-paddy-700">
            ₹{financials.realizedCashflow.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-forest-800 font-semibold">
            Net Proceeds - Expenses
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Pending Receivables (Separated!) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border-2 border-harvest-400 shadow-agro space-y-1 bg-harvest-50/40">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Pending Buyer Dues
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-terracotta-700">
            ₹{financials.pendingPayments.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-600 font-bold">
            ⚠ Separated from received income
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Category-wise Expense Breakdown */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "செலவு வகை வாரியான பகுப்பாய்வு"
            : "Category-Wise Expense Distribution"}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-7 gap-3">
          {Object.entries(financials.expenseByCategory).map(
            ([category, amt]) => {
              const pct = Math.round((amt / financials.totalExpenses) * 100);

              return (
                <div
                  key={category}
                  className="p-3.5 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 text-center space-y-1"
                >
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-ink-500 block truncate">
                    {category}
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-display font-black text-base text-forest-900">
                    ₹{amt.toLocaleString()}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-bold">
                    {pct}% of budget
                  </p>
                </div>
              );
            },
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Itemized Expense History */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "பதிவான அனைத்து செலவுகள்"
            : "Itemized Cultivation Expenses"}
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-sage-200 text-xs">
          {state.expenses.map((exp) => (
            <div
              key={exp.id}
              className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between"
            >
              <div>
                <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? exp.tamilDescription || exp.description
                    : exp.description}
                </span>
                <p className="text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  Category:{" "}
                  <span className="font-semibold text-paddy-700">
                    {exp.category}
                  </span>{" "}
                  • Date: {exp.date} • Paid via {exp.paymentMode} to{" "}
                  {exp.paidTo}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="text-right">
                <span className="font-display font-black text-base text-terracotta-600">
                  ₹{exp.amount.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Record Expense Modal */}
      {showExpenseModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "புதிய செலவு பதிவு"
                : "Log Cultivation Expense"}
            </h3>

            <form onSubmit={handleAddExpense} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Expense Category
                </label>
                <select
                  value={cat}
                  onChange={(e) => setCat(e.target.value as any)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="Labour">Labour (கூலி)</option>
                  <option value="Machinery">Machinery (இயந்திர வாடகை)</option>
                  <option value="Fertilizers">Fertilizers (உரம்)</option>
                  <option value="Bio-Inputs">Bio-Inputs (உயிரி உரங்கள்)</option>
                  <option value="Seeds">Seeds (விதை)</option>
                  <option value="Transport">Transport (போக்குவரத்து)</option>
                  <option value="Storage">Storage (கிடங்கு)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Description
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={desc}
                  onChange={(e) => setDesc(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. 5 bags Neem cake"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Amount (₹)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={amount}
                  onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. 3500"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowExpenseModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                >
                  Save to Ledger
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\HarvestPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Wheat, Store } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const HarvestPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const activeSeason = state.seasons.find(
    (season) => season.id === activeSeasonId,
  );
  const harvest = state.harvestRecords.find(
    (record) => record.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );

  const [broadcastDone, setBroadcastDone] = useState(false);
  const [actionNotice, setActionNotice] = useState("");

  const handleBroadcastToBuyers = () => {
    const result = AppStore.broadcastHarvest(activeSeasonId);
    if (!result) {
      setActionNotice(
        "No unsold harvest is available for this season. Start a fresh demo from the dashboard or choose a season with a harvest record.",
      );
      return;
    }
    setBroadcastDone(true);
    triggerRefresh();
    navigate("/offers");
  };

  if (!harvest || !activeSeason) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-sage-300 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-agro">
        <Wheat className="mx-auto mb-3 h-8 w-8 text-paddy-700" />
        <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-forest-900">
          No harvest record for this season
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-600">
          Select a season with a recorded harvest before creating a buyer
          listing.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Wheat className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "அறுவடை மேலாண்மை & மகசூல் பதிவு"
                : "Harvest Readiness & Yield Quality Certification"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "அறுவடை தயார்நிலை & தானிய தரம்"
              : "Harvest Readiness & Yield Tracker"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {activeSeason.cropName} • {activeSeason.parcelName} (
            {activeSeason.areaAcres} Acres)
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={handleBroadcastToBuyers}
          className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Store className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {broadcastDone
              ? "Listing created"
              : language === "ta"
                ? "ஆலைகளுக்கு விற்பனை அறிவிப்பு"
                : "Broadcast Lot to Buyers"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {actionNotice && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-xl border border-harvest-400 bg-harvest-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-forest-900"
        >
          {actionNotice}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Harvest Record Metric Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
            Actual Harvest
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {harvest.actualQuantityBags}{" "}
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink-500">Bags</span>
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-semibold">
            {harvest.totalYieldKg.toLocaleString()} kg (31.4 bags/acre)
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
            Grain Moisture Content
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-paddy-700">
            {harvest.moistureContentPct}%
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-paddy-800 font-bold">
            ✓ Within 14% max threshold (No discount!)
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
            Quality Classification
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900">
            {harvest.grainQualityGrade}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-forest-800 font-semibold">
            Premium long bold grain lustre
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
            Harvesting Cost
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900">
            ₹
            {(
              harvest.labourExpense + harvest.machineryExpense
            ).toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">Harvester + Bagging labour</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Produce Quality Guide (Requirement F) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "தானிய தர நிர்ணய வழிகாட்டி"
            : "Produce Quality Benchmark & Price Multiplier"}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs">
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-paddy-50 border-2 border-paddy-500 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-black text-sm text-paddy-900">
                Grade A Super (Current)
              </span>
              <span className="bg-paddy-600 text-white font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px]">
                100% Price
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-ink-700">
              Moisture &lt; 14%, clean grain lustre, foreign matter &lt; 0.5%,
              zero chalky or broken grains.
            </p>
            <p className="font-bold text-paddy-800">
              Target Buyer: Modern Rice Mills & Export Mills
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                Standard Common
              </span>
              <span className="bg-sage-200 text-forest-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px]">
                MSP Rate
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-ink-700">
              Moisture 14% - 17%, minor chaff or unhusked grain. Suitable for
              Government DPC procurement centres.
            </p>
            <p className="font-bold text-forest-900">
              Target Buyer: TNCSC Direct Purchase Centers
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-bold text-sm text-terracotta-700">
                Damaged / High Moisture
              </span>
              <span className="bg-terracotta-100 text-terracotta-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px]">
                -15% Penalty
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-ink-700">
              Moisture &gt; 18% caused by rain during harvest. Requires
              mandatory mechanical yard drying.
            </p>
            <p className="font-bold text-terracotta-700">
              Target Buyer: Cattle feed or parboiling plants
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Pre-Harvest & Post-Harvest Workflow Actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/storage")}
          className="px-5 py-3 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-forest-900 shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "கிடங்கு சேமிப்பு பதிவு"
            : "Book WDRA Warehouse Storage"}
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/logistics")}
          className="px-5 py-3 bg-white border border-sage-300 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-sage-100"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "பண்ணை போக்குவரத்து லாரி"
            : "Book Transport Vehicle"}
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/offers")}
          className="px-5 py-3 bg-harvest-400 text-forest-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl hover:bg-harvest-500 shadow-gold"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "கொள்முதல் ஏலங்கள் காண்க"
            : "View Incoming Buyer Offers"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                    {step.title}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 leading-relaxed">
                    {step.desc}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-4 mt-4 border-t border-sage-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700">
                  <span>{language === "ta" ? "தொடங்குக" : "Open feature"}</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Signature Highlight Banner: Crop Journey Passport */}
      <section className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cream-100 via-harvest-50 to-sage-100 rounded-3xl p-8 border-2 border-harvest-400/40 shadow-agro flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-8">
        <div className="space-y-4 max-w-2xl">
          <span className="inline-block bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">
            ★ {language === "ta" ? "முக்கிய அம்சம்" : "Signature V2A Feature"}
          </span>
          <h3 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பயிர் பயண பாஸ்போர்ட் (Crop Journey Passport)"
              : "The Crop Season Journey Ledger (Prototype)"}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-700 leading-relaxed">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "ஒவ்வொரு பருவங்களுக்கான விதை, உரம், உழவு செலவு, அறுவடை எடை, மில் விற்பனை ஒப்பந்தம் மற்றும் நிலுவைத் தொகையை ஒரே டிஜிட்டல் பாஸ்போர்ட்டில் இணைக்கிறது. நிலுவையில் உள்ள தொகை தனியாக காட்டப்படுகிறது."
              : "The prototype keeps sample crop-season events and expenses in a chronological local timeline. It separates amounts marked received from pending dues; records are stored in this browser and are not independently verified."}
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-2">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-forest-800 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-sage-300">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              {language === "ta"
                ? "நிகர லாபக் கணக்கீடு"
                : "Realized Net Margin"}
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-forest-800 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-sage-300">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              {language === "ta"
                ? "மாதிரி எடை பதிவு"
                : "Sample Weighment Summary"}
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-forest-800 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-sage-300">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              {language === "ta"
                ? "முன்மாதிரி ஆவண காட்சி"
                : "Prototype Document View"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="shrink-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/crop-journey")}
            className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold rounded-2xl shadow-agro transition-all text-sm"
          >
            {language === "ta" ? "பாஸ்போர்ட் பார்க்க" : "Open Crop Passport"}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/net-proceeds")}
            className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-white hover:bg-sage-50 text-forest-900 font-bold rounded-2xl border border-sage-400 transition-all text-sm"
          >
            {language === "ta"
              ? "நிகர வரவு கால்குலேட்டர்"
              : "Net Proceeds Calc"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\InputLockerPage.tsx

import React from "react";
import {
  Store,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  Package,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  ShoppingCart,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const InputLockerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const purchases = state.inputPurchases.filter(
    (purchase) => purchase.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );

  const totalOrdered = purchases.reduce(
    (sum, purchase) => sum + purchase.totalCost,
    0,
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Store className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "டெமோ ஆர்டர் வரலாறு"
                : "Demo Orders & Purchase History"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "இடுபொருள் ஆர்டர்கள்"
              : "Input Orders & Purchase History"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பயன்படுத்தப்பட்ட மற்றும் இருப்பில் உள்ள உரங்கள், விதைகள் மற்றும் இடுபொருட்களின் சான்றிதழ்"
              : "Review demo orders placed from the input shop. Orders are local records; no supplier or payment service is connected."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/input-marketplace")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <ShoppingCart className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta" ? "புதிய இடுபொருட்கள் வாங்க" : "Buy More Inputs"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Locker Summary Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "இந்த பருவத்தின் ஆர்டர்கள்"
              : "Orders This Season"}
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
            {purchases.length}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-semibold mt-1">
            Local demo purchase history
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {language === "ta" ? "ஆர்டர் மொத்தம்" : "Total Order Value"}
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
            ₹{totalOrdered.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1">
            Orders shown for selected season
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பணம் செலுத்தும் நிலை"
              : "Order & Payment Status"}
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-xl text-paddy-700 mt-1">
            Demo only · Unpaid
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1">
            Payment and delivery are not processed by this prototype.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Vault Items List */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta" ? "ஆர்டர் பட்டியல்" : "Order History"}
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-sage-200">
          {purchases.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-ink-600">
              No orders for this season yet.{" "}
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate("/input-marketplace")}
                className="font-bold text-paddy-700 underline"
              >
                Browse the demo shop
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            purchases.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="py-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-cream-100 border border-sage-300 flex items-center justify-center text-paddy-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    <Package className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                        {item.productName}
                      </h4>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-cream-200 text-ink-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {item.batchNumber}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                      Purchased on {item.purchaseDate} from {item.supplier}
                    </p>
                    {item.notes && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-forest-800 font-medium mt-1">
                        📝 {item.notes}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between md:justify-end gap-6 text-right">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 block uppercase">
                      Quantity & Rate
                    </span>
                    <p className="font-bold text-xs text-forest-900">
                      {item.quantity} {item.unit} @ ₹{item.unitPrice}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 block uppercase">
                      Total Invested
                    </span>
                    <p className="font-display font-black text-base text-forest-900">
                      ₹{item.totalCost.toLocaleString()}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <span className="bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-paddy-600" />
                    {item.orderStatus ||
                      (item.isUsedInField
                        ? "Demo record · Used"
                        : "Demo record · Placed")}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\InputMarketplacePage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ShoppingBag,
  Search,
  Filter,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plus,
  CheckCircle2,
  ArrowRight,
  Star,
  Store,
  Layers,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { InputProduct } from "../types";

export const InputMarketplacePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>("All");
  const [quantities, setQuantities] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [addedItemName, setAddedItemName] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const categories = [
    "All",
    "Organic Bio-Fertilizer",
    "NPK & Micronutrients",
    "Bio-Pesticide",
    "Seeds",
  ];

  const filteredProducts = state.inputProducts.filter((p) => {
    const matchesCategory =
      selectedCategory === "All" || p.category === selectedCategory;
    const matchesSearch =
      p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.brand.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.tamilName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
  });

  const getQty = (id: string) => quantities[id] || 1;

  const handleQtyChange = (id: string, delta: number) => {
    const product = state.inputProducts.find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!product || product.stockAvailable <= 0) return;
    const current = getQty(id);
    const next = Math.min(product.stockAvailable, Math.max(1, current + delta));
    setQuantities((currentQuantities) => ({
      ...currentQuantities,
      [id]: next,
    }));
  };

  const handleBuyProduct = (product: InputProduct) => {
    const qty = getQty(product.id);
    if (product.stockAvailable < qty || product.stockAvailable <= 0) return;
    const totalCost = product.pricePerUnit * qty;
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Place demo order for ${qty} ${product.unit} of ${product.name} for ₹${totalCost.toLocaleString()}? No payment will be taken.`,
    );
    if (!confirmed) return;

    // Add to input purchases
    AppStore.addInputPurchase({
      orderStatus: "Placed",
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      productId: product.id,
      productName: product.name,
      quantity: qty,
      unit: product.unit,
      unitPrice: product.pricePerUnit,
      totalCost,
      purchaseDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      batchNumber: `BATCH-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      supplier: product.supplierName,
      isUsedInField: false,
      notes: `Demo order placed. Payment is not processed.`,
    });

    // Also record expense automatically into season!
    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      category: product.category.includes("Fertilizer")
        ? "Fertilizers"
        : "Bio-Inputs",
      description: `${product.name} (${qty} ${product.unit})`,
      amount: totalCost,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      paymentMode: "Credit / Due",
      paidTo: product.supplierName,
    });

    setAddedItemName(product.name);
    triggerRefresh();
    setTimeout(() => {
      setAddedItemName(null);
    }, 2500);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "உயிரி & சான்றளிக்கப்பட்ட இடுபொருட்கள்"
                : "Demo Agri-Inputs Shop"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "வேளாண் இடுபொருட்கள் அங்காடி"
              : "Agri Inputs Store & Pricing"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "மாதிரி பட்டியல் மட்டும். வாங்குதல் அல்லது பணம் செலுத்துதல் உண்மையில் நடைபெறாது."
              : "Sample catalog only. Orders are saved locally; no supplier, delivery, or payment is connected."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/input-locker")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Store className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta"
              ? "டிஜிட்டல் இடுபொருள் பெட்டகம்"
              : "Open Input Locker"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Added to locker alert */}
      {addedItemName && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-900 border border-paddy-300 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-700" />
            <span>
              "{addedItemName}"{" "}
              {language === "ta"
                ? "வெற்றிகரமாக இடுபொருள் பெட்டகத்தில் சேர்க்கப்பட்டு செலவு பதிவு செய்யப்பட்டது!"
                : "Order placed (demo). No payment was charged. Track it in Purchase History."}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/input-locker")}
            className="underline font-bold text-forest-900"
          >
            {language === "ta" ? "பெட்டகம் காண்க" : "View Locker"}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search and Category Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={
                language === "ta"
                  ? "உரம், உயிர் பூச்சிக்கொல்லி அல்லது பிராண்ட் தேடவும்..."
                  : "Search bio-fertilizers, neem cake, zinc, or brand..."
              }
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-forest-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {categories.map((cat) => (
              <button
                key={cat}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat)}
                className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                  selectedCategory === cat
                    ? "bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-cream-50 text-ink-700 hover:bg-sage-100 border border-sage-300"
                }`}
              >
                {cat}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Product Catalog Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        {filteredProducts.map((product) => {
          const qty = getQty(product.id);
          const totalCost = product.pricePerUnit * qty;
          const outOfStock = product.stockAvailable <= 0;

          return (
            <div
              key={product.id}
              className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-paddy-500 transition-all"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {product.category}
                  </span>
                  {product.certifiedOrganic && (
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-black text-forest-900 bg-harvest-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      Organic tag · demo
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900 leading-snug">
                  {language === "ta" ? product.tamilName : product.name}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                  By {product.brand}
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 my-3 leading-relaxed">
                  {product.description}
                </p>

                <div className="p-2.5 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200 text-[11px] space-y-1 text-ink-700 mb-4">
                  <p className="font-bold text-forest-900">
                    Dosage: {product.dosageRecommendation}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-ink-500">
                    Supplier: {product.supplierName}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Price & Quantity Selector */}
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-sage-200 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-ink-500 block uppercase">
                      Price / {product.unit}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                      <span className="font-display font-black text-xl text-forest-900">
                        ₹{product.pricePerUnit}
                      </span>
                      {product.originalPrice && (
                        <span className="text-xs text-ink-400 line-through">
                          ₹{product.originalPrice}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Quantity Stepper */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-cream-100 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl p-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleQtyChange(product.id, -1)}
                      aria-label={`Decrease ${product.name} quantity`}
                      className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-white text-forest-900 font-bold flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-sage-100"
                    >
                      -
                    </button>
                    <span className="font-bold text-xs text-forest-900 px-1">
                      {qty}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleQtyChange(product.id, 1)}
                      disabled={qty >= product.stockAvailable}
                      aria-label={`Increase ${product.name} quantity`}
                      className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-white text-forest-900 font-bold flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-sage-100 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-ink-700">
                  <span>Estimated Total:</span>
                  <span className="text-sm font-black text-paddy-700">
                    ₹{totalCost.toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleBuyProduct(product)}
                  disabled={outOfStock || qty > product.stockAvailable}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                  <span>
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? "பெட்டகத்தில் சேர்க்க"
                      : outOfStock
                        ? "Out of Stock"
                        : "Place Demo Order"}
                  </span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleBuyProduct(product)}
                  disabled={outOfStock || qty > product.stockAvailable}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                  <span>
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? "டெமோ ஆர்டர் செய்யவும்"
                      : outOfStock
                        ? "Out of Stock"
                        : "Place Demo Order"}
                  </span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ShieldCheck, FileText, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Calculator, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const InsurancePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [acres, setAcres] = useState(3.5);
  const sumInsuredPerAcre = 37500;
  const farmerShareRate = 0.015; // 1.5% for Kharif/Rabi foodgrains

  const totalSumInsured = acres * sumInsuredPerAcre;
  const farmerPremium = totalSumInsured * farmerShareRate;
  const govtSubsidy = totalSumInsured * 0.085; // 8.5% paid by state & central govts

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}

FILE: src\pages\IrrigationPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Droplets,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Plus,
  Sun,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  TrendingDown,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const IrrigationPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [showLogModal, setShowLogModal] = useState(false);
  const [source, setSource] = useState<
    "Cauvery River Canal" | "Borewell (Solar)"
  >("Cauvery River Canal");
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(5);
  const [dieselCost, setDieselCost] = useState("0");

  const handleLogIrrigation = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    AppStore.addIrrigation({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      source,
      method: "Flood / Basin",
      durationHours: duration,
      electricityCost: 0,
      dieselCost: parseFloat(dieselCost) || 0,
      notes: `${source} operated for ${duration} hours.`,
    });

    if (parseFloat(dieselCost) > 0) {
      AppStore.addExpense({
        seasonId: activeSeasonId,
        category: "Irrigation",
        description: `Borewell Diesel Fuel (${duration} hrs)`,
        amount: parseFloat(dieselCost),
        date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
        paymentMode: "Cash",
        paidTo: "Fuel Depot Thiruvaiyaru",
      });
    }

    setShowLogModal(false);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Droplets className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "பாசன மேலாண்மை & நீர் பாதுகாப்பு"
                : "Delta Water Management & Irrigation Log"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பாசன அட்டவணை & சூரியசக்தி பம்புசெட்"
              : "Irrigation Scheduler & Solar Borewell"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "காவிரி பாசன முறை முறைப்பங்கீடு மற்றும் 5 HP சோலார் பம்புசெட் பயன்பாடு"
              : "Rotational canal turn management, water depth tracking, and solar energy offset"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => setShowLogModal(true)}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பாசனப் பதிவு சேர்க்க"
              : "Log Water Application"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Top Metric Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Active Water Depth
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
            2.5 cm
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-semibold mt-1">
            ✓ AWD (Alternate Wetting & Drying)
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Total Canal Rounds
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
            {state.irrigations.length} Events
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1">
            Grand Anicut branch canal distribution
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Solar Energy Savings
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-paddy-700 mt-1">
            ₹12,400 Saved
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-forest-800 font-semibold mt-1">
            Zero grid electricity or diesel burn
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Irrigation Log Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "நடப்பு பருவ பாசனப் பதிவுகள்"
            : "Irrigation History & Fuel Ledger"}
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-sage-200">
          {state.irrigations.map((irr) => (
            <div
              key={irr.id}
              className="py-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-paddy-50 border border-paddy-200 flex items-center justify-center text-paddy-700 shrink-0">
                  <Droplets className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                    {irr.source}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
                    Date: {irr.date} • Method: {irr.method} • Duration:{" "}
                    {irr.durationHours} Hours
                  </p>
                  {irr.notes && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-forest-800 font-medium mt-1">
                      "{irr.notes}"
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-right">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">
                    Energy Cost
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-display font-black text-sm text-paddy-700">
                    ₹{irr.dieselCost}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                  Logged ✓
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Modal */}
      {showLogModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta" ? "புதிய பாசனப் பதிவு" : "Log Irrigation Turn"}
            </h3>
            <form onSubmit={handleLogIrrigation} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Water Source
                </label>
                <select
                  value={source}
                  onChange={(e) => setSource(e.target.value as any)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="Cauvery River Canal">
                    Cauvery River Canal (கால்வாய் முறை நீர்)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Borewell (Solar)">
                    Borewell Solar Pump (சூரிய சக்தி ஆழ்துளை)
                  </option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Duration (Hours)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={duration}
                  onChange={(e) => setDuration(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Fuel / Diesel Cost (₹)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={dieselCost}
                  onChange={(e) => setDieselCost(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                  Enter 0 if solar or gravity canal
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowLogModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                >
                  Save Log
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\LabourPage.tsx
