import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Users, Phone, Calendar, CheckCircle2, Plus, Clock, CircleDollarSign, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const LabourPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [bookingModal, setBookingModal] = useState(false);
  const [workType, setWorkType] = useState('Hand Weeding');
  const [workersCount, setWorkersCount] = useState(8);
  const [dailyWage, setDailyWage] = useState(500);
  const [leadName, setLeadName] = useState('Meenakshi Weeding Team');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('+91 94861 88319');

  const handleBookLabour = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const totalWage = workersCount * dailyWage;

    // Record into season expenses
    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      category: 'Labour',
      description: `${workType} (${workersCount} Workers)`,
      amount: totalWage,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      paymentMode: 'Cash',
      paidTo: leadName,
    });

    setBookingModal(false);
    triggerRefresh();
    alert(language === 'ta' ? 'வேளாண் கூலி ஆட்கள் முன்பதிவு செய்யப்பட்டு செலவு பதிவானது!' : 'Labour team booked & wage expense logged into active crop season!');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'வேளாண் கூலி ஆட்கள் குழுக்கள்' : 'Farm Labour Groups & Wage Scheduling'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'கூலி ஆட்கள் முன்பதிவு & கூலிப்பட்டியல்' : 'Labour Teams & Field Task Crews'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'நாற்று பறித்தல், நடுவு, களை எடுத்தல் மற்றும் மூட்டை பிடிக்கும் குழுக்கள்'
              : 'Direct connection with agricultural self-help groups (SHGs) and harvesting crews'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => setBookingModal(true)}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'ஆட்கள் முன்பதிவு செய்ய' : 'Book Labour Team'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Available Labour Teams Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {state.labourBookings.map((b) => (
          <div
            key={b.id}
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  {b.workType}
                </span>
                <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900 mt-1">
                  {b.leadWorkerName}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-800 bg-paddy-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                {b.status}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Workers</span>
                <p className="font-extrabold text-sm text-forest-900">{b.workersCount} Persons</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Daily Wage</span>
                <p className="font-extrabold text-sm text-forest-900">₹{b.dailyWagePerPerson}</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Total Cost</span>
                <p className="font-extrabold text-sm text-paddy-700">₹{b.totalCostEstimate.toLocaleString()}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-ink-600 pt-1">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-forest-800">
                <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-paddy-600" />
                {b.phone}
              </span>
              <span>Required: {b.requiredDate}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Book Labour Modal */}
      {bookingModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === 'ta' ? 'புதிய கூலி ஆட்கள் குழு முன்பதிவு' : 'Schedule Farm Labour Crew'}
            </h3>

            <form onSubmit={handleBookLabour} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">Work Type</label>
                <select
                  value={workType}
                  onChange={(e) => setWorkType(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="Hand Weeding">Hand Weeding (களை எடுத்தல்)</option>
                  <option value="Paddy Nursery Pulling">Paddy Nursery Pulling (நாற்று பறித்தல்)</option>
                  <option value="Transplanting">Transplanting (நடுவு பணி)</option>
                  <option value="Harvesting & Bagging">Harvesting & Bagging (அறுவடை & மூட்டை பிடித்தல்)</option>
                  <option value="Bio-Pesticide Spraying">Bio-Pesticide Spraying (மருந்து தெளித்தல்)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">Worker Count</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={workersCount}
                    onChange={(e) => setWorkersCount(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">Wage / Person (₹)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={dailyWage}
                    onChange={(e) => setDailyWage(parseInt(e.target.value) || 0)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">Crew Lead / Contact</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={leadName}
                  onChange={(e) => setLeadName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="p-3 bg-cream-100 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs flex items-center justify-between font-bold">
                <span>Total Estimated Wage:</span>
                <span className="text-base text-forest-900">₹{(workersCount * dailyWage).toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setBookingModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                >
                  Confirm Booking
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Leaf,
  LoaderCircle,
  LockKeyhole,
  Mail,
  PlayCircle,
  Phone,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { Logo } from "../components/brand/Logo";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

type AuthMode = "signin" | "signup" | "forgot" | "reset";

function friendlyAuthError(error: unknown): string {
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
    return "Unable to connect right now. Please try again.";
  }

  const message = error.message.toLowerCase();
  if (
    message.includes("invalid login credentials") ||
    message.includes("invalid_credentials")
  ) {
    return "Email or password is incorrect.";
  }
  if (
    message.includes("email not confirmed") ||
    message.includes("email_not_confirmed")
  ) {
    return "Please verify your email before signing in.";
  }
  if (
    message.includes("already registered") ||
    message.includes("user already registered")
  ) {
    return "An account with this email already exists. Sign in or reset your password.";
  }
  if (
    message.includes("fetch") ||
    message.includes("network") ||
    message.includes("timeout")
  ) {
    return "Unable to connect right now. Please try again.";
  }
  if (message.includes("sync") || message.includes("offline")) {
    return "Your account was signed out, but changes may not have synced. Reconnect and sign in again to retry.";
  }
  if (
    message.includes("session") ||
    message.includes("recovery") ||
    message.includes("auth session missing")
  ) {
    return "This password reset link is invalid or has expired. Request a new one.";
  }
  return "We couldn't complete that request. Check your details and try again.";
}

export const LoginPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { navigate, language, currentRoute } = useNavigation();
  const {
    cloudEnabled,
    passwordRecovery,
    signIn,
    signUp,
    startDemo,
    sendPasswordReset,
    updatePassword,
  } = useAuth();
  const [selectedMode, setSelectedMode] = useState<AuthMode>("signin");
  const mode: AuthMode =
    passwordRecovery || currentRoute === "/reset-password"
      ? "reset"
      : currentRoute === "/forgot-password"
        ? "forgot"
        : currentRoute === "/signup"
          ? "signup"
          : selectedMode;
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("9000000421");
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState("4210");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirmPassword, setShowConfirmPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");

  const changeMode = (nextMode: AuthMode) => {
    setSelectedMode(nextMode === "signup" ? "signup" : "signin");
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    setPassword("");
    setConfirmPassword("");
    if (nextMode === "signup") navigate("/signup");
    else if (nextMode === "forgot") navigate("/forgot-password");
    else if (nextMode === "reset") navigate("/reset-password");
    else navigate("/login");
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (loading) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");

    if (mode === "signup" && password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }
    if ((mode === "signup" || mode === "reset") && password.length < 8) {
      setError("Use a password with at least 8 characters.");
      return;
    }
    if (mode === "reset" && password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      if (cloudEnabled && mode === "signin") {
        await signIn(email.trim(), password);
        navigate("/dashboard");
      } else if (cloudEnabled && mode === "signup") {
        const { confirmationRequired } = await signUp(
          email.trim(),
          password,
          fullName.trim(),
        );
        if (confirmationRequired) {
          setNotice(
            "Account created successfully. Please check your email to verify your account.",
          );
          setSelectedMode("signin");
          navigate("/login");
        } else {
          navigate("/dashboard");
        }
      } else if (cloudEnabled && mode === "forgot") {
        await sendPasswordReset(email.trim());
        setNotice(
          "If an account exists for that email, a password reset link is on its way.",
        );
      } else if (cloudEnabled && mode === "reset") {
        await updatePassword(password);
        setNotice(
          "Password updated. You can now sign in with your new password.",
        );
        setPassword("");
        setConfirmPassword("");
        setSelectedMode("signin");
        setError("");
        navigate("/login");
      } else if (!cloudEnabled && mode === "signin") {
        if (phone !== "9000000421" || otp !== "4210") {
          setError(
            "Use the demo phone 9000000421 and demo OTP 4210. This is local demo access, not personal account authentication.",
          );
          return;
        }
        AppStore.setCurrentUser({
          id: "USER-VIJAY-DEMO",
          phone,
          name: "VIJAY",
          tamilName: "விஜய்",
          role: "farmer",
        });
        navigate("/dashboard");
      }
    } catch (authError) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("Authentication request failed", authError);
      setError(friendlyAuthError(authError));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDemoAccess = async () => {
    if (loading) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await startDemo();
      AppStore.setCurrentUser({
        id: "USER-VIJAY-DEMO",
        phone: "9000000421",
        name: "VIJAY",
        tamilName: "விஜய்",
        role: "farmer",
      });
      navigate("/dashboard");
    } catch (demoError) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("Could not switch to demo", demoError);
      setError(friendlyAuthError(demoError));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const title = {
    signin: "Welcome back",
    signup: "Create your account",
    forgot: "Reset your password",
    reset: "Choose a new password",
  }[mode];
  const subtitle = {
    signin: "Sign in to continue to Vithai to Aruvadai.",
    signup: "Create a personal account for your farm workspace.",
    forgot: "Enter your account email and we’ll send a secure reset link.",
    reset: "Choose a new password for your V2A account.",
  }[mode];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl py-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border border-sage-200 bg-white shadow-agro md:grid md:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr]">
        <div className="relative hidden min-h-[620px] flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-forest-900 via-forest-900 to-paddy-800 p-10 text-white md:flex">
          <div className="absolute -right-20 -top-20 h-80 w-80 rounded-full border border-white/10" />
          <div className="absolute -right-5 -top-5 h-52 w-52 rounded-full border border-white/10" />
          <div className="relative">
            <Logo size="md" showTamil className="text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="relative space-y-5">
            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-white/10 text-harvest-300">
              <Leaf className="h-7 w-7" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="max-w-sm font-display text-4xl font-extrabold leading-tight">
              From seed to harvest, all in one place.
            </h1>
            <p className="max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-sage-100">
              Plan crops, keep field records, and follow your harvest journey
              with a secure account.
            </p>
          </div>
          <p className="relative text-xs text-sage-200">
            VITHAI TO ARUVADAI · விதை முதல் அறுவடை வரை
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 sm:p-10 lg:p-12">
          <div className="mb-8 md:hidden">
            <Logo size="md" showTamil />
          </div>
          <div className="mb-7">
            <p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-paddy-700">
              <Leaf className="h-4 w-4" />
              Secure farmer workspace
            </p>
            <h2 className="font-display text-3xl font-extrabold capitalize text-forest-950">
              {title}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-ink-600">{subtitle}</p>
          </div>

          {!cloudEnabled && (
            <div className="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-harvest-300 bg-harvest-50 p-4">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-forest-900">
                Local demo mode
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-ink-600">
                Supabase is not configured here. Personal account sign-in and
                recovery are unavailable; use the separate demo account.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {cloudEnabled && mode === "signup" && (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="auth-full-name"
                  className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                >
                  Full name
                </label>
                <input
                  id="auth-full-name"
                  type="text"
                  value={fullName}
                  onChange={(event) => setFullName(event.target.value)}
                  autoComplete="name"
                  minLength={2}
                  required
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none transition focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200 disabled:opacity-60"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            {cloudEnabled && mode !== "reset" && (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="auth-email"
                  className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                >
                  Email address
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-ink-400" />
                  <input
                    id="auth-email"
                    type="email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
                    autoComplete="email"
                    required
                    disabled={loading}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none transition focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200 disabled:opacity-60"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {!cloudEnabled && mode === "signin" && (
              <>
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="auth-demo-phone"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                  >
                    Demo phone number
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Phone className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-ink-400" />
                    <input
                      id="auth-demo-phone"
                      type="tel"
                      value={phone}
                      onChange={(event) => setPhone(event.target.value)}
                      autoComplete="tel"
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      required
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="auth-demo-otp"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                  >
                    Demo OTP
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="auth-demo-otp"
                    type="text"
                    value={otp}
                    onChange={(event) => setOtp(event.target.value)}
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    autoComplete="one-time-code"
                    pattern="[0-9]{4}"
                    maxLength={4}
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200"
                  />
                </div>
              </>
            )}

            {((cloudEnabled && ["signin", "signup", "reset"].includes(mode)) ||
              (!cloudEnabled && mode === "signin")) && (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="auth-password"
                  className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                >
                  {mode === "reset" ? "New password" : "Password"}
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <LockKeyhole className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-ink-400" />
                  <input
                    id="auth-password"
                    type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                    autoComplete={
                      mode === "signin" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                    }
                    minLength={cloudEnabled && mode !== "signin" ? 8 : undefined}
                    required
                    disabled={loading}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 py-3 pl-10 pr-12 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none transition focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200 disabled:opacity-60"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword((visible) => !visible)}
                    aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-ink-500 hover:bg-sage-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-600"
                  >
                    {showPassword ? (
                      <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
                {cloudEnabled && mode === "signup" && (
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] text-ink-500">
                    Use at least 8 characters.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {cloudEnabled && ["signup", "reset"].includes(mode) && (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="auth-confirm-password"
                  className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
                >
                  Confirm {mode === "reset" ? "new " : ""}password
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <LockKeyhole className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-ink-400" />
                  <input
                    id="auth-confirm-password"
                    type={showConfirmPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                    value={confirmPassword}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)
                    }
                    autoComplete="new-password"
                    minLength={8}
                    required
                    disabled={loading}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 py-3 pl-10 pr-12 text-sm text-ink-900 outline-none transition focus:border-paddy-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-200 disabled:opacity-60"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setShowConfirmPassword((visible) => !visible)
                    }
                    aria-label={
                      showConfirmPassword
                        ? "Hide confirm password"
                        : "Show confirm password"
                    }
                    className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-ink-500 hover:bg-sage-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-600"
                  >
                    {showConfirmPassword ? (
                      <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {error && (
              <p
                role="alert"
                className="rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 bg-terracotta-50 p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-terracotta-800"
              >
                {error}
              </p>
            )}
            {notice && (
              <p
                role="status"
                className="rounded-xl border border-paddy-300 bg-paddy-50 p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-paddy-800"
              >
                {notice}
              </p>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-4 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-forest-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-600 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <LoaderCircle className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                  {mode === "signin"
                    ? "Signing in..."
                    : mode === "signup"
                      ? "Creating account..."
                      : mode === "forgot"
                        ? "Sending reset link..."
                        : "Updating password..."}
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {mode === "signin"
                    ? cloudEnabled
                      ? "Login"
                      : "Sign in to demo"
                    : mode === "signup"
                      ? "Create account"
                      : mode === "forgot"
                        ? "Send reset link"
                        : "Update password"}
                  <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          {cloudEnabled && mode === "signin" && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => changeMode("forgot")}
              className="mt-4 w-full text-center text-xs font-semibold text-paddy-700 hover:underline focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-600"
            >
              Forgot password?
            </button>
          )}

          {cloudEnabled && (mode === "signin" || mode === "signup") && (
            <div className="mt-6 border-t border-sage-200 pt-5 text-center">
              <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-ink-500">
                Demo access is separate and uses local sample data.
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => void handleDemoAccess()}
                disabled={loading}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-400 bg-cream-100 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-forest-900 transition hover:bg-sage-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-paddy-600 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                <PlayCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
                Continue as demo user (VIJAY)
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 border-t border-sage-200 pt-5 text-center">
            {cloudEnabled && mode === "signin" && (
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
                New to V2A?{" "}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => changeMode("signup")}
                  className="font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:underline"
                >
                  Create an account
                </button>
              </p>
            )}
            {cloudEnabled && mode === "signup" && (
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
                Already registered?{" "}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => changeMode("signin")}
                  className="font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:underline"
                >
                  Sign in
                </button>
              </p>
            )}
            {(mode === "forgot" || mode === "reset") && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => changeMode("signin")}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:underline"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                Back to sign in
              </button>
            )}
            {!cloudEnabled && (
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "டெமோ விவசாயி: VIJAY · டெமோ OTP: 4210"
                  : "Demo farmer: VIJAY · Demo OTP: 4210"}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Truck, MapPin, Calculator, Phone, CheckCircle2, ArrowRight, CircleDollarSign } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const LogisticsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [destination, setDestination] = useState<'mill' | 'godown' | 'mandi'>('mill');
  const [selectedVehicle, setSelectedVehicle] = useState(state.transportListings[1]); // Eicher 4T
  const [bagCount, setBagCount] = useState(110);
  const [bookingDone, setBookingDone] = useState(false);

  const destinationOptions = {
    mill: {
      name: 'Thanjavur Modern Rice Mill Pvt Ltd',
      tamilName: 'தஞ்சாவூர் நவீன அரிசி ஆலை',
      distanceKm: 14,
      quotedOfferPrice: 2280,
    },
    godown: {
      name: 'Thanjavur Central WDRA Godown',
      tamilName: 'மத்திய ஒழுங்குமுறை சேமிப்புக் கிடங்கு',
      distanceKm: 9.5,
      quotedOfferPrice: 2250,
    },
    mandi: {
      name: 'Kumbakonam Regulated Market Committee (Mandi)',
      tamilName: 'கும்பகோணம் ஒழுங்குமுறை விற்பனைக் கூடம்',
      distanceKm: 28,
      quotedOfferPrice: 2260,
    },
  };

  const currentDest = destinationOptions[destination];

  // Freight calculation: Base Charge + (Distance * RatePerKm)
  const tripsNeeded = Math.ceil(bagCount / selectedVehicle.maxPayloadBags);
  const totalFreight = (selectedVehicle.baseCharge + currentDest.distanceKm * selectedVehicle.ratePerKm) * tripsNeeded;

  // Estimated Net Proceeds
  const grossValue = bagCount * currentDest.quotedOfferPrice;
  const baggingCost = bagCount * 30;
  const cess = Math.round(grossValue * 0.01);
  const netProceeds = grossValue - totalFreight - baggingCost - cess;

  const handleBookVehicle = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      category: 'Transport',
      description: `${selectedVehicle.vehicleType} transport to ${currentDest.name}`,
      amount: totalFreight,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      paymentMode: 'UPI',
      paidTo: selectedVehicle.driverName,
    });
    setBookingDone(true);
    triggerRefresh();
    setTimeout(() => {
      setBookingDone(false);
      navigate('/net-proceeds');
    }, 1500);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'பண்ணை முதல் சந்தை வரை போக்குவரத்து' : 'Farm-to-Mandi Transport Logistics'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'சரக்கு கட்டண கால்குலேட்டர் & லாரி முன்பதிவு' : 'Freight Rate Calculator & Vehicle Booking'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            Thiruvaiyaru Farm Gate • Transparent distance & vehicle payload matrix
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/net-proceeds')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'நிகர வரவு கால்குலேட்டர்' : 'Net Proceeds Breakdown'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Destination & Net Proceeds Comparison Matrix (Requirement G) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'இடத்தேர்வு & நிகர வருமான ஒப்பீடு' : 'Route & Destination Net Realization Comparison'}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
          Compare total freight deductions and net earnings across destinations:
        </p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {(['mill', 'godown', 'mandi'] as const).map((destKey) => {
            const dest = destinationOptions[destKey];
            const freight = (selectedVehicle.baseCharge + dest.distanceKm * selectedVehicle.ratePerKm) * tripsNeeded;
            const gross = bagCount * dest.quotedOfferPrice;
            const net = gross - freight - (bagCount * 30) - Math.round(gross * 0.01);
            const isSelected = destination === destKey;

            return (
              <div
                key={destKey}
                onClick={() => setDestination(destKey)}
                className={`p-5 rounded-3xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-paddy-600 bg-cream-50 shadow-agro'
                    : 'border-sage-300 bg-white hover:border-paddy-400'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {dest.distanceKm} km distance
                  </span>
                  {isSelected && (
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Selected
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <h4 className="font-bold text-base text-forest-900">{dest.name}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-0.5">Offer: ₹{dest.quotedOfferPrice}/bag</p>

                <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-sage-200 text-xs space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-ink-500">Freight:</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-terracotta-700">-₹{freight.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-sm text-forest-900 pt-1">
                    <span>Est. Net Proceeds:</span>
                    <span className="text-paddy-700">₹{net.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Vehicle Selection & Freight Calculator */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left: Vehicle Selector */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'சரக்கு வாகனம் தேர்வு' : 'Select Transport Vehicle'}
          </h3>

          {state.transportListings.map((veh) => (
            <div
              key={veh.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedVehicle(veh)}
              className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedVehicle.id === veh.id
                  ? 'bg-forest-800 text-cream-50 border-forest-900 shadow-agro'
                  : 'bg-white text-ink-900 border-sage-300 hover:border-paddy-500'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${selectedVehicle.id === veh.id ? 'text-harvest-400' : 'text-paddy-700'}`}>
                  Max Payload: {veh.maxPayloadBags} Bags
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold">Base ₹{veh.baseCharge} + ₹{veh.ratePerKm}/km</span>
              </div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-sm">{veh.vehicleType}</h4>
              <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${selectedVehicle.id === veh.id ? 'text-sage-200' : 'text-ink-500'}`}>
                Driver: {veh.driverName} ({veh.phone})
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Right 2 Cols: Freight Summary & Booking Confirmation */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
          <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'போக்குவரத்து முன்பதிவு விவரம்' : 'Freight Quote & Booking Dispatch'}
          </h3>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div className="p-4 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Distance</span>
              <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-1">{currentDest.distanceKm} km</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">Farm Gate to Destination</p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Vehicle Trips</span>
              <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-1">{tripsNeeded} Trip(s)</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">{bagCount} total bags loaded</p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">Total Freight Quote</span>
              <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-terracotta-600 mt-1">₹{totalFreight.toLocaleString()}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">Includes driver & toll</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {bookingDone ? (
            <div className="p-4 bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-900 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-bold animate-fadeIn">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-paddy-700 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                {language === 'ta'
                  ? 'வாகனம் முன்பதிவு செய்யப்பட்டது! சரக்கு கட்டணம் பயிர் செலவில் சேர்க்கப்பட்டு நிகர லாபம் கணக்கிடப்பட்டது.'
                  : 'Vehicle dispatch confirmed! Freight cost recorded in active season expenses.'}
              </span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={handleBookVehicle}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all"
            >
              {language === 'ta' ? 'லாரி முன்பதிவை உறுதி செய்க' : 'Confirm Truck Booking & Log Transport Expense'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Tractor, Calendar, Phone, CheckCircle2, Star, Clock, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const MachineryPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [bookingModal, setBookingModal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [hours, setHours] = useState(4);
  const [bookingSuccess, setBookingSuccess] = useState(false);

  const handleBookEquipment = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingModal) return;

    const totalRent = bookingModal.hourlyRate * hours;

    // Automatically record machinery expense into season
    AppStore.addExpense({
      seasonId: activeSeasonId,
      category: 'Machinery',
      description: `${bookingModal.title} (${hours} Hours)`,
      amount: totalRent,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      paymentMode: 'Cash',
      paidTo: bookingModal.contactPerson,
    });

    setBookingSuccess(true);
    triggerRefresh();
    setTimeout(() => {
      setBookingSuccess(false);
      setBookingModal(null);
    }, 1500);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Tractor className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'வாடகை சேவை மையம் (CHC)' : 'Custom Hiring Center (CHC) Mechanization'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'வேளாண் வாடகை இயந்திரங்கள்' : 'Farm Machinery & Equipment Rental'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'டிராக்டர்கள், இயந்திர நடவு கருவிகள், அறுவடை இயந்திரங்கள் மற்றும் ட்ரோன் தெளிப்பான்'
              : 'Direct hire from Primary Agricultural Cooperative Societies (PACS) and authorized operators'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/farm-calendar')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'பண்ணை நாட்காட்டி' : 'Field Operations Calendar'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Machinery Catalog Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {state.machineryListings.map((mach) => (
          <div
            key={mach.id}
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-paddy-500 transition-all"
          >
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  {mach.equipmentType}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 bg-harvest-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg">
                  <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-harvest-600 text-harvest-600" />
                  {mach.rating}
                </span>
              </div>

              <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900 leading-snug">
                {language === 'ta' ? mach.tamilTitle : mach.title}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1">{mach.chcCenterName}</p>

              <div className="my-4 p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1.5 text-xs text-ink-700">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">Operator:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-forest-900">{mach.contactPerson}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">Location:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-forest-900">{mach.village}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-ink-500">Includes Fuel & Driver:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-paddy-700">Yes (உட்பட) ✓</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-sage-200 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 uppercase font-bold block">Hourly Rate</span>
                  <span className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900">
                    ₹{mach.hourlyRate}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-ink-500"> / hour</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 uppercase font-bold block">Daily Rate</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm text-paddy-700">₹{mach.dailyRate} / day</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => setBookingModal(mach)}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all"
              >
                {language === 'ta' ? 'இப்போதே வாடகைக்கு முன்பதிவு செய்' : 'Book Machinery Rental'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Booking Modal */}
      {bookingModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === 'ta' ? 'இயந்திர வாடகை முன்பதிவு' : 'Confirm Machinery Booking'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
              {bookingModal.title} • {bookingModal.chcCenterName}
            </p>

            {bookingSuccess ? (
              <div className="p-4 bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-900 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-bold animate-fadeIn">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-paddy-700 shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  {language === 'ta'
                    ? 'முன்பதிவு உறுதி செய்யப்பட்டது! செலவு தானாக பயிர் வரவு-செலவில் சேர்க்கப்பட்டது.'
                    : 'Booking confirmed! Operator dispatched and rental cost added to season expenses.'}
                </span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleBookEquipment} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    {language === 'ta' ? 'தேவையான நேரம் (Hours)' : 'Operating Duration (Hours)'}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    max="20"
                    value={hours}
                    onChange={(e) => setHours(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                    className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 bg-cream-100 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs flex items-center justify-between font-bold">
                  <span>Total Rental Quote:</span>
                  <span className="text-base text-forest-900">₹{(bookingModal.hourlyRate * hours).toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setBookingModal(null)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
                  >
                    Confirm & Record Expense
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { CircleDollarSign, Calculator, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, CheckCircle2, TrendingUp, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const NetProceedsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [bags, setBags] = useState(110);
  const [ratePerBag, setRatePerBag] = useState(2280);
  const [mandiCessPct, setMandiCessPct] = useState(1.0);
  const [baggingRate, setBaggingRate] = useState(30);
  const [freightCost, setFreightCost] = useState(3500);
  const [moisturePenalty, setMoisturePenalty] = useState(0); // 0 because 13.8% moisture

  // Financial Computations
  const grossSale = bags * ratePerBag;
  const mandiCess = Math.round((grossSale * mandiCessPct) / 100);
  const totalBagging = bags * baggingRate;
  const totalDeductions = mandiCess + totalBagging + freightCost + moisturePenalty;
  const netProceeds = grossSale - totalDeductions;

  // Cultivation Cost from active season
  const totalExpenses = 75100;
  const finalProfit = netProceeds - totalExpenses;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <CircleDollarSign className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'வெளிப்படையான நிகர வருமானக் கணக்கீடு' : 'Transparent Realized Proceeds Engine'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'நிகர விற்பனை வரவு கால்குலேட்டர்' : 'Net Proceeds & Deductions Calculator'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === 'ta'
              ? 'மொத்த விற்பனைத் தொகையிலிருந்து செஸ், மூட்டை பிடித்தல் மற்றும் போக்குவரத்து கழிவுகள்'
              : 'Audit every single statutory deduction between gross buyer invoice and bank settlement'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/crop-journey')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'பயிர் பயணம் (Crop Journey)' : 'View Crop Journey'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Top Net Result Cards */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 via-forest-800 to-paddy-900 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-cream-100 shadow-agro grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider block">Gross Produce Invoice</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-cream-50 mt-1">
            ₹{grossSale.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-sage-200 mt-1">{bags} Bags @ ₹{ratePerBag}/bag</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider block">Total Deductions</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-terracotta-400 mt-1">
            -₹{totalDeductions.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-sage-200 mt-1">Cess + Bagging + Freight</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-sage-300 uppercase tracking-wider block">Net Sale in Bank</span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-harvest-400 mt-1">
            ₹{netProceeds.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-300 font-bold mt-1">
            Realized Margin: ₹{finalProfit.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detailed Deduction Breakdown Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'கழிவுகள் விபரம் (Itemized Deductions)' : 'Statutory & Logistic Deductions Breakdown'}
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-sage-200 text-xs">
          <div className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">1. Gross Produce Value</span>
              <p className="text-ink-500">110 bags x 75 kg = 8,250 kg @ contracted rate</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-display font-black text-base text-forest-900">
              ₹{grossSale.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">2. Regulated Market Cess / Mandi Fee (1.0%)</span>
              <p className="text-ink-500">Tamil Nadu Agricultural Marketing Board statutory fee</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-terracotta-700">
              -₹{mandiCess.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">3. Gunny Bags & Machine Stitching</span>
              <p className="text-ink-500">110 standard jute bags @ ₹30 / bag</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-terracotta-700">
              -₹{totalBagging.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">4. Freight & Transport to Mill</span>
              <p className="text-ink-500">Medium Truck Eicher 4T transport from Thiruvaiyaru to Mill</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-terracotta-700">
              -₹{freightCost.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">5. Moisture Content Penalty</span>
              <p className="text-paddy-700 font-semibold">Tested 13.8% (Below 14% max threshold - Zero penalty applied!)</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-paddy-700">
              ₹0
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="py-4 flex items-center justify-between bg-cream-100 px-4 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300">
            <div>
              <span className="font-display font-black text-forest-900 text-base">Net Proceeds Realized</span>
              <p className="text-ink-600">Contracted payable balance in bank NEFT</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-paddy-800">
              ₹{netProceeds.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Net Profit Realization vs Total Expenses */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-sage-50 rounded-3xl border border-sage-300 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 uppercase">Season Total Field Expenses:</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">₹{totalExpenses.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 uppercase">Net Realized Proceeds:</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 text-sm">₹{netProceeds.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-sage-300">
            <div>
              <span className="font-display font-black text-forest-900 text-lg">Final Farm Net Margin (Profit)</span>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">After all field inputs, machinery, labour, and marketing costs</p>
            </div>
            <span className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-paddy-700">

      </div>

      {/* Recommended Crop Rotation Sequence */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'டெல்டா மண்வள பயிர் சுழற்சி முறை' : 'Cauvery Delta 3-Tier Cropping Sequence'}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          <div className="p-5 rounded-3xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 space-y-3">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-500">Tier 1 • Completed ✓</span>
            <h4 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              Samba Long Duration Paddy
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">CR 1009 Sub 1 • 135 Days • 110 Bags harvested @ ₹2,280/bag.</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700">Realized Profit: ₹1,66,392</p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-5 rounded-3xl bg-paddy-50 border-2 border-paddy-500 space-y-3">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-800 bg-paddy-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              Tier 2 • Recommended Next
            </span>
            <h4 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              Rice Fallow Blackgram (VBN 8)
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">Sown directly into standing paddy stubble 7-10 days before harvest utilizing residual dew moisture.</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-800">

            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-5 rounded-3xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 space-y-3">

            <h4 className="font-display font-extrabold text-lg text-forest-900">
              Summer Sesame (TMV 7)
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">Low water demand, drought hardy, fetches premium oil mill rate (&gt;₹10,000/quintal) in summer markets.</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700">Est. Net Margin: ₹38,000 / Acre</p>
          </div>

      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-forest-900 to-paddy-900 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-cream-100 shadow-agro flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
        <div className="space-y-2 max-w-xl">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-harvest-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Advance Seed Booking Active
          </span>
          <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-cream-50">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'அடுத்த பருவ சான்றளிக்கப்பட்ட விதைகளை இப்போதே முன்பதிவு செய்க' : 'Reserve Certified VBN 8 Pulse Seeds in Advance'}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-sage-200">
            Direct allocation from TNAU National Pulses Research Centre, Vamban. Guarantees 90%+ germination rate.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/seed-passport')}
          className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-harvest-400 hover:bg-harvest-500 text-forest-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-gold transition-all shrink-0"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'விதை பாஸ்போர்ட் திறக்க' : 'Reserve Certified Seeds'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\pages\OffersPage.tsx
