import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Tag,
  CheckCircle2,
  ArrowRight,
  CircleDollarSign,
  Store,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const OffersPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const listing = state.produceListings.find(
    (item) => item.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );
  const offers = listing
    ? state.buyerOffers.filter((offer) => offer.listingId === listing.id)
    : [];
  const [actionNotice, setActionNotice] = useState("");

  const handleAccept = (offerId: string) => {
    const accepted = AppStore.acceptOffer(offerId);
    if (!accepted) {
      setActionNotice(
        "This offer is no longer available. Refresh the page and review the current listing.",
      );
      triggerRefresh();
      return;
    }
    triggerRefresh();
    setTimeout(() => {
      navigate("/sales");
    }, 1200);
  };

  const handleReject = (offerId: string) => {
    const rejected = AppStore.rejectOffer(offerId);
    if (!rejected) {
      setActionNotice("This offer is no longer pending.");
    } else {
      setActionNotice("Offer declined.");
    }
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Tag className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "நேரடி கொள்முதல் ஏலங்கள்"
                : "Direct Buyer Tender Bids & Negotiations"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "கொள்முதல் சலுகைகள் & ஏலங்கள்"
              : `Buyer offers${listing ? ` on ${listing.quantityBags}-bag lot` : ""}`}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {listing
              ? `${listing.cropName} • ${listing.qualityGrade} (${listing.moisturePct}% moisture)`
              : "Review offers after broadcasting an available harvest lot."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/net-proceeds")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "நிகர லாப கணக்கீட்டாளர்"
            : "Calculate Net Proceeds"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {actionNotice && (
        <div
          role="status"
          className="rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-sage-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-forest-900"
        >
          {actionNotice}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Lot Status Card */}
      {listing && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cream-100 via-harvest-50 to-sage-100 rounded-3xl p-6 border border-harvest-400 shadow-agro flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-800 bg-paddy-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {listing.status} Harvest Lot
            </span>
            <h3 className="font-display font-black text-xl text-forest-900">
              {listing.quantityBags} Bags (
              {(listing.quantityBags * listing.bagWeightKg).toLocaleString()}{" "}
              kg) • {listing.pickupLocation}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
              Asking Price: ₹{listing.askingPricePerBag.toLocaleString()}/bag •
              Quality: {listing.qualityGrade} • Moisture: {listing.moisturePct}%
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/harvest")}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 hover:bg-sage-50"
            >
              {language === "ta" ? "அறுவடை சான்றிதழ்" : "Quality Certificate"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Offers Comparison Cards (Requirement H) */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {offers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-3xl border border-sage-300 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-agro">
            <Store className="mx-auto mb-3 h-8 w-8 text-paddy-700" />
            <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-forest-900">
              No offers for this season yet
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-600">
              Broadcast an unsold harvest lot to generate buyer offers for this
              demo.
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate("/harvest")}
              className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-harvest-300 hover:bg-forest-900"
            >
              Go to Harvest
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          offers.map((offer) => {
            const grossAmount =
              offer.offeredPricePerBag * offer.quantityBagsRequested;
            const isAccepted = offer.status === "Accepted";

            return (
              <div
                key={offer.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border-2 shadow-agro space-y-4 transition-all ${
                  isAccepted
                    ? "border-paddy-600 bg-paddy-50/20"
                    : "border-sage-300"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-sage-200">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                        {offer.buyerOrg}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className={`text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                          isAccepted
                            ? "bg-paddy-600 text-white"
                            : offer.status === "Rejected"
                              ? "bg-terracotta-100 text-terracotta-700"
                              : "bg-harvest-200 text-forest-900"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {offer.status}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                      {offer.buyerName}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                      Offer received on {offer.offerDate}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 block uppercase">
                      Offered Bid Price
                    </span>
                    <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
                      ₹{offer.offeredPricePerBag}{" "}
                      <span className="text-xs font-normal text-ink-500">
                        / 75kg bag
                      </span>
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700">
                      Gross: ₹{grossAmount.toLocaleString()} (
                      {offer.quantityBagsRequested} Bags)
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Terms Breakdown */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs">
                  <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
                    <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">
                      Pickup Terms
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 mt-0.5 block">
                      {offer.pickupTerms}
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                      {offer.pickupTerms === "Buyer Farm-gate Pickup"
                        ? "Buyer arranges transport"
                        : "Farmer delivery required"}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
                    <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">
                      Payment Settlement
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 mt-0.5 block">
                      {offer.paymentTerms}
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                      Settlement follows the terms above
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
                    <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">
                      Quality Acceptance
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 mt-0.5 block">
                      {offer.gradeAccepted}
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                      Grade requirement: {offer.gradeAccepted}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {offer.notes && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-700 italic bg-cream-100 p-3 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                    📝 Buyer Remarks: "{offer.notes}"
                  </p>
                )}

                {/* Action Buttons */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-3 border-t border-sage-200">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => navigate("/net-proceeds")}
                    className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 hover:text-paddy-900 flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    <CircleDollarSign className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>
                      {language === "ta"
                        ? "நிகர லாப கணக்கீடு பார்க்க"
                        : "Simulate Net Proceeds after Deductions"}
                    </span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    {!isAccepted && offer.status !== "Rejected" && (
                      <>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleReject(offer.id)}
                          className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 text-terracotta-700 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-terracotta-50 transition-colors"
                        >
                          {language === "ta" ? "மறுக்கவும்" : "Decline Offer"}
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleAccept(offer.id)}
                          className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all flex items-center gap-2"
                        >
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                          <span>
                            {language === "ta"
                              ? "விலை சலுகையை ஏற்கிறேன்"
                              : "Accept Offer & Generate Bill"}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      </>
                    )}

                    {isAccepted && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => navigate("/sales")}
                        className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-paddy-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2"
                      >
                        <span>
                          {language === "ta"
                            ? "விற்பனை ரசீது பார்க்க"
                            : "View Sale Deed & Weighment"}
                        </span>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\OnboardingPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Sprout,
  ArrowRight,
  ArrowLeft,
  MapPin,
  Layers,
  Droplets,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import type { LandParcel, SeasonName } from "../types";

const SOIL_TYPES: LandParcel["soilType"][] = [
  "Clay Loam",
  "Alluvial",
  "Red Sandy Loam",
  "Black Cotton",
  "Sandy",
];
const WATER_SOURCES: LandParcel["irrigationType"][] = [
  "Canal",
  "Borewell",
  "Drip Irrigation",
  "Open Well",
  "Rainfed",
];

export const OnboardingPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { navigate, language, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);

  // Form State
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<{
    fullName: string;
    tamilFullName: string;
    phone: string;
    village: string;
    taluk: string;
    district: string;
    pincode: string;
    totalAcres: number;
    surveyNumber: string;
    pattaNumber: string;
    soilType: LandParcel["soilType"] | "";
    waterSource: LandParcel["irrigationType"] | "";
    primaryCrop: string;
    plannedVariety: string;
    seasonName: SeasonName;
  }>(() => {
    const state = AppStore.getState();
    const parcel = state.farm.parcels[0];
    const season = state.seasons.find(
      (item) => item.id === state.activeSeasonId,
    );

    return {
      fullName: state.farmer.fullName,
      tamilFullName: state.farmer.tamilFullName,
      phone: state.currentUser.phone,
      village: state.farmer.village,
      taluk: state.farmer.taluk,
      district: state.farmer.district,
      pincode: state.farmer.pincode,
      totalAcres: state.farmer.totalLandAcres,
      surveyNumber: parcel?.surveyNumber || "",
      pattaNumber: parcel?.pattaNumber || "",
      soilType: parcel?.soilType || "",
      waterSource: parcel?.irrigationType || "",
      primaryCrop:
        state.crops.find(
          (item) => item.name === state.farmer.primaryCrops[0],
        )?.id || "",
      plannedVariety: season?.variety || "",
      seasonName: season?.seasonName || "General Season",
    };
  });
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState("");

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (step < 4) setStep(step + 1);
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (step > 1) setStep(step - 1);
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const state = AppStore.getState();
    const existingParcel = state.farm.parcels[0];
    const soilType = SOIL_TYPES.find((item) => item === formData.soilType);
    const waterSource = WATER_SOURCES.find(
      (item) => item === formData.waterSource,
    );
    const crop = state.crops.find((item) => item.id === formData.primaryCrop);
    const totalAcres = Number(formData.totalAcres);
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(totalAcres) ||
      totalAcres <= 0 ||
      !soilType ||
      !waterSource ||
      !crop
    ) {
      setValidationError(
        "Enter your actual farm area, soil and irrigation, and choose a crop before continuing.",
      );
      setStep(
        !Number.isFinite(totalAcres) || totalAcres <= 0
          ? 2
          : !soilType || !waterSource
            ? 3
            : 4,
      );
      return;
    }
    setValidationError("");
    const parcelId = existingParcel?.id || `PARCEL-${Date.now()}`;
    const parcel: LandParcel = {
      ...(existingParcel || {
        id: parcelId,
        surveyNumber: "",
        subDivision: "",
        pattaNumber: "",
        acres: 0,
        soilType,
        irrigationType: waterSource,
        locationName: "",
      }),
      surveyNumber: formData.surveyNumber,
      pattaNumber: formData.pattaNumber,
      acres: Number(formData.totalAcres),
      soilType,
      irrigationType: waterSource,
      locationName: formData.village
        ? `${formData.village} Field`
        : "Farm field",
    };
    AppStore.updateFarmerAndFarm(
      {
        fullName: formData.fullName,
        tamilFullName: formData.tamilFullName,
        village: formData.village,
        taluk: formData.taluk,
        district: formData.district,
        pincode: formData.pincode,
        totalLandAcres: Number(formData.totalAcres),
        primaryCrops: [crop.name],
        soilTypes: [soilType],
        waterSources: [waterSource],
      },
      {
        farmName: `${formData.fullName || "My"}'s Farm`,
        village: formData.village,
        district: formData.district,
        totalAreaAcres: Number(formData.totalAcres),
        parcels: [parcel],
      },
    );
    AppStore.setCurrentUser({
      ...state.currentUser,
      phone: formData.phone,
      name: formData.fullName,
      tamilName: formData.tamilFullName,
      role: "farmer",
    });

    // Create new season and navigate to crop planner or dashboard
    AppStore.createSeason({
      farmerId: state.farmer.id,
      cropId: crop.id,
      cropName: crop.name,
      tamilCropName: crop.tamilName,
      variety: formData.plannedVariety,
      seasonName: formData.seasonName,
      parcelId,
      parcelName: `${formData.village || "Farm field"} (${formData.totalAcres} Acres)`,
      areaAcres: Number(formData.totalAcres),
      startDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      sowingDate: new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 86400000)
        .toISOString()
        .split("T")[0],
      plannedHarvestDate: new Date(Date.now() + 130 * 86400000)
        .toISOString()
        .split("T")[0],
      stage: "Planning",
      targetYieldBags: Math.round(
        Number(formData.totalAcres) * crop.avgYieldBagsPerAcre,
      ),
      allocatedBudget: Number(formData.totalAcres) * crop.avgCostPerAcre,
    });
    triggerRefresh();
    navigate("/crop-planner");
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto py-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Progress Header */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "விவசாயி பதிவு & நில விபரம்"
                : "Farmer Onboarding & Farm Setup"}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "படி " + step + " / 4: "
                : "Step " + step + " of 4: "}
              {step === 1 &&
                (language === "ta"
                  ? "சுயவிவரம் & முகவரி"
                  : "Personal & Village Details")}
              {step === 2 &&
                (language === "ta"
                  ? "நிலம் & பட்டா விவரங்கள்"
                  : "Land Parcels & Patta")}
              {step === 3 &&
                (language === "ta"
                  ? "மண்வளம் & பாசன முறை"
                  : "Soil & Water Sources")}
              {step === 4 &&
                (language === "ta"
                  ? "பயிர் தேர்வு & புதிய பருவம்"
                  : "Season & Crop Planning")}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-sm shadow-sm">
            {step}/4
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Step indicator bar */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((s) => (
            <div
              key={s}
              className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${
                s <= step ? "bg-paddy-600" : "bg-sage-200"
              }`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Form Card */}
      <form
        onSubmit={
          step === 4
            ? handleSubmit
            : (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                handleNext();
              }
        }
        className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-6"
      >
        {validationError && (
          <p
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 bg-terracotta-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-terracotta-800"
          >
            {validationError}
          </p>
        )}

        {step === 1 && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "1. விவசாயி பெயர் & இருப்பிடம்"
                  : "1. Personal & Location Information"}
              </span>
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Full Name (English)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.fullName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  முழு பெயர் (தமிழ்)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.tamilFullName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, tamilFullName: e.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-tamil font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                  ? "தொலைபேசி எண் (விருப்பத்தேர்வு)"
                  : "Phone Number (Optional)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  autoComplete="tel"
                  value={formData.phone}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })
                  }
                  pattern="[0-9]{10}"
                  maxLength={10}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "கிராமம் (Village)" : "Village"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.village}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, village: e.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "மாவட்டம் (District)" : "District"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.district}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, district: e.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                  placeholder="Enter your district"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "2. நிலம் & பட்டா விபரங்கள்"
                  : "2. Land Parcel & Patta Information"}
              </span>
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "மொத்த சாகுபடி பரப்பு (ஏக்கர்)"
                    : "Total Land Area (Acres)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  min="0.1"
                  value={formData.totalAcres}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({
                      ...formData,
                      totalAcres: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-forest-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "சர்வே எண் (Survey Number)"
                    : "Survey Number"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.surveyNumber}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, surveyNumber: e.target.value })
                  }
                  placeholder="e.g. 142/3B"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "பட்டா எண் (Patta Number)"
                    : "Patta Number"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.pattaNumber}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, pattaNumber: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="p-3 bg-sage-100 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-3">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-8 h-8 text-paddy-600 shrink-0" />
                <p className="text-xs text-forest-800">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "அரசு நில ஆவண சரிபார்ப்பு (Tamil Nilam API) தானாக இணைக்கப்படும்."
                    : "Land records linkable with Tamil Nilam digital land repository."}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Droplets className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "3. மண்வளம் & பாசன முறை"
                  : "3. Soil Type & Water Sources"}
              </span>
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "மண் வகை (Soil Type)" : "Soil Type"}
                </label>
                <select
                  value={formData.soilType}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({
                      ...formData,
                      soilType:
                        SOIL_TYPES.find((item) => item === e.target.value) ||
                        "",
                    })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="">Select your soil type</option>
                  <option value="Clay Loam">Clay Loam (களிமண் படிவு)</option>
                  <option value="Alluvial">Alluvial Delta (வண்டல் மண்)</option>
                  <option value="Red Sandy Loam">
                    Red Sandy Loam (செம்மண்)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Black Cotton">
                    Black Cotton (கரிசல் மண்)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Sandy">Sandy (மணல் மண்)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "முக்கிய பாசன ஆதாரம்"
                    : "Primary Irrigation Source"}
                </label>
                <select
                  value={formData.waterSource}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({
                      ...formData,
                      waterSource:
                        WATER_SOURCES.find(
                          (item) => item === e.target.value,
                        ) || "",
                    })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="">Select your irrigation source</option>
                  <option value="Canal">
                    Cauvery Canal Turn (கால்வாய் பாசனம்)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Borewell">
                    Solar / Grid Borewell (ஆழ்துளை கிணறு)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Drip Irrigation">
                    Drip Irrigation (சொட்டு நீர்)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Open Well">
                    Open Well (திறந்தவெளி கிணறு)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Rainfed">Rainfed (மானாவாரி)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sprout className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "4. சாகுபடி பயிர் & புதிய பருவம்"
                  : "4. Initial Crop Season Setup"}
              </span>
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "பருவம் (Season)" : "Season"}
                </label>
                <select
                  value={formData.seasonName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({
                      ...formData,
                      seasonName: e.target.value as SeasonName,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="Samba (Main-Rabi)">
                    Samba (Main-Rabi / சம்பா)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Kuruvai (Summer-Kharif)">
                    Kuruvai (Summer-Kharif / குறுவை)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Thaladi (Late-Rabi)">
                    Thaladi (Late-Rabi / தாளடி)
                  </option>
                  <option value="Navarai (Summer)">
                    Navarai (Summer / நவரை)
                  </option>
                  <option value="General Season">General Season</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta"
                    ? "திட்டமிட்ட பயிர்"
                    : "Planned Crop"}
                </label>
                <select
                  value={formData.primaryCrop}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, primaryCrop: e.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                >
                  <option value="">Choose your crop</option>
                  {AppStore.getState().crops.map((crop) => (
                    <option key={crop.id} value={crop.id}>
                      {crop.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  {language === "ta" ? "விதை/ரக விவரம்" : "Variety (Optional)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.plannedVariety}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setFormData({ ...formData, plannedVariety: e.target.value })
                  }
                  placeholder="Enter a variety if known"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-harvest-400/40 space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "இணைக்கப்பட்ட V2A சுழற்சி துவக்கம்:"
                  : "Ready to Launch Connected V2A Lifecycle:"}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "பதிவு செய்தவுடன் உங்கள் நிலத்திற்கு மண் பரிசோதனை பரிந்துரைகள், விதை பாஸ்போர்ட் மற்றும் சாகுபடி வரவு-செலவு தானாக தயாராகும்."
                  : "Upon submission, your farm profile, soil advisory, seed passport generator, and cultivation budget calculator will be configured."}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Form Navigation Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-sage-200">
          {step > 1 ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleBack}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-ink-700 font-semibold text-xs hover:bg-sage-100 transition-colors"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{language === "ta" ? "முந்தைய படி" : "Back"}</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <div />
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all text-sm"
          >
            <span>
              {step === 4
                ? language === "ta"
                  ? "பதிவு செய்து தொடங்கு"
                  : "Finish & Start Crop Plan"
                : language === "ta"
                  ? "அடுத்த படி"
                  : "Next Step"}
            </span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\PaymentsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  CreditCard,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  AlertTriangle,
  Banknote,
  RefreshCw,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const PaymentsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId, triggerRefresh } =
    useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const financials = AppStore.calculateSeasonFinancials(activeSeasonId);
  const seasonPayments = state.payments.filter(
    (payment) => payment.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );
  const seasonSales = state.sales.filter(
    (sale) => sale.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );
  const totalBagsSold = seasonSales.reduce(
    (total, sale) => total + sale.bagsSold,
    0,
  );
  const totalGrossSales = seasonSales.reduce(
    (total, sale) => total + sale.grossAmount,
    0,
  );
  const pendingPayment = seasonPayments.find(
    (payment) => payment.status === "PENDING",
  );

  const [settlingId, setSettlingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSettle = (paymentId: string) => {
    setSettlingId(paymentId);
    setTimeout(() => {
      AppStore.recordPaymentSettlement(
        paymentId,
        `NEFT-IOB-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`,
      );
      setSettlingId(null);
      triggerRefresh();
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "வங்கி வரவு & தீர்வு கண்காணிப்பாளர்"
                : "Bank Settlements & Dues Ledger"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பணவரவு & நிலுவைத் தொகை"
              : "Payment Status & Pending Balances"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "பெறப்பட்ட வங்கி வரவு மற்றும் வரவேண்டிய நிலுவை தொகை பிரித்துக் காட்டப்படுகிறது"
              : "Demo ledger separates recorded receipts from pending receivables. No real bank transfer is initiated."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/finance")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === "ta" ? "முழு பண்ணை லாப-நஷ்டம்" : "Farm Financial P&L"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Critical Segregation Notice (Prompt Rule) */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border-l-4 border-harvest-500 text-xs text-forest-900 flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm">
        <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-harvest-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <p className="font-bold">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "மாதிரி பணவரவு உருவகப்படுத்துதல் (Simulation Only):"
              : "Demo only — no real bank transaction is performed."}
          </p>
          <p className="text-ink-600 leading-relaxed">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "வரவேண்டிய நிலுவைத் தொகைகள் வங்கியில் பெறப்படும் வரை பெறப்பட்ட வருமானமாக கணக்கிடப்படாது. இந்த உருவகப்படுத்துதல் உள்ளூர் கணக்கியல் பதிவை மட்டுமே மாற்றும்; உண்மையான வங்கிப் பரிவர்த்தனை நடைபெறாது."
              : "Simulate Bank Credit only changes the application's local payment ledger for hackathon evaluation and does NOT perform an actual bank transfer."}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Balance Summary Matrix */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {/* Received In Bank */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border-2 border-paddy-600 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
              Recorded as Received (Demo)
            </span>
            <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Recorded
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-paddy-700">
            ₹{financials.receivedPayments.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 font-medium">
            Local demo record only — no bank transfer occurs
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Pending Due (Separated) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border-2 border-harvest-400 shadow-agro space-y-1 bg-harvest-50/30">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900 uppercase tracking-wider">
              Pending Buyer Dues
            </span>
            <span className="bg-harvest-200 text-forest-900 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Receivable
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-terracotta-600">
            ₹{financials.pendingPayments.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-600 font-medium">
            {pendingPayment
              ? `Due ${pendingPayment.dueDate} from ${pendingPayment.buyerName}`
              : "No pending buyer dues"}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Total Invoiced Contract */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
            Contracted Net Value
          </span>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            ₹{financials.netProceedsInvoiced.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-semibold">
            {totalBagsSold} Bags sold
            {totalBagsSold > 0
              ? ` • Avg ₹${Math.round(totalGrossSales / totalBagsSold).toLocaleString()}/bag`
              : ""}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Payment Records Ledger */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "பணப்பரிவர்த்தனை பட்டியல்"
            : "Payment Transaction Ledger"}
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-sage-200">
          {seasonPayments.map((p) => {
            const isPending = p.status === "PENDING";

            return (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className="py-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div
                    className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                      isPending
                        ? "bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900"
                        : "bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Banknote className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900">
                        {p.buyerName}
                      </h4>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-cream-200 text-ink-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {p.paymentType}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
                      Method: {p.method} • Due: {p.dueDate}{" "}
                      {p.referenceNo ? `• Ref: ${p.referenceNo}` : ""}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between md:justify-end gap-6 text-right">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">
                      Amount
                    </span>
                    <p
                      className={`font-display font-black text-xl ${isPending ? "text-terracotta-600" : "text-paddy-700"}`}
                    >
                      ₹{p.amount.toLocaleString()}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {isPending ? (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleSettle(p.id)}
                      disabled={settlingId === p.id}
                      className="px-4 py-2 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      {settlingId === p.id ? (
                        <>
                          <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                          <span>Simulating credit...</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-harvest-400" />
                          <span>
                            {language === "ta"
                              ? "வங்கி வரவு உருவகப்படுத்து"
                              : "Simulate Bank Credit"}
                          </span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
                      Received (demo)
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";

  User,

export const ProfilePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, setLanguage, navigate, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const farmer = state.farmer;
  const isDemoAccount = state.currentUser.id === "USER-VIJAY-DEMO";

  const handleResetDemo = () => {
    if (
      window.confirm(
        language === "ta"
          ? "அனைத்து மாதிரி தகவல்களையும் மீட்டமைக்கவா?"
          : "Reset entire platform to factory demo state?",

      AppStore.resetToDemo();
      triggerRefresh();
      navigate("/dashboard");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">

            {isDemoAccount
              ? `Demo account: ${state.currentUser.name} • ${state.currentUser.phone} • Farmer`
              : `Signed in as: ${state.currentUser.name} • ${state.currentUser.phone}`}
          </p>
        </div>

        {isDemoAccount && (
          <button
            onClick={handleResetDemo}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 text-ink-700 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl border border-sage-300 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-terracotta-600" />

                : "Reset Demo Data"}

          </button>

import React from "react";
import {
  Receipt,
  Scale,
  ShieldCheck,
  Download,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";

export const SalesPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, activeSeasonId } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const sale = state.sales.find((item) => item.seasonId === activeSeasonId);
  const harvest = state.harvestRecords.find(
    (item) => item.seasonId === activeSeasonId,
  );

  if (!sale) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-extrabold text-forest-900">
            No sale recorded for this season
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-600">
            Review buyer offers and accept one to create a demo sale invoice.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/offers")}
            className="rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-harvest-300"
          >
            Review buyer offers
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/harvest")}
            className="rounded-xl border border-sage-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-forest-900"
          >
            Go to harvest
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Receipt className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta" ? "டெமோ விற்பனை ரசீது" : "Demo Sale Record"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விற்பனை விவரம் & எடை சுருக்கம்"
              : "Sale Details & Weighment Summary"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            Demo record only — not a certified weighbridge slip, tax invoice, or
            official purchase deed.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/payments")}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === "ta"
            ? "வங்கி வரவு நிலை காண்க"
            : "Payment Status & Bank Dues"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Demo Sale Record Card */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border-2 border-paddy-600/40 shadow-agro space-y-6">
        {/* Invoice Top Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-sage-200">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold bg-cream-200 text-forest-900 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">
                {sale.saleInvoiceNumber}
              </span>
              <span className="bg-harvest-200 text-forest-900 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                {sale.status}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-2">
              {sale.buyerName}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
              Procurement Unit: {sale.buyerOrg} • Sale Date: {sale.saleDate}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase block">
              Net Invoiced Value
            </span>
            <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
              ₹{sale.netSaleProceeds.toLocaleString()}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-paddy-700 font-semibold">
              Gross: ₹{sale.grossAmount.toLocaleString()} ({sale.bagsSold} Bags)
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Digital Weighment Slip Section (Requirement H) */}
        <div className="p-5 bg-cream-100 rounded-3xl border border-sage-300 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Scale className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-700" />
              <h4 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900">
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "டெமோ எடை விவரம்"
                  : "Demo Weighment Summary"}
              </h4>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-ink-600">
              Demo reference: {sale.weighmentSlipNumber}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-center text-xs">
            <div className="p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-ink-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                Bag Count
              </span>
              <p className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                {sale.bagsSold} bags
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-ink-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                Bag Weight
              </span>
              <p className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                {harvest?.bagWeightKg ?? 75} kg
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-ink-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                Calculated Produce Weight
              </span>
              <p className="font-display font-black text-sm text-paddy-700 mt-0.5">
                {(
                  sale.bagsSold * (harvest?.bagWeightKg ?? 75)
                ).toLocaleString()}{" "}
                kg
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-200">
              <span className="text-ink-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                Recorded Moisture
              </span>
              <p className="font-bold text-sm text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                {harvest ? `${harvest.moistureContentPct}%` : "Not recorded"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Action Link to Payments */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-3 border-t border-sage-200">
          <button
            onClick={() => window.print()}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-cream-100 hover:bg-cream-200 text-forest-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl border border-sage-300"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "அச்சிடு / PDF ஆக சேமி"
                : "Print / Save as PDF"}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("/payments")}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
          >
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "பணவரவு மற்றும் நிலுவை பார்க்க"
                : "Inspect Bank Payments & Dues"}
            </span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Landmark, ShieldCheck, CheckSquare, ExternalLink, FileText, ArrowRight, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigation } from '../context/NavigationContext';
import { AppStore } from '../services/store';

export const SchemesPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [checkedDocs, setCheckedDocs] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    'doc-aadhaar': true,
    'doc-patta': true,
    'doc-bank': true,
  });

  const toggleDoc = (id: string) => {
    setCheckedDocs({ ...checkedDocs, [id]: !checkedDocs[id] });
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Landmark className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'அரசு மானியங்கள் & திட்டங்கள்' : 'Government Subsidies & Farmer Welfare Schemes'}</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === 'ta' ? 'வேளாண் நலத்திட்டங்கள் & ஆவண சரிபார்ப்பு பட்டியல்' : 'Agri Schemes & Document Readiness Checklist'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            Direct DBT subsidies from Tamil Nadu Department of Agriculture and Ministry of Agriculture & Farmers Welfare
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/profile')}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro"
        >
          {language === 'ta' ? 'விவசாயி KYC சுயவிவரம்' : 'Farmer KYC Profile'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Mandatory Regulatory Transparency Notice (Phase 5) */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border-l-4 border-harvest-500 text-xs text-forest-900 space-y-1 shadow-sm">
        <p className="font-bold">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'அரசு திட்ட விண்ணப்ப வெளிப்படைத்தன்மை:' : 'Official Scheme Application Notice:'}
        </p>
        <p className="text-ink-600">
          {language === 'ta'
            ? 'V2A தளம் அதிகாரப்பூர்வ அரசு ஆவண சரிபார்ப்பை மட்டுமே வழங்குகிறது. இறுதி விண்ணப்பங்கள் உழவன் செயலி அல்லது மத்திய அரசு வலைத்தளத்தில் மட்டுமே சமர்ப்பிக்கப்பட வேண்டும்.'
            : 'V2A prepares your eligibility credentials and document package. Formal DBT disbursements require authenticated submission via the official government portal links provided.'}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Universal Document Readiness Checklist (Requirement I) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-forest-900">
          {language === 'ta' ? 'அரசு மானியங்களுக்கான கட்டாய ஆவண சரிபார்ப்பு' : 'Universal Farmer Scheme Document Checklist'}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
          Check off your physical documents to ensure seamless verification at the block agriculture depot:
        </p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
          {[
            { id: 'doc-aadhaar', title: 'Aadhaar Card (Aadhaar Seeded)', status: 'Verified ✓' },
            { id: 'doc-patta', title: 'Land Patta & Chitta (e-Adangal)', status: 'Verified ✓' },
            { id: 'doc-bank', title: 'Nationalized Bank Passbook Copy', status: 'Linked ✓' },
            { id: 'doc-ration', title: 'Family Smart Ration Card', status: 'Required' },
          ].map((doc) => (
            <div
              key={doc.id}
              onClick={() => toggleDoc(doc.id)}
              className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border cursor-pointer transition-all flex items-center justify-between ${
                checkedDocs[doc.id]
                  ? 'bg-paddy-50 border-paddy-500 text-forest-900'
                  : 'bg-white border-sage-300 text-ink-700'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <CheckSquare className={`w-5 h-5 ${checkedDocs[doc.id] ? 'text-paddy-700' : 'text-ink-400'}`} />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold">{doc.title}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cream-200">
                {checkedDocs[doc.id] ? 'Ready' : 'Pending'}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Schemes Directory */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {state.schemes.map((scheme) => (
          <div
            key={scheme.id}
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4"
          >
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-sage-200">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-paddy-700 bg-paddy-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {scheme.sponsoredBy}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-cream-200 text-ink-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                    {scheme.code}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                  {language === 'ta' ? scheme.tamilName : scheme.name}
                </h3>
              </div>

              <a
                href={scheme.applicationPortalUrl}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noreferrer"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro hover:bg-forest-900 transition-colors shrink-0"
              >
                <span>{language === 'ta' ? 'அதிகாரப்பூர்வ தளம்' : 'Official Portal'}</span>
                <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-harvest-400" />
              </a>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs">
              <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1">
                <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">Subsidy Benefit</span>
                <p className="font-bold text-forest-900">{scheme.subsidyAmountOrBenefit}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1">
                <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">Target Beneficiary</span>
                <p className="font-semibold text-forest-900">{scheme.targetGroup}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1">
                <span className="text-ink-500 font-bold block uppercase text-[10px]">Application Window</span>
                <p className="font-semibold text-paddy-700">{scheme.deadlineText}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2 text-xs">
              <span className="font-bold text-forest-900 block mb-1">Required Document Checklist:</span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {scheme.requiredDocuments.map((doc, i) => (
                  <span
                    key={i}
                    className="bg-cream-100 text-forest-900 font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-sage-300 flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    ✓ {doc}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
