================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\SeedPassportPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  QrCode,
  ShieldCheck,
  Search,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Camera,
  Save,
  Flag,
  Sparkles,
  Info,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { SeedBatch } from "../types";

export const SeedPassportPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();

  const [batchInput, setBatchInput] = useState("TN-TSSCA-2025-P90412");
  const [selectedBatch, setSelectedBatch] = useState<SeedBatch | null>(
    state.seedBatches[0],
  );
  const selectedBatchIsExpired = selectedBatch
    ? new Date(`${selectedBatch.expiryDate}T23:59:59`).getTime() < Date.now()
    : false;
  const [isScanning, setIsScanning] = useState(false);
  const [showReportModal, setShowReportModal] = useState(false);
  const [reportText, setReportText] = useState("");
  const [savedSuccess, setSavedSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [scanError, setScanError] = useState("");
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const scanControlsRef = useRef<{ stop: () => void } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => scanControlsRef.current?.stop(), []);

  const stopCamera = () => {
    scanControlsRef.current?.stop();
    scanControlsRef.current = null;
    setIsScanning(false);
  };

  const handleVerifyBatch = (code: string) => {
    const normalizedCode = code.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!normalizedCode) return;
    const currentBatches = AppStore.getState().seedBatches;
    const found = currentBatches.find(
      (batch) =>
        batch.batchNumber.toLowerCase() === normalizedCode ||
        batch.qrPayload.toLowerCase() === normalizedCode ||
        normalizedCode.includes(batch.batchNumber.toLowerCase()),
    );
    if (found) {
      setSelectedBatch(found);
      setScanError("");
    } else {
      setSelectedBatch(null);
      setScanError(
        "Code was read, but it does not match a batch in this local demo. No official seed verification service is connected.",
      );
    }
  };

  const startCameraScan = async () => {
    if (!videoRef.current) return;
    setScanError("");
    setIsScanning(true);
    try {
      const { BrowserQRCodeReader } = await import("@zxing/browser");
      const reader = new BrowserQRCodeReader();
      scanControlsRef.current = await reader.decodeFromVideoDevice(
        undefined,
        videoRef.current,
        (result) => {
          if (!result) return;
          const scannedCode = result.getText();
          setBatchInput(scannedCode);
          handleVerifyBatch(scannedCode);
          stopCamera();
        },
      );
    } catch {
      stopCamera();
      setScanError(
        "Camera access failed. Allow camera access on HTTPS, or use a sample batch/manual code.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleSaveToFarm = () => {
    if (!selectedBatch) return;
    AppStore.addSeedBatch(selectedBatch);
    setSavedSuccess(true);
    triggerRefresh();
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSavedSuccess(false);
      navigate("/crop-journey");
    }, 1200);
  };

  const handleReportIssue = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setShowReportModal(false);
    setReportText("");
    alert(
      language === "ta"
        ? "விதை குறைபாடு புகார் விதை ஆய்வு அதிகாரிகளுக்கு சமர்ப்பிக்கப்பட்டது."
        : "Seed discrepancy report logged for Department of Seed Inspection.",
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "சான்றளிக்கப்பட்ட விதை பாஸ்போர்ட்"
                : "Seed QR Lookup Demo"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "விதை தொகுதி விவரங்கள்"
              : "Seed Batch Details & Sample Lookup"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "தமிழ்நாடு விதை சான்றளிப்பு முகமை (TSSCA) மற்றும் TNAU சான்றிதழ் ஆய்வு முறை"
              : "Scans QR codes and matches them against sample batches stored in this browser. No official seed database is connected."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-600 bg-cream-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-sage-300">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "ஆய்வு நிலை: மாதிரி சேவை"
              : "Mock Verification Boundary Active"}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mandatory Regulatory Transparency Notice */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border-l-4 border-harvest-500 text-xs text-forest-900 flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm">
        <Info className="w-5 h-5 text-harvest-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <p className="font-bold">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "வெளிப்படைத்தன்மை குறிப்பு (Mock Verification Protocol):"
              : "Regulatory Integration Boundary Notice:"}
          </p>
          <p className="text-ink-600 leading-relaxed">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "இந்த மாதிரி அமைப்பில் உள்ள சான்றிதழ் தகவல்கள் மாதிரி செயல்முறையை விளக்குவதற்காக மட்டுமே. உண்மையான அரசு API (TSSCA/NSCL) இணைக்கப்படும் வரை, இது போலி சான்றிதழை உண்மை என உரிமை கொண்டாடாது."
              : "Camera scanning reads real QR content. A match only confirms the sample exists in this browser; it is not official certificate verification."}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* QR Scanner & Batch Code Entry Matrix */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left Column: Camera QR Scanner */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-5">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-base text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <QrCode className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "விதை பை QR குறியீடு ஸ்கேன்"
                : "Scan Seed Bag QR Code"}
            </span>
          </h3>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="relative aspect-video max-w-[420px] mx-auto overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-forest-950 border-4 border-forest-800 shadow-inner">
              <video
                ref={videoRef}
                autoPlay
                muted
                playsInline
                className={`h-full w-full object-cover ${isScanning ? "" : "hidden"}`}
              />
              {!isScanning && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center">
                  <Camera className="h-10 w-10 text-harvest-400" />
                  <p className="px-4 text-xs text-sage-200">
                    Allow camera permission, then point at a QR code.
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-center gap-2">
              {isScanning ? (
                <button
                  onClick={stopCamera}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-terracotta-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white"
                >
                  Stop Camera
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  onClick={startCameraScan}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-forest-800 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-harvest-300"
                >
                  Open Camera & Scan QR
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            {scanError && (
              <p
                role="alert"
                className="rounded-xl bg-terracotta-50 p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-terracotta-800"
              >
                {scanError}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Pre-Set Bag QRs to Test */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-500">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "மாதிரி விதை பைகள் (தட்டவும்):"
                : "Sample Demo Seed Bags (Tap to look up):"}
            </p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setBatchInput("TN-TSSCA-2025-P90412");
                  handleVerifyBatch("TN-TSSCA-2025-P90412");
                }}
                className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl text-left bg-cream-50 hover:bg-forest-50 border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-forest-900 flex items-center justify-between"
              >
                <span>🌾 CR 1009 Sub 1 (Demo record)</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                  94% Germ
                </span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setBatchInput("TNAU-VBN-2026-B811");
                  handleVerifyBatch("TNAU-VBN-2026-B811");
                }}
                className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl text-left bg-cream-50 hover:bg-forest-50 border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-forest-900 flex items-center justify-between"
              >
                <span>🌱 VBN 8 Blackgram (Demo record)</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                  91% Germ
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Manual Entry */}
          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-sage-200 space-y-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "அல்லது பேட்ச் எண்ணை உள்ளிடுக:"
                : "Or enter Batch Tag Number manually:"}
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={batchInput}
                onChange={(e) => setBatchInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. TN-TSSCA-2025-P90412"
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-forest-900"
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => handleVerifyBatch(batchInput)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm"
              >
                Verify
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right 2 Cols: The Digital Seed Passport Certificate Card */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          {selectedBatch ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border-2 border-paddy-600/40 shadow-agro space-y-6 relative overflow-hidden">
              {/* Certificate Ribbon */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-sage-200">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 text-xs font-extrabold px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-700" />
                      {selectedBatch.verificationStatus === "VERIFIED_OFFICIAL"
                        ? language === "ta"
                          ? "அரசு விதை சான்றளிப்பு சரிபார்க்கப்பட்டது"
                          : "Official Batch Verified"
                        : "Demo Record · Not Officially Verified"}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-ink-500">
                      {selectedBatch.certNumber}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-2">
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? selectedBatch.tamilVariety
                      : selectedBatch.variety}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
                    {selectedBatch.cropName} • {selectedBatch.manufacturer} (
                    {selectedBatch.producerType})
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="shrink-0 text-right">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 block uppercase">
                    Batch Code
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-sm font-black text-forest-900 bg-cream-200 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">
                    {selectedBatch.batchNumber}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Lab Quality Test Metrics */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 text-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                    Germination Rate
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-paddy-700 mt-1">
                    {selectedBatch.germinationRatePct}%
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[9px] text-paddy-800 font-bold mt-0.5">
                    Min Standard: 80%
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 text-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                    Physical Purity
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                    {selectedBatch.physicalPurityPct}%
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[9px] text-forest-700 font-bold mt-0.5">
                    Weed seeds: 0.0%
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 text-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                    Genetic Purity
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                    {selectedBatch.geneticPurityPct}%
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[9px] text-forest-700 font-bold mt-0.5">
                    Breeder Pedigree
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3.5 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 text-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                    Bag Weight / MRP
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-display font-black text-2xl text-forest-900 mt-1">
                    {selectedBatch.packageWeightKg} kg
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-terracotta-700 font-bold mt-0.5">
                    MRP: ₹{selectedBatch.mrp}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Chemical Treatment & Validity Dates */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
                <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                  <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 block">
                    Lab Testing & Expiry Dates:
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                    Tested: {selectedBatch.testingDate} •{" "}
                    {selectedBatchIsExpired ? "Expired on" : "Expires on"}:{" "}
                    {selectedBatch.expiryDate}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 bg-cream-50 rounded-xl border border-sage-200">
                  <span className="font-bold text-ink-600 block">
                    Fungicide Seed Treatment:
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-forest-900 mt-0.5">
                    {selectedBatch.treatedWithPesticide
                      ? "Pre-treated with Carbendazim / Thiram (Safe for sowing)"
                      : "Untreated Organic Certified Seeds"}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Action Buttons: Save to Farm vs Report Issue */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-sage-200">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={handleSaveToFarm}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro transition-all"
                  >
                    <Save className="w-4 h-4 text-harvest-400" />
                    <span>
                      {language === "ta"
                        ? "எனது பண்ணையில் சேமிக்கவும்"
                        : "Save Seed to Farmer's Farm"}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  {savedSuccess && (
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 flex items-center gap-1 animate-fadeIn">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      {language === "ta"
                        ? "சேமிக்கப்பட்டது!"
                        : "Saved to Active Season!"}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowReportModal(true)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300 text-terracotta-700 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-terracotta-50 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Flag className="w-4 h-4 text-terracotta-600" />
                  <span>
                    {language === "ta"
                      ? "குறைபாடு புகார் செய்"
                      : "Report Issue / Defect"}
                  </span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-12 text-center border border-sage-300 shadow-agro">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-ink-600">
                Scan or select a seed bag to view the passport certificate.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Report Issue Modal */}
      {showReportModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-terracotta-600" />
              <span>
                {language === "ta"
                  ? "விதை தரம் குறித்த புகார் பதிவு"
                  : "Report Seed Discrepancy"}
              </span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "முளைப்புத்திறன் குறைவாக இருந்தாலோ அல்லது போலி விதையாக சந்தேகம் இருந்தாலோ ஆய்வுத்துறைக்கு தகவல் அனுப்பலாம்."
                : "Describe a poor germination result or damaged tag. This demo stores the report locally; it does not contact an inspection authority."}
            </p>

            <form onSubmit={handleReportIssue} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Issue Description
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={reportText}
                  onChange={(e) => setReportText(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="e.g. Germination rate observed below 60% after 6 days in nursery bed..."
                  className="w-full p-2.5 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowReportModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-terracotta-600 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-sm"
                >
                  Save Demo Report
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\SoilHealthPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plus,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Droplets,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigation } from "../context/NavigationContext";
import { AppStore } from "../services/store";
import { compressImageToDataUrl } from "../utils/images";

export const SoilHealthPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, navigate, triggerRefresh } = useNavigation();
  const state = AppStore.getState();
  const soilReport = state.soilReports[0];

  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [phInput, setPhInput] = useState("7.2");
  const [nInput, setNInput] = useState("250");
  const [pInput, setPInput] = useState("21");
  const [kInput, setKInput] = useState("290");
  const [soilPhotoDataUrl, setSoilPhotoDataUrl] = useState("");
  const [soilPhotoName, setSoilPhotoName] = useState("");
  const [photoError, setPhotoError] = useState("");

  const handleSoilPhotoChange = async (file?: File) => {
    if (!file) return;
    setPhotoError("");
    try {
      setSoilPhotoDataUrl(await compressImageToDataUrl(file));
      setSoilPhotoName(file.name);
    } catch (error) {
      setPhotoError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load this image.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleSaveReport = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const currentReport = AppStore.getState().soilReports[0];
    if (!currentReport) return;

    const ph = Number(phInput);
    const nitrogen = Number(nInput);
    const phosphorus = Number(pInput);
    const potassium = Number(kInput);
    if (
      ![ph, nitrogen, phosphorus, potassium].every(Number.isFinite) ||
      ph < 0 ||
      ph > 14
    )
      return;

    const statusFor = (
      value: number,
      lowLimit: number,
      mediumLimit: number,
    ): "Low" | "Medium" | "High" =>
      value < lowLimit ? "Low" : value < mediumLimit ? "Medium" : "High";
    const phDescription =
      ph < 6.5
        ? "Acidic"
        : ph <= 7.5
          ? "Neutral"
          : ph <= 8.5
            ? "Slightly Alkaline"
            : "Alkaline";

    AppStore.addSoilReport({
      ...currentReport,
      farmId: state.farm.id,
      parcelId: state.farm.parcels[0]?.id ?? currentReport.parcelId,
      sampleDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      labName: "Farmer-entered record (unverified)",
      photoDataUrl: soilPhotoDataUrl || undefined,
      ph,
      phDescription,
      nitrogenValueKgPerHa: nitrogen,
      nitrogenStatus: statusFor(nitrogen, 280, 560),
      phosphorusValueKgPerHa: phosphorus,
      phosphorusStatus: statusFor(phosphorus, 10, 25),
      potassiumValueKgPerHa: potassium,
      potassiumStatus: statusFor(potassium, 110, 280),
    });
    setShowAddModal(false);
    triggerRefresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-12">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-paddy-600" />
            <span>
              {language === "ta"
                ? "டெமோ மண்வள பதிவு"
                : "Soil Health Demo Record"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "மண்வள அறிக்கை & ஊட்டச்சத்து மேலாண்மை"
              : "Sample Soil Nutrient Overview"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-500">
            {soilReport.labName} • Recorded on {soilReport.sampleDate}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
            Photo is visual evidence only. pH and N/P/K values must come from a
            test kit or lab; this app does not measure soil chemistry from a
            picture.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-forest-800 hover:bg-forest-900 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-agro flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>
            {language === "ta" ? "புதிய அறிக்கை சேர்க்க" : "Log New Soil Test"}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {soilReport.photoDataUrl && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300 bg-white p-4">
          <img
            src={soilReport.photoDataUrl}
            alt="Saved soil sample photo"
            className="h-24 w-24 rounded-xl border border-sage-200 object-cover"
          />
          <div>
            <p className="font-bold text-forest-900">
              Soil sample photo attached
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
              Stored in this browser’s demo record; not lab analysis.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Soil Gauge Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* pH Card */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
              Soil pH Index
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {soilReport.phDescription}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {soilReport.ph}
          </p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="bg-paddy-600 h-full rounded-full"
              style={{ width: "71%" }}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "நடுநிலை கார அமிலத்தன்மை (பயிருக்கு சிறந்தது)"
              : "Optimum range (6.5 - 7.5) for nutrient uptake"}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Nitrogen (N) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
              Available Nitrogen (N)
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {soilReport.nitrogenStatus}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {soilReport.nitrogenValueKgPerHa}{" "}
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-500">kg/ha</span>
          </p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="bg-harvest-500 h-full rounded-full"
              style={{ width: "58%" }}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "மிதமான தழைச்சத்து. 3 தவணைகளில் யூரியா இடவும்."
              : "Medium rating. Split nitrogen application advised."}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Phosphorus (P) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
              Phosphorus (P₂O₅)
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-harvest-100 text-forest-900 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {soilReport.phosphorusStatus}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {soilReport.phosphorusValueKgPerHa}{" "}
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-500">kg/ha</span>
          </p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="bg-paddy-500 h-full rounded-full"
              style={{ width: "62%" }}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "சாம்பல் சத்து / மணிச்சத்து நல்ல நிலையில் உள்ளது."
              : "Adequate root development support."}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Potassium (K) */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-500 uppercase">
              Potassium (K₂O)
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {soilReport.potassiumStatus}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="font-display font-black text-3xl text-forest-900">
            {soilReport.potassiumValueKgPerHa}{" "}
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-500">kg/ha</span>
          </p>
          <div className="w-full bg-sage-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="bg-paddy-600 h-full rounded-full"
              style={{ width: "85%" }}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-600">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "அதிக சாம்பல் சத்து (நோய் எதிர்ப்பு சக்தி அதிகம்)"
              : "High reserve, excellent lodging resistance"}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Organic Carbon and Micronutrient Deficiencies */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* Organic Carbon Card */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "கரிம வளம் & நுண்ணுயிர்கள்"
              : "Organic Carbon & Biological Life"}
          </h3>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6 p-4 bg-cream-100 rounded-2xl border border-sage-300">
            <div className="text-center shrink-0">
              <p className="font-display font-black text-4xl text-forest-900">
                {soilReport.organicCarbonPct}%
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-paddy-700">
                Medium Organic C
              </p>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-700 leading-relaxed">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "டெல்டா வண்டல் மண்ணில் அங்கக கரிமத்தை 0.8% வரை உயர்த்த பசுந்தாள் உரம் (தக்கைப்பூண்டு / சணப்பை) அல்லது ஏக்கருக்கு 2 டன் மக்கிய தொழுவுரம் இடவும்."
                : "Target organic carbon is >0.75%. Green manuring with Sesbania (Daincha) or 2 tonnes composted FYM recommended before puddle ploughing."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-sage-50 rounded-2xl border border-sage-200 space-y-1">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
              Electrical Conductivity (EC): {soilReport.electricalConductivity}{" "}
              dS/m
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-600">
              Sample record only; confirm salinity and nutrient levels with a
              test kit or lab.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Micronutrients */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
            {language === "ta"
              ? "நுண் ஊட்டச்சத்துக்கள் (Micronutrients)"
              : "Micronutrient Status & Deficiencies"}
          </h3>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-2xl bg-terracotta-50 border border-terracotta-200">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-terracotta-600" />
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                    Zinc (Zn)
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-terracotta-700 font-semibold">
                    Deficient (பற்றாக்குறை)
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-white text-terracotta-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-xl border border-terracotta-300">
                Action: Apply Zinc Sulphate 10kg/acre
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-2xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-200">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                    Iron (Fe)
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold">
                    Sufficient (போதுமானது)
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-xl">
                Normal
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-2xl bg-cream-50 border border-sage-200">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-paddy-600" />
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-forest-900">
                    Boron (B)
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-paddy-700 font-semibold">
                    Sufficient (போதுமானது)
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-paddy-100 text-paddy-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-xl">
                Normal
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Official Agronomic Recommendations (Requirement A) */}
      <div className="bg-cream-100 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-sage-300 shadow-agro space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
          {language === "ta"
            ? "அரசு ஆய்வக பரிந்துரைகள்"
            : "Sample Soil Action Suggestions · Confirm with an Expert"}
        </h3>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {(language === "ta"
            ? soilReport.tamilRecommendations
            : soilReport.recommendations
          ).map((rec, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage-300"
            >
              <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-xs shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                {i + 1}
              </span>
              <p className="text-xs text-forest-900 font-semibold leading-relaxed">
                {rec}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Add New Soil Test Modal */}
      {showAddModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-ink-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-sage-300 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-forest-900">
              {language === "ta"
                ? "புதிய மண் பரிசோதனை பதிவு"
                : "Log New Soil Test Entry"}
            </h3>
            <form onSubmit={handleSaveReport} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700">
                  Soil photo (camera or upload)
                </label>
                <label className="block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-sage-300 bg-cream-50 p-3 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-forest-900 hover:bg-cream-100">
                  {soilPhotoName || "Take or choose a photo of the soil sample"}
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    capture="environment"
                    className="sr-only"
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      void handleSoilPhotoChange(event.target.files?.[0])
                    }
                  />
                </label>
                {photoError && (
                  <p role="alert" className="mt-1 text-xs text-terracotta-700">
                    {photoError}
                  </p>
                )}
                {soilPhotoDataUrl && (
                  <img
                    src={soilPhotoDataUrl}
                    alt="Soil sample photo attached to this local soil record"
                    className="mt-2 max-h-40 w-full rounded-xl border border-sage-300 object-contain bg-cream-50"
                  />
                )}
                <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-ink-500">
                  Photo is saved with this browser’s demo record. It cannot
                  measure pH or N/P/K; enter readings from a test kit or lab.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                  Soil pH
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  max="14"
                  step="0.1"
                  value={phInput}
                  required
                  onChange={(e) => setPhInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    N (kg/ha)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    value={nInput}
                    required
                    onChange={(e) => setNInput(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    P (kg/ha)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    value={pInput}
                    required
                    onChange={(e) => setPInput(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1">
                    K (kg/ha)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    value={kInput}
                    required
                    onChange={(e) => setKInput(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-cream-50 border border-sage-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-sage-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowAddModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sage-300 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-600"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-forest-800 text-harvest-300 font-bold text-xs shadow-sm"
                >
                  Save Soil Card
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// Comprehensive Mock State and Data Service for VITHAI TO ARUVADAI (V2A)
import {
  FarmerProfile,
  Farm,
  CropSeason,
  Crop,
  SoilReport,
  SeedBatch,
  InputProduct,
  InputPurchase,
  FarmTask,
  IrrigationRecord,
  CropObservation,
  ExpertRequest,
  MachineryListing,
  LabourBooking,
  HarvestRecord,
  StorageListing,
  TransportListing,
  Buyer,
  ProduceListing,
  BuyerOffer,
  SaleRecord,
  Payment,
  Expense,
  GovernmentScheme,
  JourneyEvent,
  Language,
  User,
} from "../types";
import { supabase } from "./supabase";

const DEMO_STORAGE_KEY = "v2a_demo_state";
const STARTER_SEASON_NOTE =
  "Starter template only. Choose your crop and enter your actual field details before using this plan.";

export type CloudSyncStatus =
  | "local"
  | "syncing"
  | "synced"
  | "error"
  | "offline";


export interface AppState {
  language: Language;
  activeSeasonId: string;
  currentUser: User;
  farmer: FarmerProfile;
  farm: Farm;
  crops: Crop[];
  seasons: CropSeason[];
  soilReports: SoilReport[];
  seedBatches: SeedBatch[];
  inputProducts: InputProduct[];
  inputPurchases: InputPurchase[];
  tasks: FarmTask[];
  irrigations: IrrigationRecord[];
  cropObservations: CropObservation[];
  expertRequests: ExpertRequest[];
  machineryListings: MachineryListing[];
  labourBookings: LabourBooking[];
  harvestRecords: HarvestRecord[];
  storageListings: StorageListing[];
  transportListings: TransportListing[];
  buyers: Buyer[];
  produceListings: ProduceListing[];
  buyerOffers: BuyerOffer[];
  sales: SaleRecord[];
  payments: Payment[];
  expenses: Expense[];
  schemes: GovernmentScheme[];
  journeyEvents: JourneyEvent[];
  lastSyncTime: string;
}

const requiredStateArrays: (keyof AppState)[] = [
  "crops",
  "seasons",
  "soilReports",
  "seedBatches",
  "inputProducts",
  "inputPurchases",
  "tasks",
  "irrigations",
  "cropObservations",
  "expertRequests",
  "machineryListings",
  "labourBookings",
  "harvestRecords",
  "storageListings",
  "transportListings",
  "buyers",
  "produceListings",
  "buyerOffers",
  "sales",
  "payments",
  "expenses",
  "schemes",
  "journeyEvents",
];

function isAppState(value: unknown): value is AppState {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  const state = value as AppState;
  return (
    typeof state.activeSeasonId === "string" &&
    typeof state.currentUser === "object" &&
    state.currentUser !== null &&
    typeof state.farmer === "object" &&
    state.farmer !== null &&
    typeof state.farm === "object" &&
    state.farm !== null &&
    requiredStateArrays.every((key) => Array.isArray(state[key]))
  );
}

export class AppStore {
  private static inMemoryState: AppState | null = null;
  private static cloudUserId: string | null = null;
  private static cloudSaveTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private static cloudSaveQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
  private static saveGeneration = 0;
  private static syncStatus: CloudSyncStatus = "local";
  private static syncListeners = new Set<(status: CloudSyncStatus) => void>();

  private static createDefaultState(): AppState {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState)) as AppState;
  }

  private static getUserStorageKey(userId: string): string {
    return `v2a_user_${userId}_state`;
  }

  private static getStorageKey(): string {
    return this.cloudUserId
      ? this.getUserStorageKey(this.cloudUserId)
      : DEMO_STORAGE_KEY;
  }

  private static readUserState(userId: string): AppState | null {
    const state = this.readStoredState(this.getUserStorageKey(userId));
    if (!state) return null;
    return state.currentUser.id === userId || state.farmer.userId === userId
      ? state
      : null;
  }

  private static isLegacyDemoSeed(state: AppState): boolean {
    return state.farmer.id === "FARMER-TN-0421" && state.farm.id === "FARM-DELTA-01";
  }

  private static getSafeUserState(user: User, state: AppState | null): AppState {
    return state && !this.isLegacyDemoSeed(state)
      ? state
      : this.createNewUserInitialState(user);
  }

  private static createNewUserInitialState(user: User): AppState {
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const seasonId = `CR-${currentYear}-SAMBA-01`;
    const cleanId = user.id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 8).toUpperCase() || "NEW";
    const farmerId = `FARMER-${cleanId}`;
    const farmId = `FARM-${cleanId}`;
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
    const harvestDate = new Date(Date.now() + 120 * 86400000)
      .toISOString()
      .split("T")[0];

    const displayName = user.name && user.name.trim() ? user.name.trim() : "Farmer";
    const displayTamilName =
      user.tamilName && user.tamilName.trim()
        ? user.tamilName.trim()
        : displayName === "Farmer"
          ? "விவசாயி"
          : displayName;

    return {
      language: "en",
      activeSeasonId: seasonId,
      currentUser: {
        id: user.id,
        phone: user.phone || "",
        name: displayName,
        tamilName: displayTamilName,
        role: "farmer",
      },
      farmer: {
        id: farmerId,
        userId: user.id,
        fullName: displayName,
        tamilFullName: displayTamilName,
        fatherName: "",
        village: "",
        taluk: "",
        district: "",
        state: "",
        pincode: "",
        kisanCardNumber: "",
        pmKisanBeneficiaryId: "",
        totalLandAcres: 0,
        primaryCrops: [],
        soilTypes: [],
        waterSources: [],
        aadhaarVerified: false,
        memberSince: today,
      },
      farm: {
        id: farmId,
        farmerId,
        farmName: `${displayName}'s Farm`,
        village: "",
        district: "",
        totalAreaAcres: 0,
        activeSeasonsCount: 0,
        parcels: [],
      },
      crops: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.crops)),
      seasons: [
        {
          id: seasonId,
          seasonCode: seasonId,
          cropId: "CROP-PADDY-SAMBA",
          cropName: "Sample plan only — choose a crop",
          tamilCropName: "மாதிரி திட்டம் — பயிரைத் தேர்ந்தெடுக்கவும்",
          variety: "",
          farmerId,
          parcelId: "",
          parcelName: "No parcel selected",
          seasonName: "Samba (Main-Rabi)",
          startDate: today,
          sowingDate: today,
          plannedHarvestDate: harvestDate,
          areaAcres: 0,
          targetYieldBags: 0,
          stage: "Planning",
          allocatedBudget: 0,
          notes: STARTER_SEASON_NOTE,
        },
      ],
      soilReports: [],
      seedBatches: [],
      inputProducts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.inputProducts)),
      inputPurchases: [],
      tasks: [],
      irrigations: [],
      cropObservations: [],
      expertRequests: [],
      machineryListings: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.machineryListings)),
      labourBookings: [],
      harvestRecords: [],
      storageListings: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.storageListings)),
      transportListings: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.transportListings)),
      buyers: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.buyers)),
      produceListings: [],
      buyerOffers: [],
      sales: [],
      payments: [],
      expenses: [],
      schemes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultState.schemes)),
      journeyEvents: [],
      lastSyncTime: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
    };
  }

  private static setSyncStatus(status: CloudSyncStatus): void {
    this.syncStatus = status;
    this.syncListeners.forEach((listener) => listener(status));
  }

  public static getSyncStatus(): CloudSyncStatus {
    return this.syncStatus;
  }

  public static subscribeToSyncStatus(
    listener: (status: CloudSyncStatus) => void,
  ): () => void {
    this.syncListeners.add(listener);
    listener(this.syncStatus);
    return () => this.syncListeners.delete(listener);
  }

  public static activateDemoMode(): AppState {
    this.clearCloudUser();
    const demoStored = this.readStoredState(DEMO_STORAGE_KEY);
    const state = demoStored || this.createDefaultState();
    this.inMemoryState = state;
    this.writeLocalState(DEMO_STORAGE_KEY, state);
    this.setSyncStatus("local");
    return state;
  }

  public static clearCloudUser(): void {
    this.saveGeneration++;
    if (this.cloudSaveTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.cloudSaveTimer);
      this.cloudSaveTimer = null;
    }
    this.cloudUserId = null;
    this.inMemoryState = null;
    this.setSyncStatus("local");
  }

  public static async flushCloudSync(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.cloudSaveTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.cloudSaveTimer);
      this.cloudSaveTimer = null;
    }
    if (!supabase || !this.cloudUserId) return;

    const userId = this.cloudUserId;
    const currentGen = ++this.saveGeneration;
    const state = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.loadState())) as AppState;
    this.setSyncStatus("syncing");
    try {
      await this.enqueueCloudSave(userId, state, currentGen);
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.cloudUserId !== userId || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) return;
      this.setSyncStatus("error");
      throw error;
    }
    if (this.cloudUserId === userId && this.saveGeneration === currentGen) {
      this.setSyncStatus("synced");
    }
  }

  public static async activateCloudUser(user: User): Promise<void> {
    if (!supabase) throw new Error("Supabase cloud sync is not configured.");

    if (this.cloudSaveTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.cloudSaveTimer);
      this.cloudSaveTimer = null;
    }
    const currentGen = ++this.saveGeneration;
    this.cloudUserId = user.id;
    this.inMemoryState = null;

    // If offline, fall back directly to user's cached state
    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && !navigator.onLine) {
      const state = this.getSafeUserState(user, this.readUserState(user.id));
      this.inMemoryState = state;
      this.writeLocalState(this.getUserStorageKey(user.id), state);
      this.setSyncStatus("offline");
      return;
    }

    this.setSyncStatus("syncing");

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("v2a_user_state")
      .select("state")
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (this.cloudUserId !== user.id || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) {
      return;
    }

    if (error) {
      // Supabase or network error: attempt cached fallback
      const cached = this.readUserState(user.id);
      if (cached) {
        const state = this.getSafeUserState(user, cached);
        this.inMemoryState = state;
        this.writeLocalState(this.getUserStorageKey(user.id), state);
        this.setSyncStatus("error");
        return;
      }
      this.setSyncStatus("error");
      throw new Error(`Could not load cloud data: ${error.message}`);
    }

    let state: AppState;
    let shouldSaveInitialState = false;
    if (data && isAppState(data.state)) {
      state = data.state;
      if (this.isLegacyDemoSeed(state)) {
        state = this.createNewUserInitialState(user);
        shouldSaveInitialState = true;
      }
    } else {
      // No record in cloud: use cached user state or clean initial state
      const cachedState = this.readUserState(user.id);
      state = this.getSafeUserState(user, cachedState);
      shouldSaveInitialState = true;
    }

    if (shouldSaveInitialState) {
      try {
        await this.enqueueCloudSave(user.id, state, currentGen);
      } catch (saveError) {
        if (this.cloudUserId !== user.id || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) return;
        this.setSyncStatus("error");
        throw new Error(
          `Your account data could not be initialized in the cloud: ${
            saveError instanceof Error ? saveError.message : "Please try again."
          }`,
        );
      }
    }

    if (this.cloudUserId !== user.id || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) {
      return;
    }

    state.currentUser = user;
    if (user.role === "farmer") {
      state.farmer = {
        ...state.farmer,
        userId: user.id,
        fullName: user.name || state.farmer.fullName,
        tamilFullName: user.tamilName || user.name || state.farmer.fullName,
      };
    }

    this.inMemoryState = state;
    this.writeLocalState(this.getUserStorageKey(user.id), state);
    this.setSyncStatus("synced");
  }

  private static async enqueueCloudSave(
    userId: string,
    state: AppState,
    currentGen: number,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const client = supabase;
    if (!client) throw new Error("Supabase cloud sync is not configured.");

    const saveTask = this.cloudSaveQueue.then(async () => {
      if (this.cloudUserId !== userId || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) return;

      const { data, error: sessionError } = await client.auth.getSession();
      if (sessionError) {
        throw new Error(`Could not verify your cloud session: ${sessionError.message}`);
      }
      if (data.session?.user.id !== userId) {
        throw new Error("Your cloud session changed before the data could be saved. Sign in again.");
      }
      if (this.cloudUserId !== userId || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) return;

      const { error } = await client.from("v2a_user_state").upsert(
        {
          user_id: userId,
          state,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
        { onConflict: "user_id" },
      );
      if (error) throw new Error(`Could not finish cloud sync: ${error.message}`);
    });

    this.cloudSaveQueue = saveTask.then(
      () => undefined,
      () => undefined,
    );
    await saveTask;
  }

  private static readStoredState(storageKey: string): AppState | null {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
      if (!stored) return null;
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
      return isAppState(parsed) ? parsed : null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("Could not read cached app state", error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  private static writeLocalState(storageKey: string, state: AppState): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(state));
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("Could not save local app state", error);
    }
  }

  private static scheduleCloudSave(state: AppState): void {
    const client = supabase;
    if (!client || !this.cloudUserId) return;

    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && !navigator.onLine) {
      this.setSyncStatus("offline");
      return;
    }

    const userId = this.cloudUserId;
    const currentGen = ++this.saveGeneration;
    const snapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)) as AppState;

    if (this.cloudSaveTimer) clearTimeout(this.cloudSaveTimer);
    this.setSyncStatus("syncing");

    this.cloudSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.cloudUserId !== userId || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) {
        return;
      }
      this.cloudSaveTimer = null;
      void this.enqueueCloudSave(userId, snapshot, currentGen)
        .then(() => {
          if (this.cloudUserId === userId && this.saveGeneration === currentGen) {
            this.setSyncStatus("synced");
          }
        })
        .catch((error: unknown) => {
          if (this.cloudUserId !== userId || this.saveGeneration !== currentGen) return;
          this.setSyncStatus("error");
          console.error("Cloud sync failed", error);
        });
    }, 700);
  }

  public static markCloudOffline(): void {
    if (this.cloudUserId) this.setSyncStatus("offline");
  }

  private static loadState(): AppState {
    if (this.inMemoryState) {
      return this.inMemoryState;
    }

    const key = this.getStorageKey();
    const stored = this.readStoredState(key);
    if (stored) {
      this.inMemoryState = stored;
      return stored;
    }

    if (this.cloudUserId) {
      const clean = this.createNewUserInitialState({
        id: this.cloudUserId,
        name: "Farmer",
        phone: "",
        role: "farmer",
      });
      this.inMemoryState = clean;
      this.writeLocalState(key, clean);
      return clean;
    }

    const demo = this.createDefaultState();
    this.inMemoryState = demo;
    this.writeLocalState(DEMO_STORAGE_KEY, demo);
    return demo;
  }

  private static saveState(state: AppState): void {
    try {
      state.lastSyncTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
      this.inMemoryState = state;
      this.writeLocalState(this.getStorageKey(), state);
      this.scheduleCloudSave(state);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Could not save state to localStorage", e);
    }
  }

  public static getState(): AppState {
    return this.loadState();
  }

  public static setLanguage(lang: Language): AppState {
    const s = this.loadState();
    s.language = lang;
    this.saveState(s);
    return s;
  }

  public static setCurrentUser(user: User): AppState {
    const s = this.loadState();
    s.currentUser = user;
    if (user.role === "farmer") {
      s.farmer = {
        ...s.farmer,
        userId: user.id,
        fullName: user.name,
        tamilFullName: user.tamilName || s.farmer.tamilFullName,
      };
    }
    this.saveState(s);
    return s;
  }

  public static setActiveSeason(seasonId: string): AppState {
    const s = this.loadState();
    s.activeSeasonId = seasonId;
    this.saveState(s);
    return s;
  }

  public static updateFarmerAndFarm(
    farmerUpdates: Partial<FarmerProfile>,
    farmUpdates: Partial<Farm>,
  ): AppState {
    const s = this.loadState();
    s.farmer = { ...s.farmer, ...farmerUpdates };
    s.farm = { ...s.farm, ...farmUpdates };
    s.currentUser = {
      ...s.currentUser,
      name: s.farmer.fullName,
      tamilName: s.farmer.tamilFullName,
    };
    this.saveState(s);
    return s;
  }

  public static addExpense(expense: Omit<Expense, "id">): Expense {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newExp: Expense = {
      ...expense,
      id: `EXP-${Date.now()}`,
    };
    s.expenses.unshift(newExp);

    // also record a journey event
    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: newExp.seasonId,
      stage: "Crop Nutrition",
      title: `Expense: ${newExp.description}`,
      tamilTitle: newExp.tamilDescription || `செலவு: ${newExp.description}`,
      date: newExp.date,
      description: `Amount ₹${newExp.amount} paid via ${newExp.paymentMode} to ${newExp.paidTo}.`,
      costImpact: newExp.amount,
      badge: `₹${newExp.amount}`,
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return newExp;
  }

  public static addTask(task: Omit<FarmTask, "id">): FarmTask {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newTask: FarmTask = { ...task, id: `TASK-${Date.now()}` };
    s.tasks.unshift(newTask);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newTask;
  }

  public static addIrrigation(
    record: Omit<IrrigationRecord, "id">,
  ): IrrigationRecord {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newRecord: IrrigationRecord = { ...record, id: `IRR-${Date.now()}` };
    s.irrigations.unshift(newRecord);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newRecord;
  }

  public static addExpertRequest(
    request: Omit<ExpertRequest, "id">,
  ): ExpertRequest {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newRequest: ExpertRequest = { ...request, id: `EXP-${Date.now()}` };
    s.expertRequests.unshift(newRequest);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newRequest;
  }

  public static addJourneyEvent(event: Omit<JourneyEvent, "id">): JourneyEvent {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newEvent: JourneyEvent = { ...event, id: `EV-${Date.now()}` };
    s.journeyEvents.unshift(newEvent);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newEvent;
  }

  public static addSoilReport(report: Omit<SoilReport, "id">): SoilReport {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newReport: SoilReport = { ...report, id: `SOIL-${Date.now()}` };
    s.soilReports.unshift(newReport);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newReport;
  }

  public static addInputPurchase(
    purchase: Omit<InputPurchase, "id">,
  ): InputPurchase {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newPurchase: InputPurchase = {
      ...purchase,
      id: `INPUT-${Date.now()}`,
    };
    s.inputPurchases.unshift(newPurchase);
    const product = s.inputProducts.find(
      (item) => item.id === purchase.productId,
    );
    if (product)
      product.stockAvailable = Math.max(
        0,
        product.stockAvailable - purchase.quantity,
      );
    this.saveState(s);
    return newPurchase;
  }

  public static addSeedBatch(batch: Omit<SeedBatch, "id">): SeedBatch {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newBatch: SeedBatch = {
      ...batch,
      id: `SEED-${Date.now()}`,
    };
    s.seedBatches.unshift(newBatch);

    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: s.activeSeasonId,
      stage: "Seed Sourcing",
      title: `Seed Batch Recorded: ${newBatch.variety}`,
      tamilTitle: `விதை தொகுதி பதிவு: ${newBatch.tamilVariety || newBatch.variety}`,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      description: `Batch ${newBatch.batchNumber} from ${newBatch.manufacturer}. Germination: ${newBatch.germinationRatePct}%.`,
      badge:
        newBatch.verificationStatus === "VERIFIED_OFFICIAL"
          ? "Official verification"
          : "Demo batch lookup",
      verifiedOfficial: newBatch.verificationStatus === "VERIFIED_OFFICIAL",
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return newBatch;
  }

  public static addCropObservation(
    obs: Omit<CropObservation, "id">,
  ): CropObservation {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newObs: CropObservation = {
      ...obs,
      id: `OBS-${Date.now()}`,
    };
    s.cropObservations.unshift(newObs);

    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: newObs.seasonId,
      stage: "Protection",
      title: `Diagnosis: ${newObs.aiDiagnosis.diseaseName}`,
      tamilTitle: `நோய் கண்டறிதல்: ${newObs.aiDiagnosis.tamilDiseaseName}`,
      date: newObs.date,
      description: `Detected with ${newObs.aiDiagnosis.confidencePct}% match. Severity: ${newObs.aiDiagnosis.severity}.`,
      badge: `${newObs.aiDiagnosis.confidencePct}% AI Match`,
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return newObs;
  }

  public static addProduceListing(
    listing: Omit<ProduceListing, "id">,
  ): ProduceListing {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const newListing: ProduceListing = {
      ...listing,
      id: `LIST-${Date.now()}`,
    };
    s.produceListings.unshift(newListing);
    this.saveState(s);
    return newListing;
  }

  public static broadcastHarvest(
    seasonId: string,
  ): { listing: ProduceListing; offers: BuyerOffer[] } | null {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const season = s.seasons.find((item) => item.id === seasonId);
    const harvest = s.harvestRecords.find((item) => item.seasonId === seasonId);
    if (!season || !harvest) return null;

    const existingListing = s.produceListings.find(
      (item) =>
        item.seasonId === seasonId &&
        (item.status === "Active" || item.status === "Under Negotiation"),
    );
    if (existingListing) {
      return {
        listing: existingListing,
        offers: s.buyerOffers.filter(
          (offer) => offer.listingId === existingListing.id,
        ),
      };
    }

    const alreadySoldBags = s.sales
      .filter((sale) => sale.seasonId === seasonId)
      .reduce((total, sale) => total + sale.bagsSold, 0);
    const availableBags = harvest.actualQuantityBags - alreadySoldBags;
    if (availableBags <= 0) return null;

    const crop = s.crops.find((item) => item.id === season.cropId);
    const askingPricePerBag = crop?.marketRateRange
      ? Math.round((crop.marketRateRange.min + crop.marketRateRange.max) / 2)
      : 2300;
    const listing: ProduceListing = {
      id: `LIST-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId,
      farmerId: season.farmerId,
      cropName: season.cropName,
      variety: season.variety,
      quantityBags: availableBags,
      bagWeightKg: harvest.bagWeightKg,
      askingPricePerBag,
      qualityGrade: harvest.grainQualityGrade,
      moisturePct: harvest.moistureContentPct,
      pickupLocation: `${s.farm.village} Farm Gate, ${s.farm.district}`,
      listingDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      status: "Active",
    };

    const matchingBuyers = s.buyers.filter((buyer) =>
      buyer.targetCrops.some((target) =>
        target
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(season.cropName.split(" ")[0].toLowerCase()),
      ),
    );
    const buyers = matchingBuyers.length > 0 ? matchingBuyers : s.buyers;
    const offers: BuyerOffer[] = buyers.map((buyer, index) => ({
      id: `OFFER-${Date.now()}-${index}`,
      listingId: listing.id,
      seasonId,
      buyerId: buyer.id,
      buyerName: buyer.name,
      buyerOrg: buyer.organization,
      offeredPricePerBag: Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(askingPricePerBag * (1 - index * 0.025)),
      ),
      quantityBagsRequested: availableBags,
      gradeAccepted: harvest.grainQualityGrade,
      pickupTerms: "Buyer Farm-gate Pickup",
      paymentTerms: index === 0 ? "Immediate Bank NEFT" : "3-Day Post-delivery",
      status: "Pending",
      offerDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      notes:
        "Demo buyer offer. Confirm price and settlement terms before accepting.",
    }));

    s.produceListings.unshift(listing);
    s.buyerOffers.unshift(...offers);
    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId,
      stage: "Market Sale",
      title: "Harvest broadcast to buyers",
      tamilTitle: "அறுவடை விளைபொருள் வாங்குபவர்களுக்கு அறிவிக்கப்பட்டது",
      date: listing.listingDate,
      description: `${availableBags} bags were listed and ${offers.length} demo buyer offer(s) generated for review.`,
      badge: "Listing Active",
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return { listing, offers };
  }

  public static acceptOffer(offerId: string): BuyerOffer | null {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const offer = s.buyerOffers.find((o) => o.id === offerId);
    if (!offer || offer.status !== "Pending") return null;
    const listing = s.produceListings.find(
      (item) => item.id === offer.listingId,
    );
    if (
      !listing ||
      listing.status !== "Active" ||
      offer.quantityBagsRequested <= 0 ||
      offer.quantityBagsRequested > listing.quantityBags ||
      s.sales.some((sale) => sale.listingId === listing.id)
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    offer.status = "Accepted";
    listing.status = "Sold";
    s.buyerOffers.forEach((otherOffer) => {
      if (
        otherOffer.listingId === offer.listingId &&
        otherOffer.id !== offer.id &&
        otherOffer.status === "Pending"
      ) {
        otherOffer.status = "Rejected";
      }
    });

    // Auto-create or update sale record
    const gross = offer.offeredPricePerBag * offer.quantityBagsRequested;
    const cess = Math.round(gross * 0.01);
    const bagging = offer.quantityBagsRequested * 30;
    const transport = offer.pickupTerms === "Buyer Farm-gate Pickup" ? 0 : 3500;
    const net = gross - cess - bagging - transport;

    const sale: SaleRecord = {
      id: `SALE-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: offer.seasonId,
      listingId: offer.listingId,
      buyerId: offer.buyerId,
      buyerName: offer.buyerName,
      buyerOrg: offer.buyerOrg,
      saleDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      bagsSold: offer.quantityBagsRequested,
      pricePerBag: offer.offeredPricePerBag,
      grossAmount: gross,
      deductions: {
        mandiCessOrMarketFee: cess,
        baggingCost: bagging,
        moistureDeduction: 0,
        logisticsPaidByFarmer: transport,
      },
      netSaleProceeds: net,
      weighmentSlipNumber: `WS-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      saleInvoiceNumber: `INV-V2A-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      status: "Invoiced",
    };
    s.sales.unshift(sale);

    // Record receivables according to the buyer's agreed settlement terms.
    const now = Date.now();
    const dueToday = new Date(now).toISOString().split("T")[0];
    const dueInThreeDays = new Date(now + 3 * 24 * 3600 * 1000)
      .toISOString()
      .split("T")[0];
    const paymentMilestones: Omit<Payment, "id">[] =
      offer.paymentTerms === "50% Advance + 50% on Weighment"
        ? [
            {
              saleId: sale.id,
              seasonId: offer.seasonId,
              buyerName: offer.buyerName,
              amount: Math.round(net / 2),
              paymentType: "Advance",
              method: "UPI / Direct Bank Transfer",
              status: "PENDING",
              dueDate: dueToday,
            },
            {
              saleId: sale.id,
              seasonId: offer.seasonId,
              buyerName: offer.buyerName,
              amount: net - Math.round(net / 2),
              paymentType: "Final Settlement",
              method: "UPI / Direct Bank Transfer",
              status: "PENDING",
              dueDate: dueInThreeDays,
            },
          ]
        : [
            {
              saleId: sale.id,
              seasonId: offer.seasonId,
              buyerName: offer.buyerName,
              amount: net,
              paymentType: "Final Settlement",
              method: "UPI / Direct Bank Transfer",
              status: "PENDING",
              dueDate:
                offer.paymentTerms === "Immediate Bank NEFT"
                  ? dueToday
                  : dueInThreeDays,
            },
          ];
    for (const milestone of paymentMilestones) {
      s.payments.unshift({
        ...milestone,
        id: `PAY-${Date.now()}-${s.payments.length}`,
      });
    }

    this.saveState(s);
    return offer;
  }

  public static rejectOffer(offerId: string): BuyerOffer | null {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const offer = s.buyerOffers.find((item) => item.id === offerId);
    if (!offer || offer.status !== "Pending") return null;
    offer.status = "Rejected";
    this.saveState(s);
    return offer;
  }

  public static recordPaymentSettlement(
    paymentId: string,
    refNo: string,
  ): Payment | null {
    const s = this.loadState();
    const payment = s.payments.find((p) => p.id === paymentId);
    if (!payment) return null;

    payment.status = "RECEIVED";
    payment.paymentDate = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
    payment.referenceNo = refNo;

    // Check if associated sale is now settled
    const allSalePayments = s.payments.filter(
      (p) => p.saleId === payment.saleId,
    );
    const hasPending = allSalePayments.some((p) => p.status === "PENDING");
    const sale = s.sales.find((sal) => sal.id === payment.saleId);
    if (sale) {
      sale.status = hasPending ? "Partially Paid" : "Settled";
    }
    const hasPendingSeasonPayments = s.payments.some(
      (item) =>
        item.seasonId === payment.seasonId && item.status !== "RECEIVED",
    );
    if (
      !hasPendingSeasonPayments &&
      s.sales.some((item) => item.seasonId === payment.seasonId)
    ) {
      const season = s.seasons.find((item) => item.id === payment.seasonId);
      if (season) season.stage = "Sold & Settled";
    }

    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: payment.seasonId,
      stage: "Financial Settlement",
      title: `Bank Settlement Received: ₹${payment.amount.toLocaleString()}`,
      tamilTitle: `வங்கிப் பரிவர்த்தனை வரவு: ₹${payment.amount.toLocaleString()}`,
      date: payment.paymentDate,
      description: `Payment settled via ${payment.method}. Reference: ${refNo}.`,
      incomeImpact: payment.amount,
      badge: "Bank Settled",
      verifiedOfficial: true,
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return payment;
  }

  public static createSeason(
    seasonData: Omit<CropSeason, "id" | "seasonCode">,
  ): CropSeason {
    const s = this.loadState();
    s.seasons = s.seasons.filter(
      (season) => season.notes !== STARTER_SEASON_NOTE,
    );
    const newId = `CR-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
    const newSeason: CropSeason = {
      ...seasonData,
      id: newId,
      seasonCode: newId,
    };
    s.seasons.unshift(newSeason);
    s.activeSeasonId = newId;

    s.journeyEvents.push({
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      seasonId: newId,
      stage: "Planning & Soil",
      title: `Season Initialized: ${newSeason.cropName}`,
      tamilTitle: `புதிய சாகுபடி துவக்கம்: ${newSeason.tamilCropName || newSeason.cropName}`,
      date: newSeason.startDate,
      description: `Targeting ${newSeason.targetYieldBags} bags on ${newSeason.areaAcres} acres in ${newSeason.seasonName}.`,
      badge: "Season Started",
    });

    this.saveState(s);
    return newSeason;
  }

  public static resetToDemo(): AppState {
    if (this.cloudUserId) {
      throw new Error("Demo data cannot replace a signed-in account's data.");
    }
    const state = this.createDefaultState();
    state.produceListings = [];
    state.buyerOffers = [];
    state.sales = [];
    state.payments = [];
    const harvestSeason = state.seasons.find(
      (season) => season.id === state.activeSeasonId,
    );
    if (harvestSeason) harvestSeason.stage = "Harvested";
    state.journeyEvents = state.journeyEvents.filter(
      (event) =>
        event.seasonId !== state.activeSeasonId ||
        (event.stage !== "Market Sale" &&
          event.stage !== "Financial Settlement"),
    );
    this.saveState(state);
    return state;
  }

  // Pure Financial Calculators
  public static calculateSeasonFinancials(seasonId?: string) {
    if (!seasonId) {
      return {
        totalExpenses: 0,
        grossSales: 0,
        totalDeductions: 0,
        netProceedsInvoiced: 0,
        receivedPayments: 0,
        pendingPayments: 0,
        projectedNetResult: 0,
        realizedCashflow: 0,
        expenseByCategory: {},
        expenseCount: 0,
        salesCount: 0,
      };
    }
    const s = this.loadState();
    const seasonExpenses = s.expenses.filter((e) => e.seasonId === seasonId);
    const seasonSales = s.sales.filter((sl) => sl.seasonId === seasonId);
    const seasonPayments = s.payments.filter((p) => p.seasonId === seasonId);

    const totalExpenses = seasonExpenses.reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.amount, 0);

    const grossSales = seasonSales.reduce((sum, sl) => sum + sl.grossAmount, 0);
    const totalDeductions = seasonSales.reduce((sum, sl) => {
      const d = sl.deductions;
      return (
        sum +
        d.mandiCessOrMarketFee +
        d.baggingCost +
        d.moistureDeduction +
        d.logisticsPaidByFarmer
      );
    }, 0);
    const netProceedsInvoiced = grossSales - totalDeductions;

    const receivedPayments = seasonPayments
      .filter((p) => p.status === "RECEIVED")
      .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.amount, 0);

    const pendingPayments = seasonPayments
      .filter((p) => p.status !== "RECEIVED")
      .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.amount, 0);

    // CRITICAL: Realized Net Profit = Realized Proceeds - Total Expenses
    // Net Result = Sales Revenue - Deductions - Total Expenses
    const projectedNetResult = netProceedsInvoiced - totalExpenses;
    const realizedCashflow = receivedPayments - totalExpenses;

    // Breakdown by category
    const expenseByCategory: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const exp of seasonExpenses) {
      expenseByCategory[exp.category] =
        (expenseByCategory[exp.category] || 0) + exp.amount;
    }

    return {
      totalExpenses,
      grossSales,
      totalDeductions,
      netProceedsInvoiced,
      receivedPayments,
      pendingPayments, // NOT counted as received income!
      projectedNetResult,
      realizedCashflow,
      expenseByCategory,
      expenseCount: seasonExpenses.length,
      salesCount: seasonSales.length,
    };
  }
}

if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.addEventListener("online", () => {
    if (
      AppStore.getSyncStatus() === "offline" ||
      AppStore.getSyncStatus() === "error"
    ) {
      void AppStore.flushCloudSync().catch((e) => {
        console.warn("Auto-sync after reconnect failed:", e);
      });
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener("offline", () => {
    AppStore.markCloudOffline();
  });
}

import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

function normalizeSupabaseUrl(rawUrl?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!rawUrl) return undefined;
  const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
  const dashboardMatch = trimmed.match(
    /supabase\.com\/dashboard\/project\/([a-z0-9]+)/i,
  );
  if (dashboardMatch) {
    return `https://${dashboardMatch[1]}.supabase.co`;
  }
  return trimmed.replace(/\/+$/, "");
}

const supabaseUrl = normalizeSupabaseUrl(import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL);
const supabaseAnonKey = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY?.trim();

export const isCloudSyncEnabled = Boolean(
  supabaseUrl &&
    supabaseAnonKey &&
    !supabaseUrl.includes("YOUR_PROJECT_ID") &&
    !supabaseAnonKey.includes("YOUR_SUPABASE"),
);

export const supabase = isCloudSyncEnabled
  ? createClient(supabaseUrl!, supabaseAnonKey!, {
      auth: {
        autoRefreshToken: true,
        persistSession: true,
        detectSessionInUrl: true,
        storageKey: "v2a_supabase_auth_token",
      },
    })
  : null;

  | "Kuruvai (Summer-Kharif)"

================================================================================
FILE: src\utils\images.ts
================================================================================
export async function compressImageToDataUrl(
  file: File,
  maxDimension = 720,
  quality = 0.68,
): Promise<string> {
  if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
    throw new Error("Choose an image file such as JPG, PNG, or WebP.");
  }
  if (file.size > 12 * 1024 * 1024) {
    throw new Error("Image must be smaller than 12 MB.");
  }

  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
  const scale = Math.min(
    1,
    maxDimension / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height),
  );
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.width * scale));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.height * scale));
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!context) {
    bitmap.close();
    throw new Error("This browser could not prepare the image.");
  }
  context.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  bitmap.close();

  const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
    canvas.toBlob(
      (result) =>
        result
          ? resolve(result)
          : reject(new Error("Could not compress the image.")),
      "image/jpeg",
      quality,
    );
  });

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () =>
      typeof reader.result === "string"
        ? resolve(reader.result)
        : reject(new Error("Could not read the image."));
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Could not read the image."));
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: supabase\schema.sql
================================================================================
-- V2A cloud sync: one complete app-state document per authenticated account.
-- Run this in the Supabase SQL Editor for your project.

create table if not exists public.v2a_user_state (
  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  state jsonb not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.v2a_user_state enable row level security;

revoke all on public.v2a_user_state from anon;
grant select, insert, update, delete on public.v2a_user_state to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "Users can read their own V2A state" on public.v2a_user_state;
create policy "Users can read their own V2A state"
  on public.v2a_user_state for select to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

drop policy if exists "Users can create their own V2A state" on public.v2a_user_state;
create policy "Users can create their own V2A state"
  on public.v2a_user_state for insert to authenticated
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

drop policy if exists "Users can update their own V2A state" on public.v2a_user_state;
create policy "Users can update their own V2A state"
  on public.v2a_user_state for update to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

drop policy if exists "Users can delete their own V2A state" on public.v2a_user_state;
create policy "Users can delete their own V2A state"
  on public.v2a_user_state for delete to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);
